import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import { Toaster as Sonner } from "@/components/ui/sonner";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { BrowserRouter, HashRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import Dashboard from "./components/Dashboard";
import RecipesPage from "./components/RecipesPage";
import RecipeFormPage from "./components/RecipeFormPage";
import BatchesPage from "./components/BatchesPage";
import InventoryPage from "./components/InventoryPage";
import CalculatorsPage from "./components/CalculatorsPage";
import ProfilesPage from "./components/ProfilesPage";
import BatchDetailPage from "./components/BatchDetailPage";
import WaterPage from "./components/WaterPage";
import ReportsPage from "./components/ReportsPage";
import StylesPage from "./components/StylesPage";
import StyleDetailPage from "./components/StyleDetailPage";
import BrewDayPage from "./components/BrewDayPage";
import SettingsPage from "./components/SettingsPage";
import AiAssistant from "./components/AiAssistant";
import NotFound from "./pages/NotFound";
import { SettingsTheme } from "./lib/settings";

const queryClient = new QueryClient();

// Em hospedagens estáticas como GitHub Pages e em arquivos abertos direto do disco,
// o BrowserRouter pode quebrar por causa de rotas sem fallback do servidor.
// Usamos HashRouter nesses cenários para manter a navegação estável.
const Router = typeof window !== "undefined" &&
  (window.location.protocol === "file:" || window.location.hostname.endsWith("github.io"))
  ? HashRouter
  : BrowserRouter;

const App = () => (
  <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
    <TooltipProvider>
      <SettingsTheme />
      <Toaster />
      <Sonner />
      <Router>
        <Layout>
          <Routes>
            <Route path="/" element={<Dashboard />} />
            <Route path="/recipes" element={<RecipesPage />} />
            <Route path="/recipes/new" element={<RecipeFormPage />} />
            <Route path="/recipes/:id" element={<RecipeFormPage />} />
            <Route path="/batches" element={<BatchesPage />} />
            <Route path="/batches/:id" element={<BatchDetailPage />} />
            <Route path="/inventory" element={<InventoryPage />} />
            <Route path="/calculators" element={<CalculatorsPage />} />
            <Route path="/brew-day" element={<BrewDayPage />} />
            <Route path="/brewday" element={<BrewDayPage />} />
            <Route path="/profiles" element={<ProfilesPage />} />
            <Route path="/profiles/:type" element={<ProfilesPage />} />
            <Route path="/water" element={<WaterPage />} />
            <Route path="/styles" element={<StylesPage />} />
            <Route path="/styles/:code" element={<StyleDetailPage />} />
            <Route path="/estilos" element={<StylesPage />} />
            <Route path="/estilos/:code" element={<StyleDetailPage />} />
            <Route path="/fermentation" element={<Navigate to="/profiles/fermentacao" replace />} />
            <Route path="/reports" element={<ReportsPage />} />
            <Route path="/settings" element={<SettingsPage />} />
            <Route path="/ai-assistant" element={<AiAssistant />} />
            {/* ADD ALL CUSTOM ROUTES ABOVE THE CATCH-ALL "*" ROUTE */}
            <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
          </Routes>
        </Layout>
      </Router>
    </TooltipProvider>
  </QueryClientProvider>
);

export default App;

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from "react";
import { 
  Send, 
  User, 
  Loader2, 
  PlusCircle, 
  Trash2, 
  Image as ImageIcon, 
  AlertCircle,
  Mic,
  MicOff,
  Settings2,
  Brain
} from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { ScrollArea } from "@/components/ui/scroll-area";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { GeminiService, geminiLimiter, type Message, GEMINI_3_LIVE_MODELS, DEFAULT_GEMINI_3_MODEL } from "@/lib/gemini";
import { useGeminiLive } from "@/hooks/use-gemini-live";
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipProvider,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import {
  AI_SYNC_ENABLED_STORAGE_KEY,
  getSizeMB,
  readStoredAiChats,
  syncAiChats,
  writeStoredAiChats,
  type ChatSession,
} from "@/lib/cloud-sync";
import { useInventory, useRecipes, uid } from "@/lib/store";
import { type Recipe, defaultEquipment, recipeStats, type InventoryItem, type HopUse } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { saveSettings } from "@/lib/settings";
import geminiLogo from "../../gemini-svg.svg";

type AiAction = {
  action: "ADD_INVENTORY" | "CREATE_RECIPE" | "UPDATE_RECIPE" | "UPDATE_SETTING";
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  message: string;
};

const parseRecipeMarkdown = (content: string) => {
  const trimmed = (content ?? "").trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;

  const fenceMatch = trimmed.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/i);
  if (fenceMatch) {
    const jsonText = fenceMatch[1].trim();
    const before = trimmed.slice(0, fenceMatch.index ?? 0).trim();
    const after = trimmed.slice((fenceMatch.index ?? 0) + fenceMatch[0].length).trim();

    try {
      return {
        recipe: JSON.parse(jsonText),
        explanation: [before, after].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n") || "Receita pronta para importação.",
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  const jsonMatch = trimmed.match(/(\{[\s\S]*\})/);
  if (!jsonMatch) return null;

  try {
    return {
      recipe: JSON.parse(jsonMatch[1]),
      explanation: trimmed.replace(jsonMatch[1], "").trim() || "Receita pronta para importação.",
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

const parseAiAction = (content: string): AiAction | null => {
  const trimmed = (content ?? "").trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;

  const candidates: string[] = [];
  const fenced = trimmed.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/i);
  if (fenced) candidates.push(fenced[1].trim());

  if (trimmed.startsWith("{")) {
    candidates.push(trimmed);
  } else {
    const jsonMatch = trimmed.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
    if (jsonMatch) candidates.push(jsonMatch[0]);
  }

  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(candidate);
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && typeof parsed.action === "string" && typeof parsed.message === "string") {
        return {
          action: parsed.action,
          payload: parsed.payload && typeof parsed.payload === "object" ? parsed.payload : {},
          message: parsed.message,
        };
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore malformed JSON and continue searching.
    }
  }

  return null;
};


export default function AiAssistant() {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<ChatSession[]>([]);
  const [activeChatId, setActiveChatId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isHistoryOpen, setIsHistoryOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cloudSyncEnabled, setCloudSyncEnabled] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
    return localStorage.getItem(AI_SYNC_ENABLED_STORAGE_KEY) === "true";
  });
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [selectedImage, setSelectedImage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [retryMessage, setRetryMessage] = useState<{ text: string; image?: string } | null>(null);
  const [actualModelUsed, setActualModelUsed] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fallbackOccurred, setFallbackOccurred] = useState(false);
  const [liveModel, setLiveModel] = useState<string>("gemini-3.8-live");
  
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const sendLockRef = useRef(false);
  const recognitionRef = useRef<{ stop: () => void } | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useRecipes();
  const [inventory, setInventory] = useInventory();

  const apiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY;

  const live = useGeminiLive({
    apiKey: apiKey || "",
    model: liveModel,
    onResponse: (text) => {
      // In Live mode, we might want to append to a special "live" message
      // or just handle it as transient. For now, let's keep it in context.
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      toast({
        title: "Erro no Gemini Live",
        description: err.message,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  });
  const totalHistorySizeMB = history.reduce((total, chat) => total + Number(getSizeMB(chat)), 0);

  const scrollToBottom = () => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scrollRef.current) {
      scrollRef.current.scrollTop = scrollRef.current.scrollHeight;
    }
    scrollToBottom();
  }, [messages, isLoading]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(AI_SYNC_ENABLED_STORAGE_KEY, String(cloudSyncEnabled));
  }, [cloudSyncEnabled]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const savedChats = readStoredAiChats();
    if (savedChats.length > 0) {
      setHistory(savedChats);
      setActiveChatId(savedChats[0].id);
      setMessages(savedChats[0].messages);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isHistoryOpen && cloudSyncEnabled) {
      void (async () => {
        await syncAiChats();
        setHistory(readStoredAiChats());
      })();
    }
  }, [isHistoryOpen, cloudSyncEnabled]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
    };
  }, []);

  const toggleRecording = () => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

    type SpeechRecognitionInstance = {
      lang: string;
      interimResults: boolean;
      continuous: boolean;
      onresult?: ((event: {
        resultIndex: number;
        results: ArrayLike<{
          isFinal: boolean;
          0: { transcript: string };
        }>;
      }) => void) | null;
      onend?: (() => void) | null;
      onerror?: ((event: { error?: string }) => void) | null;
      start: () => void;
      stop: () => void;
    };

    const SpeechRecognition =
      (window as Window & {
        SpeechRecognition?: new () => SpeechRecognitionInstance;
        webkitSpeechRecognition?: new () => SpeechRecognitionInstance;
      }).SpeechRecognition ||
      (window as Window & {
        SpeechRecognition?: new () => SpeechRecognitionInstance;
        webkitSpeechRecognition?: new () => SpeechRecognitionInstance;
      }).webkitSpeechRecognition;

    if (!SpeechRecognition) {
      toast({
        title: "Microfone não suportado",
        description: "Seu navegador não oferece suporte para reconhecimento de voz.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    if (isRecording && recognitionRef.current) {
      recognitionRef.current.stop();
      recognitionRef.current = null;
      setIsRecording(false);
      return;
    }

    const recognition = new SpeechRecognition();
    recognition.lang = "pt-BR";
    recognition.continuous = true;
    recognition.interimResults = true;
    recognition.onresult = (event) => {
      let interimTranscript = "";
      let finalTranscript = "";

      for (let i = event.resultIndex; i < event.results.length; ++i) {
        if (event.results[i].isFinal) {
          finalTranscript += event.results[i][0].transcript;
        } else {
          interimTranscript += event.results[i][0].transcript;
        }
      }

      if (finalTranscript) {
        setInput((prev) => prev + (prev.length > 0 ? " " : "") + finalTranscript.trim());
      }
    };
    recognition.onend = () => {
      recognitionRef.current = null;
      setIsRecording(false);
    };
    recognition.onerror = () => {
      recognitionRef.current = null;
      setIsRecording(false);
    };

    recognitionRef.current = recognition;
    setIsRecording(true);
    recognition.start();
  };

  const persistChatHistory = (nextMessages: Message[], chatIdOverride?: string) => {
    const sessionId = chatIdOverride ?? activeChatId ?? uid();
    const firstUserMessage = nextMessages.find((message) => message.role === "user")?.content?.trim();
    const title = (firstUserMessage && firstUserMessage.length > 0 ? firstUserMessage : "Nova conversa")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .slice(0, 48);

    const nextSession: ChatSession = {
      id: sessionId,
      title,
      messages: nextMessages,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    setActiveChatId(sessionId);
    setHistory((prev) => {
      const existing = prev.some((session) => session.id === sessionId);
      const nextHistory = existing
        ? prev.map((session) => (session.id === sessionId ? nextSession : session))
        : [nextSession, ...prev];
      writeStoredAiChats(nextHistory);
      return nextHistory;
    });
  };

  const retryLastRequest = () => {
    if (!retryMessage) return;
    setInput(retryMessage.text);
    setSelectedImage(retryMessage.image ?? null);
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      void handleSend();
    }, 0);
  };

  const handleSend = async (event?: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event?.preventDefault();

    if (isRecording) {
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
      recognitionRef.current = null;
      setIsRecording(false);
    }

    if (!input.trim() && !selectedImage) return;
    if (!apiKey) {
      toast({
        title: "API Key não configurada",
        description: "Configure VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY no seu arquivo .env",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    // Safeguard: never fire a second request while one is still running (double clicks / Enter spam)
    if (isLoading || sendLockRef.current) return;
    const waitMs = geminiLimiter.waitTime();
    if (waitMs > 0) {
      toast({ title: "Aguarde um instante", description: `Para não estourar o limite da IA, envie novamente em ${Math.ceil(waitMs / 1000)}s.` });
      return;
    }
    sendLockRef.current = true;

    const currentText = input.trim();
    const currentImage = selectedImage || undefined;
    const userMessage: Message = {
      role: "user",
      content: currentText || input,
      image: currentImage,
    };

    setRetryMessage({
      text: currentText || input,
      image: currentImage,
    });

    const nextAbortController = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = nextAbortController;
    const currentChatId = activeChatId ?? uid();
    const emptyAssistantMessage: Message = { role: "model", content: "" };
    const nextMessages = [...messages, userMessage, emptyAssistantMessage];

    setMessages(nextMessages);
    persistChatHistory(nextMessages, currentChatId);
    setInput("");
    setSelectedImage(null);
    setIsLoading(true);
    setFallbackOccurred(false);
    setActualModelUsed(null);

    try {
      const gemini = new GeminiService(apiKey);

      const context = {
        recipeCount: recipes.length,
        recipes: recipes.map(r => ({ name: r.name, style: r.styleCode })),
      };

      const streamResult = await gemini.chatStream([...messages, userMessage], context, nextAbortController.signal);
      let streamedText = "";
      
      setActualModelUsed(streamResult.modelUsed);
      setFallbackOccurred(streamResult.isFallback);

      for await (const chunk of streamResult.stream) {
        const chunkText = chunk.text;
        if (!chunkText) continue;

        streamedText += chunkText;

        setMessages((prev) => {
          const next = [...prev];
          const lastIndex = next.length - 1;
          if (next[lastIndex]?.role !== "model") return prev;

          next[lastIndex] = {
            ...next[lastIndex],
            content: (next[lastIndex].content ?? "") + chunkText,
          };

          persistChatHistory(next, currentChatId);
          return next;
        });
      }

      const finalText = streamedText.trim();
      if (!finalText) {
        setMessages((prev) => {
          const next = [...prev];
          const lastIndex = next.length - 1;
          if (next[lastIndex]?.role === "model") {
            next[lastIndex] = {
              ...next[lastIndex],
              content: "A IA respondeu sem conteúdo. Tente novamente com outra pergunta ou ajuste o contexto da sua mensagem.",
            };
          }
          persistChatHistory(next, currentChatId);
          return next;
        });
      } else {
        const action = parseAiAction(finalText);
        if (action) {
          applyAiAction(finalText);
          setMessages((prev) => {
            const next = [...prev];
            const lastIndex = next.length - 1;
            if (next[lastIndex]?.role === "model") {
              next[lastIndex] = {
                ...next[lastIndex],
                content: action.message,
              };
            }
            persistChatHistory(next, currentChatId);
            return next;
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Não foi possível obter resposta da IA";
      const name = error instanceof Error ? error.name : "Error";

      if (name === "AbortError" || message.includes("aborted")) {
        return;
      }

      toast({
        title: "Erro",
        description: message,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      if (abortControllerRef.current === nextAbortController) {
        abortControllerRef.current = null;
      }
      sendLockRef.current = false;
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onloadend = () => {
        setSelectedImage(reader.result as string);
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
  };

  const clearConversation = () => {
    abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    abortControllerRef.current = null;
    setMessages([]);
    setSelectedImage(null);
    setRetryMessage(null);
    if (activeChatId) {
      setHistory((prev) => {
        const nextHistory = prev.filter((session) => session.id !== activeChatId);
        writeStoredAiChats(nextHistory);
        return nextHistory;
      });
    }
    setActiveChatId(null);
  };

  const deleteChatSession = (chatId: string) => {
    setHistory((prev) => {
      const nextHistory = prev.filter((session) => session.id !== chatId);
      writeStoredAiChats(nextHistory);
      return nextHistory;
    });

    if (activeChatId === chatId) {
      setMessages([]);
      setActiveChatId(null);
    }
  };

  const loadChatSession = (chat: ChatSession) => {
    setMessages(chat.messages);
    setActiveChatId(chat.id);
    setRetryMessage(null);
    setIsHistoryOpen(false);
  };

  const normalizeAiRecipe = (recipeLike: Record<string, unknown> | undefined) => {
    const payload = recipeLike ?? {};
    const equipmentSource = (payload.equipment ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const defaultFermentable = { id: uid(), name: "Pale Ale", kg: 4, potential: 1.037, color: 3, type: "grao", price: 0 };
    const defaultHop = { id: uid(), name: "Cascade", grams: 25, alpha: 5.5, minutes: 60, use: "fervura" as const, price: 0 };
    const defaultYeast = { id: uid(), name: "SafAle US-05", attenuation: 81, packs: 1, price: 0 };

    const equipment = {
      ...(defaultEquipment),
      ...((payload.equipment ?? {}) as Partial<Recipe["equipment"]>),
      batchL: Number(equipmentSource.batchL ?? payload.batchSize ?? defaultEquipment.batchL) || defaultEquipment.batchL,
      boilMinutes: Number(equipmentSource.boilMinutes ?? payload.boilTime ?? defaultEquipment.boilMinutes) || defaultEquipment.boilMinutes,
      efficiency: Number(equipmentSource.efficiency ?? payload.efficiency ?? defaultEquipment.efficiency) || defaultEquipment.efficiency,
    };

    const normalizeUse = (value: unknown): HopUse => {
      const raw = String(value ?? "fervura").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (raw.includes("dry") || raw.includes("dryhop") || raw.includes("dry-hop")) return "dryhop";
      if (raw.includes("whirl") || raw.includes("whirlpool")) return "whirlpool";
      if (raw.includes("mosto") || raw.includes("first wort") || raw.includes("first_wort")) return "primeiro_mosto";
      return "fervura";
    };

    const fermentables = Array.isArray(payload.fermentables) && payload.fermentables.length > 0
      ? payload.fermentables.map((item: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => ({
          id: String(item.id ?? `${uid()}-${index}`),
          name: String(item.name ?? defaultFermentable.name),
          kg: Number(item.kg ?? item.amount ?? 0),
          potential: Number(item.potential ?? item.yield ?? 80) > 0
            ? (Number(item.potential ?? item.yield ?? 80) < 2 ? Number(item.potential ?? item.yield) : 1 + (((Number(item.potential ?? item.yield ?? 80) / 100) * 0.046)))
            : defaultFermentable.potential,
          color: Number(item.color ?? 0),
          type: String(item.type ?? "grao"),
          price: Number(item.price ?? 0),
        }))
      : [defaultFermentable];

    const hops = Array.isArray(payload.hops) && payload.hops.length > 0
      ? payload.hops.map((item: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => ({
          id: String(item.id ?? `${uid()}-${index}`),
          name: String(item.name ?? defaultHop.name),
          grams: Number(item.grams ?? item.amount ?? 0),
          alpha: Number(item.alpha ?? 5),
          minutes: Number(item.minutes ?? item.time ?? 60),
          use: normalizeUse(item.use),
          price: Number(item.price ?? 0),
        }))
      : [defaultHop];

    const yeasts = Array.isArray(payload.yeasts) && payload.yeasts.length > 0
      ? payload.yeasts.map((item: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => ({
          id: String(item.id ?? `${uid()}-${index}`),
          name: String(item.name ?? defaultYeast.name),
          attenuation: Number(item.attenuation ?? 75),
          packs: Number(item.packs ?? 1),
          price: Number(item.price ?? 0),
        }))
      : [defaultYeast];

    const finalRecipe: Recipe = {
      id: String(payload.id ?? uid()),
      name: String(payload.name ?? "Nova Receita IA").trim() || "Nova Receita IA",
      styleCode: String(payload.styleCode ?? payload.style ?? "Sem Estilo").trim(),
      author: String(payload.author ?? "IA Assistant"),
      notes: String(payload.notes ?? `Receita gerada automaticamente pela IA em ${new Date().toLocaleString("pt-BR")}`),
      equipment,
      mashSteps: Array.isArray(payload.mashSteps) ? payload.mashSteps.map((step: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => ({
        name: String(step.name ?? `Etapa ${index + 1}`),
        tempC: Number(step.tempC ?? 66),
        minutes: Number(step.minutes ?? 60),
      })) : [{ name: "Sacarificação", tempC: 66, minutes: 60 }],
      fermentationTempC: Number(payload.fermentationTempC ?? 19),
      fermentationDays: Number(payload.fermentationDays ?? 14),
      fermentables,
      hops,
      yeasts,
      miscs: Array.isArray(payload.miscs) ? payload.miscs.map((item: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => ({
        id: String(item.id ?? `${uid()}-${index}`),
        name: String(item.name ?? "Adjunto"),
        amount: Number(item.amount ?? 0),
        unit: String(item.unit ?? "un"),
        use: String(item.use ?? "Fervura"),
        price: Number(item.price ?? 0),
      })) : [],
      createdAt: String(payload.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString()),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const computedStats = recipeStats(finalRecipe);
    return {
      ...finalRecipe,
      og: computedStats.og,
      fg: computedStats.fg,
      abv: computedStats.abv,
      ibu: computedStats.ibu,
      srm: computedStats.srm,
    };
  };

  const applyAiAction = (content: string) => {
    try {
      const action = parseAiAction(content);
      if (!action) return false;

      const parsedData = action;
      console.log("Ação interceptada:", parsedData);

      switch (action.action) {
        case "ADD_INVENTORY": {
          const rawItem = (action.payload?.item ?? action.payload) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
          if (!rawItem || typeof rawItem !== "object") {
            throw new Error("Payload de inventário inválido: objeto ausente.");
          }

          const normalizedType = String(rawItem.type ?? rawItem.kind ?? "Misc").trim();
          const typeMap: Record<string, InventoryItem["kind"]> = {
            Fermentable: "fermentavel",
            fermentable: "fermentavel",
            Hop: "lupulo",
            hop: "lupulo",
            Yeast: "levedura",
            yeast: "levedura",
            Misc: "outro",
            misc: "outro",
          };

          const kind = typeMap[normalizedType] ?? "outro";
          const amountRaw = Number(rawItem.amount ?? 0);
          const amount = Number.isFinite(amountRaw) ? amountRaw : 0;
          const name = String(rawItem.name ?? "").trim() || "Item IA";

          const validInventoryUnits = new Set(["kg", "g", "L", "ml", "un"]);
          const requestedUnit = String(rawItem.unit ?? "").trim();
          const normalizedUnit = validInventoryUnits.has(requestedUnit) ? requestedUnit : (() => {
            if (kind === "fermentavel") return "kg";
            if (kind === "lupulo") return "g";
            if (kind === "levedura") return "un";
            return "un";
          })();

          const item: InventoryItem = {
            id: typeof rawItem.id === "string" && rawItem.id.trim()
              ? rawItem.id.trim()
              : (typeof globalThis.crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in globalThis.crypto
                ? globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()
                : Date.now().toString()),
            kind,
            name,
            amount,
            unit: kind === "levedura" ? "pacote" : normalizedUnit,
            price: Number(rawItem.price ?? 0),
            minAmount: Number(rawItem.minAmount ?? 0),
            alpha: rawItem.alpha !== undefined && rawItem.alpha !== null ? Number(rawItem.alpha) : undefined,
            potential: rawItem.potential !== undefined && rawItem.potential !== null ? Number(rawItem.potential) : undefined,
            color: rawItem.color !== undefined && rawItem.color !== null ? Number(rawItem.color) : undefined,
            attenuation: rawItem.attenuation !== undefined && rawItem.attenuation !== null ? Number(rawItem.attenuation) : undefined,
          };

          if (!item.name) {
            throw new Error("Inventário IA: o nome do item não pode ficar vazio.");
          }

          const existingItem = inventory.find((current) =>
            current.kind === item.kind && current.name.toLowerCase() === item.name.toLowerCase(),
          );

          const nextInventory = existingItem
            ? inventory.map((current) => current.id === existingItem.id
              ? { ...current, ...item, amount: Number(current.amount) + Number(item.amount) }
              : current)
            : [item, ...inventory];

          setInventory(nextInventory);
          return true;
        }
        case "CREATE_RECIPE": {
          const recipeData = (action.payload?.recipe ?? action.payload) as Partial<Recipe> & Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
          const newRecipe = normalizeAiRecipe(recipeData);

          // Check if recipe with same name exists recently to avoid duplicates on fallback retries
          const exists = recipes.some(r => r.name === newRecipe.name && (Date.now() - new Date(r.createdAt).getTime() < 30000));
          if (!exists) {
             setRecipes([...recipes, newRecipe as Recipe]);
          }
          return true;
        }
        case "UPDATE_RECIPE": {
          const payload = action.payload as Partial<Recipe> & Record<string, unknown>;

          if (!payload.id) {
            throw new Error("Payload de atualização de receita inválido: id ausente.");
          }

          const currentRecipes = recipes;
          const previousRecipe = currentRecipes.find((recipe) => recipe.id === payload.id);

          if (!previousRecipe) {
            throw new Error("Receita para atualização não encontrada.");
          }

          const mergedRecipe = {
            ...previousRecipe,
            ...payload,
            id: payload.id,
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          } as Recipe;

          const computedStats = recipeStats(mergedRecipe);
          const receitaAtualizadaCalculada = {
            ...mergedRecipe,
            og: computedStats.og,
            fg: computedStats.fg,
            abv: computedStats.abv,
            ibu: computedStats.ibu,
            srm: computedStats.srm,
          } as Recipe;

          const updatedRecipes = currentRecipes.map((recipe) => recipe.id === payload.id ? receitaAtualizadaCalculada : recipe);
          
          // Only update if something actually changed to avoid redundant updates on retries
          if (JSON.stringify(previousRecipe) !== JSON.stringify(receitaAtualizadaCalculada)) {
            setRecipes(updatedRecipes);
          }
          return true;
        }
        case "UPDATE_SETTING": {
          saveSettings(action.payload as Record<string, unknown>);
          return true;
        }
        default:
          return false;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Failed to apply AI action", error);
      return false;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-[calc(100vh-120px)] max-w-4xl mx-auto">
      <div className="mb-4 flex w-full flex-col flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center">
        <div className="flex w-full flex-row flex-wrap items-center gap-2 md:w-auto">
          <img src={geminiLogo} alt="Assistente IA" className="w-16 h-16 object-contain" />
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Assistente IA</h1>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
              <TooltipProvider>
                <Tooltip>
                  <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-help">
                      <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-[10px] py-0 h-5 font-normal">
                        {actualModelUsed || DEFAULT_GEMINI_3_MODEL}
                      </Badge>
                      {fallbackOccurred && (
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px] py-0 h-5 border-amber-500 text-amber-600 bg-amber-50">
                          Fallback Ativo
                        </Badge>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </TooltipTrigger>
                  <TooltipContent>
                    <p className="text-xs">Modelo em uso: {actualModelUsed || DEFAULT_GEMINI_3_MODEL}</p>
                    {fallbackOccurred && <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 mt-1">O modelo principal atingiu limite de cota.</p>}
                  </TooltipContent>
                </Tooltip>
              </TooltipProvider>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex w-full flex-row flex-wrap gap-2 md:w-auto">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => setIsHistoryOpen((prev) => !prev)}>
            Histórico
          </Button>

          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={clearConversation}>
            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
            Limpar
          </Button>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => {
            setMessages([]);
            setActiveChatId(null);
          }}>
            <PlusCircle className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
            Nova Conversa
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {!apiKey && (
        <Card className="mb-4 border-destructive bg-destructive/10">
          <CardContent className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4">
            <AlertCircle className="text-destructive" />
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-destructive">API Key não configurada</p>
              <p className="text-sm">VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY não foi encontrada. O assistente não funcionará sem uma chave válida.</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-1 gap-4 min-h-0">
        {isHistoryOpen && (
          <aside className="w-80 shrink-0 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-900/90 text-slate-100 shadow-lg backdrop-blur-sm overflow-hidden">
            <div className="border-b border-slate-700/80 p-3">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">Histórico de chats</h2>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-300">{history.length} chats</span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-slate-300">Total: {Number(totalHistorySizeMB).toFixed(3)} MB</p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3 p-3">
              <label className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-md border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/80 px-2 py-2 text-xs text-slate-200">
                <span>Habilitar sincronização em nuvem</span>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={cloudSyncEnabled}
                  onChange={(e) => setCloudSyncEnabled(e.target.checked)}
                  className="h-4 w-4 accent-primary"
                />
              </label>

              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="w-full"
                onClick={() => {
                  setHistory([]);
                  writeStoredAiChats([]);
                  setMessages([]);
                  setActiveChatId(null);
                }}
              >
                Excluir Todos os Chats
              </Button>
            </div>

            <div className="max-h-[calc(100vh-250px)] overflow-y-auto p-3 pt-0">
              {history.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Nenhum chat salvo ainda.</p>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {history.map((session) => (
                    <div
                      key={session.id}
                      className={`rounded-lg border p-2 text-left transition ${activeChatId === session.id ? "border-primary bg-slate-800" : "border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/60"}`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                        <button type="button" className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left" onClick={() => loadChatSession(session)}>
                          <p className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-slate-100">{session.title}</p>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-400">{new Date(session.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")}</p>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-300">{Number(getSizeMB(session)).toFixed(3)} MB</p>
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="rounded p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-destructive"
                          onClick={() => deleteChatSession(session.id)}
                          aria-label={`Excluir chat ${session.title}`}
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </aside>
        )}

        <Card className="flex-1 overflow-hidden flex flex-col bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 min-h-0 relative">
          <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 flex items-center gap-2 z-10">
            {live.isActive && (
              <Badge variant="outline" className="bg-red-50 text-red-600 border-red-200 animate-pulse flex items-center gap-1">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-red-600" />
                Gemini Live Ativo
                {live.isSpeaking && " (Falando)"}
              </Badge>
            )}
          </div>
          <ScrollArea className="flex-1 p-4" ref={scrollRef}>
            {messages.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-center space-y-4 pt-12">
                <img src={geminiLogo} alt="Assistente IA" className="w-20 h-20 object-contain opacity-20" />
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xl font-medium text-muted-foreground">Como posso ajudar na sua brassagem hoje?</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground max-w-xs mx-auto">
                    Você pode pedir receitas, tirar dúvidas técnicas ou analisar problemas na sua cerveja.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {messages.map((m, i) => {
                  const action = m.role === "model" ? parseAiAction(m.content) : null;
                  const recipePreview = !action && m.role === "model" ? parseRecipeMarkdown(m.content) : null;
                  const displayContent = action ? action.message : recipePreview?.explanation || m.content;

                  return (
                    <div key={i} className={`flex ${m.role === "user" ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"}`}>
                      <div className={`flex gap-3 max-w-[85%] ${m.role === "user" ? "flex-row-reverse" : "flex-row"}`}>
                        <div className={`mt-1 p-2 rounded-full h-fit ${m.role === "user" ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "bg-muted"}`}>
                          {m.role === "user" ? <User className="w-4 h-4" /> : <img src={geminiLogo} alt="Assistente IA" className="w-10 h-10 object-contain" />}
                        </div>
                        <div className="space-y-2">
                          <div className={`p-3 rounded-2xl ${
                            m.role === "user" 
                            ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground rounded-tr-none" 
                            : "bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border rounded-tl-none shadow-sm"
                          }`}>
                            {m.image && (
                              <img src={m.image} alt="Upload" className="max-w-xs rounded-lg mb-2 border" />
                            )}

                            {recipePreview ? (
                              <div className="space-y-3">
                                {recipePreview.explanation && (
                                  <p className="text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
                                    {recipePreview.explanation}
                                  </p>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            ) : (
                              <p className="text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">{displayContent}</p>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {m.role === "model" && m.content.includes("A IA respondeu sem conteúdo") && (
                            <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-300 bg-amber-50 dark:border-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-950/20 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-100">
                              <p className="font-medium">Resposta vazia da IA</p>
                              <p className="mt-1">A IA não retornou conteúdo útil. Você pode tentar novamente.</p>
                              <Button 
                                variant="secondary" 
                                size="sm" 
                                className="mt-3 w-full gap-2"
                                type="button"
                                onClick={retryLastRequest}
                              >
                                <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
                                Tentar novamente
                              </Button>
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
                {isLoading && (
                  <div className="flex justify-start">
                    <div className="flex gap-3 max-w-[85%]">
                      <div className="mt-1 p-2 rounded-full bg-muted h-fit">
                        <img src={geminiLogo} alt="Assistente IA" className="w-10 h-10 object-contain" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none p-4 shadow-sm">
                        <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin text-primary" />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
              </div>
            )}
          </ScrollArea>

          <form onSubmit={handleSend} className="p-4 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-t">
            {selectedImage && (
              <div className="relative inline-block mb-2">
                <img src={selectedImage} alt="Preview" className="h-20 w-20 object-cover rounded-md border" />
                <button 
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedImage(null)}
                  className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground rounded-full p-1 shadow-lg"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                className="hidden"
                ref={fileInputRef}
                onChange={handleImageUpload}
              />
              <Button 
                variant="outline" 
                size="icon" 
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={isLoading}
              >
                <ImageIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="icon"
                type="button"
                onClick={live.isActive ? live.disconnect : live.connect}
                disabled={isLoading || live.isConnecting}
                className={live.isActive ? "bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100" : ""}
                aria-label={live.isActive ? "Encerrar conversa Live" : "Iniciar Gemini Live"}
              >
                {live.isConnecting ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
                ) : live.isActive ? (
                  <MicOff className="w-5 h-5" />
                ) : (
                  <Brain className="w-5 h-5" />
                )}
              </Button>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  size="icon"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={toggleRecording}
                  disabled={isLoading || live.isActive}
                  aria-label={isRecording ? "Parar gravação de voz" : "Gravar por voz"}
                >
                  <Mic className={`w-5 h-5 ${isRecording ? "text-red-500 animate-pulse" : ""}`} />
                </Button>
                {live.isActive && (
                   <select 
                     value={liveModel} 
                     onChange={(e) => setLiveModel(e.target.value)}
                     className="absolute -top-8 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border rounded text-[10px] px-1 shadow-sm"
                   >
                     {GEMINI_3_LIVE_MODELS.map(m => <option key={m} value={m}>{m}</option>)}
                   </select>
                )}
              </div>
              <Input
                value={input}
                onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Pergunte sobre sua cerveja..."
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="flex-1"
              />
              <Button type="submit" disabled={isLoading || (!input.trim() && !selectedImage)}>
                <Send className="w-5 h-5" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/components/AppSidebar.tsx ===
import {

  FileText,
  Calculator,

  BarChart3,

import { NavLink, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useSettings } from "@/lib/settings";
import {

  SidebarGroup,
  SidebarGroupContent,

const mainItems = [

export function AppSidebar() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const [settings] = useSettings();
  const isEnglish = settings.language === "en-US";
  const t = (pt: string, en: string) => (isEnglish ? en : pt);

  const mainItems = [
    { title: t("Dashboard", "Dashboard"), url: "/", icon: Home },
    { title: t("Receitas", "Recipes"), url: "/recipes", icon: FileText },
    { title: t("Lotes", "Batches"), url: "/batches", icon: Beaker },
    { title: t("Inventário", "Inventory"), url: "/inventory", icon: Package },
    { title: t("Assistente IA", "AI Assistant"), url: "/ai-assistant", icon: Sparkles },
  ];

  const toolsItems = [
    { title: t("Calculadoras", "Calculators"), url: "/calculators", icon: Calculator },
    { title: "Brew Day", url: "/brew-day", icon: Clock3 },
    { title: t("Estilos", "Styles"), url: "/styles", icon: BookOpen },
    { title: t("Relatórios", "Reports"), url: "/reports", icon: BarChart3 },
  ];

  const profileItems = [
    { title: t("Equipamento", "Equipment"), url: "/profiles/equipamento", icon: Wrench },
    { title: t("Mostura", "Mash"), url: "/profiles/mostura", icon: CookingPot },
    { title: t("Fermentação", "Fermentation"), url: "/profiles/fermentacao", icon: Thermometer },
    { title: t("Água", "Water"), url: "/profiles/agua", icon: Droplets },
    { title: t("Estilos Personalizados", "Custom Styles"), url: "/profiles/estilos", icon: BookOpen },
  ];

  const getNavClassName = (path: string) => {
    const isActive = location.pathname === path;
    return isActive
      ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground font-medium shadow-brew"
      : "hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground";
  };

  const profilesActive = location.pathname.startsWith("/profiles");

  return (
    <Sidebar className="border-r border-border bg-[#edf3fb] text-slate-800 dark:bg-[#171b20] dark:text-[#eaf1ff]">

        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupLabel className="text-slate-500 dark:text-[#d9e7ff]/70 font-medium">{t("Principal", "Main")}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              {mainItems.filter(item => item.url !== "/inventory" || settings.inventory).map((item) => (
                <SidebarMenuItem key={item.title}>
                  <SidebarMenuButton asChild>
                    <NavLink
                      to={item.url}
                      className={getNavClassName(item.url)}
                    >
                      <item.icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                      <span>{item.title}</span>
                    </NavLink>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              ))}
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>

        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupLabel className="text-slate-500 dark:text-[#d9e7ff]/70 font-medium">{t("Ferramentas", "Tools")}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              {toolsItems.map((item) => (
                <SidebarMenuItem key={item.title}>
                  <SidebarMenuButton asChild>
                    <NavLink
                      to={item.url}
                      className={getNavClassName(item.url)}
                    >
                      <item.icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                      <span>{item.title}</span>
                    </NavLink>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              ))}

              <SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuButton asChild isActive={profilesActive}>
                  <NavLink to="/profiles/equipamento" className={profilesActive ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground font-medium shadow-brew" : "hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}>
                    <Layers className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>{t("Perfis", "Profiles")}</span>
                  </NavLink>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
                {profilesActive && (
                  <SidebarMenuSub>
                    {profileItems.map((item) => (
                      <SidebarMenuSubItem key={item.title}>
                        <SidebarMenuSubButton asChild isActive={location.pathname === item.url}>
                          <NavLink to={item.url}>
                            <item.icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            <span>{item.title}</span>
                          </NavLink>
                        </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                      </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                    ))}
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                )}
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>

        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuButton asChild>
                  <NavLink
                    to="/settings"
                    className={getNavClassName("/settings")}
                  >
                    <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>{t("Configurações", "Settings")}</span>
                  </NavLink>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>

              <SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuButton asChild>
                  <NavLink
                    to="/settings#cloud-sync"
                    className={getNavClassName("/settings")}
                  >
                    <Cloud className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>{t("Nuvem e sincronização", "Cloud & sync")}</span>
                  </NavLink>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>

              <SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuButton asChild>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => console.log("Offline version pending")}
                    className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground"
                  >
                    <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>{t("Versão Offline", "Offline Version")}</span>

                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>

=== ARQUIVO: src/components/BatchDetailPage.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { ArrowLeft, Check, Pause, Play, RotateCcw, Trash2, PackageMinus } from "lucide-react";
import { CartesianGrid, Line, LineChart, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from "recharts";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { batchAbv, batchStatuses, boilSchedule, deductInventory, recipeStats, type Batch } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { useSettings } from "@/lib/settings";
import { fmt, uid, useBatches, useInventory } from "@/lib/store";

const mmss = (s: number) => `${String(Math.floor(Math.max(0, s) / 60)).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(Math.floor(Math.max(0, s) % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}`;

function useTimer(totalSec: number) {
  const [left, setLeft] = useState(totalSec);
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => { setLeft(totalSec); setRunning(false); }, [totalSec]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return;
    const t = setInterval(() => setLeft(l => (l <= 1 ? (setRunning(false), 0) : l - 1)), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [running]);
  return { left, running, toggle: () => setRunning(r => !r), reset: () => { setRunning(false); setLeft(totalSec); } };
}

function TimerControls({ t }: { t: ReturnType<typeof useTimer> }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2">
      <Button size="sm" onClick={t.toggle}>{t.running ? <Pause className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" /> : <Play className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />}{t.running ? "Pausar" : "Iniciar"}</Button>
      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={t.reset} aria-label="Reiniciar"><RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
    </div>
  );
}

function BrewDay({ b }: { b: Batch }) {
  const r = b.recipe;
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const mash = r.mashSteps[step];
  const mashT = useTimer((mash?.minutes ?? 0) * 60);
  const boilT = useTimer(r.equipment.boilMinutes * 60);
  const events = boilSchedule(r);
  const announced = useRef(new Set<number>());
  const boilMinLeft = boilT.left / 60;
  useEffect(() => {
    events.forEach((e, i) => {
      if (boilT.left !== r.equipment.boilMinutes * 60 && boilMinLeft <= e.minutes && !announced.current.has(i)) {
        announced.current.add(i); toast(`Adicionar: ${e.label}`, { description: `${e.minutes} min restantes` });
      }
    });
    if (boilT.left === r.equipment.boilMinutes * 60) announced.current.clear();
  }, [boilT.left]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  useEffect(() => { if (mashT.left === 0 && mash) toast.success(`Passo "${mash.name}" concluído`); }, [mashT.left]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  return (
    <Card className="shadow-card">
      <CardHeader><CardTitle>Dia de brassagem</CardTitle></CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">Mostura</h3>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">{r.mashSteps.map((s, i) => <Button key={i} size="sm" variant={i === step ? "default" : "outline"} onClick={() => setStep(i)}>{s.name} · {s.tempC}°C</Button>)}</div>
          {mash ? <><div className="text-5xl font-mono font-bold text-primary">{mmss(mashT.left)}</div><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{mash.name}: manter {mash.tempC}°C por {mash.minutes} min</p><TimerControls t={mashT} /></> : <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Sem passos de mostura.</p>}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">Fervura</h3>
          <div className="text-5xl font-mono font-bold text-primary">{mmss(boilT.left)}</div>
          <TimerControls t={boilT} />
          <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm">
            {events.map((e, i) => {
              const done = boilT.left !== r.equipment.boilMinutes * 60 && boilMinLeft <= e.minutes;
              return <li key={i} className={`flex justify-between rounded px-2 py-1 ${done ? "bg-muted line-through text-muted-foreground" : ""}`}><span>{e.label}</span><span className="font-mono">{e.minutes} min</span></li>;
            })}
            {events.length === 0 && <li className="text-muted-foreground">Sem adições na fervura.</li>}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

export default function BatchDetailPage() {
  const [settings] = useSettings();
  const locale = settings.language || "pt-BR";
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [batches, saveBatches] = useBatches();
  const [inventory, saveInventory] = useInventory();
  const b = batches.find(x => x.id === id);
  const [m, setM] = useState({ date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16), sg: "", tempC: "", note: "" });
  if (!b) return <div className="py-12 text-center"><p className="text-muted-foreground">Lote não encontrado.</p><Link to="/batches" className="text-primary underline">Voltar aos lotes</Link></div>;

  const update = (patch: Partial<Batch>) => saveBatches(batches.map(x => (x.id === b.id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)));
  const stats = recipeStats(b.recipe);
  const statusIdx = batchStatuses.findIndex(s => s.id === b.status);
  const deduct = () => { saveInventory(deductInventory(b.recipe, inventory)); update({ inventoryDeducted: true }); toast.success("Ingredientes descontados do inventário"); };
  const setStatus = (s: Batch["status"]) => {
    update({ status: s });
    if (s === "brassagem" && !b.inventoryDeducted && confirm("Descontar os ingredientes do inventário agora?")) deduct();
  };
  const addMeasurement = () => {
    const sg = Number(m.sg.replace(",", ".")), tempC = Number(m.tempC.replace(",", "."));
    if (!(sg > 0.98 && sg < 1.2)) { toast.error("Informe uma densidade válida (ex: 1.012)."); return; }
    update({ measurements: [...b.measurements, { id: uid(), date: new Date(m.date).toISOString(), sg, tempC: Number.isFinite(tempC) ? tempC : 0, note: m.note }] });
    setM({ ...m, sg: "", note: "" });
  };
  const sorted = [...b.measurements].sort((a, c) => a.date.localeCompare(c.date));
  const chart = sorted.map(x => ({ data: new Date(x.date).toLocaleDateString(locale, { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit" }), SG: x.sg, Temp: x.tempC }));
  const abv = batchAbv(b);
  const dryHops = b.recipe.hops.filter(h => h.use === "dryhop");
  const addDays = (d: number) => { const t = new Date(b.brewDate + "T12:00"); t.setDate(t.getDate() + d); return t.toLocaleDateString(locale); };
  const numOrUndef = (v: string) => (v === "" ? undefined : Number(v.replace(",", ".")));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-16">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" onClick={() => navigate("/batches")} aria-label="Voltar"><ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
          <div><h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Lote #{b.number} · {b.name}</h1>
            <Link to={`/recipes/${b.recipeId}`} className="text-sm text-primary underline">Ver receita original</Link></div>
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => { if (confirm("Excluir este lote?")) { saveBatches(batches.filter(x => x.id !== b.id)); navigate("/batches"); } }}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Excluir</Button>
      </div>

      <Card className="shadow-card"><CardContent className="p-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {batchStatuses.map((s, i) => (
            <Button key={s.id} size="sm" variant={i === statusIdx ? "default" : i < statusIdx ? "secondary" : "outline"} onClick={() => setStatus(s.id)}>
              {i < statusIdx && <Check className="h-3 w-3 mr-1" />}{s.label}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </CardContent></Card>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <Card className="shadow-card lg:col-span-2">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Dados do lote</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
            <div className="space-y-1 col-span-2 sm:col-span-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Data da brassagem</Label><Input type="date" value={b.brewDate} onChange={e => update({ brewDate: e.target.value })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">OG medida (prev. {fmt(stats.og, 3)})</Label><Input type="number" step="0.001" value={b.measuredOg ?? ""} onChange={e => update({ measuredOg: numOrUndef(e.target.value) })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">FG medida (prev. {fmt(stats.fg, 3)})</Label><Input type="number" step="0.001" value={b.measuredFg ?? ""} onChange={e => update({ measuredFg: numOrUndef(e.target.value) })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Volume final (L)</Label><Input type="number" step="0.1" value={b.measuredVolumeL ?? ""} onChange={e => update({ measuredVolumeL: numOrUndef(e.target.value) })} /></div>
            <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-4 flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-sm">
              <span>ABV: <b>{abv !== undefined ? `${fmt(abv)}%` : "—"}</b></span>
              <span>IBU previsto: <b>{fmt(stats.ibu, 0)}</b></span>
              <span>Fermentação: <b>{b.recipe.fermentationTempC}°C por {b.recipe.fermentationDays} dias</b> (até {addDays(b.recipe.fermentationDays)})</span>
            </div>
            <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-4 space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Anotações</Label><Textarea value={b.notes} onChange={e => update({ notes: e.target.value })} /></div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Inventário e agenda</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3 text-sm">
            {b.inventoryDeducted ? <Badge className="bg-success text-success-foreground">Ingredientes já descontados</Badge> : <Button variant="outline" className="w-full" onClick={deduct}><PackageMinus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Descontar do inventário</Button>}
            <ul className="space-y-1">
              <li>Brassagem: <b>{addDays(0)}</b></li>
              {dryHops.map(h => <li key={h.id}>Dry hop {h.grams} g {h.name}: <b>{addDays(h.dryHopDay ?? 3)}</b></li>)}
              <li>Fim da fermentação: <b>{addDays(b.recipe.fermentationDays)}</b></li>
            </ul>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <BrewDay b={b} />

      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Medições de fermentação</CardTitle></CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr_1fr_2fr_auto] gap-2 items-end">
            <div className="space-y-1 col-span-2 sm:col-span-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Data e hora</Label><Input type="datetime-local" value={m.date} onChange={e => setM({ ...m, date: e.target.value })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Densidade (SG)</Label><Input type="number" step="0.001" placeholder="1.020" value={m.sg} onChange={e => setM({ ...m, sg: e.target.value })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Temp. (°C)</Label><Input type="number" step="0.1" value={m.tempC} onChange={e => setM({ ...m, tempC: e.target.value })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1 col-span-2 sm:col-span-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nota</Label><Input value={m.note} onChange={e => setM({ ...m, note: e.target.value })} /></div>
            <Button onClick={addMeasurement} className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">Adicionar</Button>
          </div>
          {chart.length > 0 && (
            <div className="h-64">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <LineChart data={chart}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="hsl(var(--border))" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="data" stroke="hsl(var(--muted-foreground))" fontSize={12} />

                <span>{new Date(x.date).toLocaleString(locale)}</span><span className="font-mono">{x.sg.toFixed(3)}</span><span>{x.tempC}°C</span><span className="text-muted-foreground flex-1">{x.note}</span>

=== ARQUIVO: src/components/BatchesPage.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Progress } from "@/components/ui/progress";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { batchAbv, batchStatuses } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { useSettings } from "@/lib/settings";
import { fmt, uid, useBatches, useRecipes } from "@/lib/store";

export default function BatchesPage() {
  const [settings] = useSettings();
  const locale = settings.language || "pt-BR";
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [batches, saveBatches] = useBatches();
  const [recipes] = useRecipes();
  const [recipeId, setRecipeId] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("todos");

  const create = () => {
    const r = recipes.find(x => x.id === recipeId);
    if (!r) { toast.error("Escolha uma receita."); return; }
    const number = batches.reduce((m, b) => Math.max(m, b.number), 0) + 1;
    const b = { id: uid(), number, name: r.name, recipeId: r.id, recipe: structuredClone(r), status: "planejamento" as const, brewDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), measurements: [], notes: "", inventoryDeducted: false, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    saveBatches([...batches, b]);
    navigate(`/batches/${b.id}`);
  };
  const list = [...batches].filter(b => filter === "todos" || b.status === filter).sort((a, b) => b.number - a.number);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Lotes</h1><p className="text-muted-foreground">Acompanhe cada brassagem do planejamento ao copo.</p></div>
      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardContent className="p-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Select value={recipeId} onValueChange={setRecipeId}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-full sm:w-72"><SelectValue placeholder="Receita para o novo lote" /></SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>{recipes.map(r => <SelectItem key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
          </Select>
          <Button onClick={create} className="bg-gradient-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-brew"><Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Novo Lote</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {[{ id: "todos", label: "Todos" }, ...batchStatuses].map(s => (
          <Button key={s.id} size="sm" variant={filter === s.id ? "default" : "outline"} onClick={() => setFilter(s.id)}>{s.label} ({s.id === "todos" ? batches.length : batches.filter(b => b.status === s.id).length})</Button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {list.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted-foreground">Nenhum lote ainda. Crie um a partir de uma receita.</p>}
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {list.map(b => {
          const idx = batchStatuses.findIndex(s => s.id === b.status);
          const abv = batchAbv(b);
          return (
            <Card key={b.id} className="shadow-card hover:shadow-brew cursor-pointer" onClick={() => navigate(`/batches/${b.id}`)}>
              <CardHeader className="pb-2"><div className="flex justify-between gap-2"><CardTitle className="text-lg">#{b.number} {b.name}</CardTitle><Badge>{batchStatuses[idx].label}</Badge></div>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Brassagem: {new Date(b.brewDate + "T12:00").toLocaleDateString(locale)}</p></CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2">
                <Progress value={(idx / (batchStatuses.length - 1)) * 100} />
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm text-muted-foreground"><span>{b.measurements.length} medição(ões)</span><span>{abv !== undefined ? `${fmt(abv, 1, locale)}% ABV` : "ABV —"}</span></div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle2, Clock3, Droplets, FlaskConical, ListChecks, Plus, TimerReset } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useState } from "react";
import { recipeStats, volumes } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { fmt, useRecipes } from "@/lib/store";

export default function BrewDayPage() {
  const [recipes] = useRecipes();
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(recipes[0]?.id ?? "");
  const recipe = recipes.find(item => item.id === selectedId) ?? recipes[0] ?? null;

  if (!recipe) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Brew Day</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Crie uma receita para começar um brew day guiado.</p>
        <Link to="/recipes/new">
          <Button className="gap-2"><Plus className="h-4 w-4" />Nova receita</Button>
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const stats = recipeStats(recipe);
  const vol = volumes(recipe.equipment);
  const timeline = [
    { title: "Preparação", detail: `${recipe.fermentables.length} maltes · ${recipe.hops.length} lúpulos`, done: true },
    ...recipe.mashSteps.map(step => ({ title: step.name, detail: `${step.tempC}°C · ${step.minutes} min`, done: false })),
    { title: "Fervura", detail: `${recipe.equipment.boilMinutes} min · ${recipe.equipment.batchL} L`, done: false },
    ...recipe.hops.filter(item => item.use !== "dryhop").sort((a, b) => b.minutes - a.minutes).map(item => ({
      title: `Adicionar ${item.name}`,
      detail: `${item.grams} g · ${item.alpha}% · ${item.minutes} min`,
      done: false,
    })),
    { title: "Resfriamento", detail: `Até ${recipe.fermentationTempC}°C`, done: false },
    { title: "Fermentação", detail: `${recipe.fermentationDays} dias`, done: false },
  ];

  const checklist = [
    "Pesar maltes e adjuntos",
    "Aquecer água de mostura",
    "Executar os passos de mosturação",
    "Fervurar o mosto e registrar tempo",
    "Adicionar lúpulo conforme cronograma",
    "Resfriar até temperatura de inoculação",
    "Pitchar levedura",
    "Registrar fermentação e observações",
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">Operação</p>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Brew Day</h1>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <label className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Receita</label>
          <select
            value={recipe.id}
            onChange={e => setSelectedId(e.target.value)}
            className="rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm text-foreground"
          >
            {recipes.map(item => (
              <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.name}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
        {[
          ["Volume final", `${fmt(vol.fermenter, 0)} L`, "volume"],
          ["OG", stats.og.toFixed(3), "SG"],
          ["IBU", stats.ibu.toFixed(0), "IBU"],
          ["SRM", stats.srm.toFixed(1), "SRM"],
          ["ABV", `${stats.abv.toFixed(1)} %`, "ABV"],
        ].map(([label, value, suffix]) => (
          <Card key={label} className="shadow-card">
            <CardHeader className="pb-2">
              <CardTitle className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">{label}</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">{value}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{suffix}</div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[1.2fr_0.8fr]">
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2"><Clock3 className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />Linha do tempo</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            {timeline.map((step, index) => (
              <div key={`${step.title}-${index}`} className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-border bg-background p-3">
                <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary">
                  {step.done ? <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" /> : <span className="text-[10px] font-bold">{index + 1}</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="font-medium text-foreground">{step.title}</div>
                  <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{step.detail}</div>
                </div>
                <Badge variant={step.done ? "default" : "secondary"}>{step.done ? "OK" : "Pendente"}</Badge>
              </div>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2"><ListChecks className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />Checklist</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            {checklist.map((item, index) => (
              <label key={item} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-2">
                <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked={index < 2} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-border text-primary" />
                <span className="text-sm text-foreground">{item}</span>
              </label>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2"><FlaskConical className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />Resumo da receita</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-background p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground"><Droplets className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />Água</div>
            <div className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-foreground">{fmt(vol.preBoil, 0)} L pré-fervura</div>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{fmt(vol.postBoil, 0)} L pós-fervura · {fmt(vol.fermenter, 0)} L fermentador</p>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-background p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground"><TimerReset className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />Mostura</div>
            <div className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-foreground">{recipe.mashSteps.reduce((sum, step) => sum + step.minutes, 0)} min</div>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{recipe.mashSteps.map(step => `${step.name} ${step.tempC}°C`).join(" · ") || "Sem passos registrados"}</p>

      </Card>
    </div>
