import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Calculator, ChevronRight, RotateCcw, Search, ArrowLeftRight, Star, Thermometer } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { calculators, validateCalculator, type CalculatorDefinition, type Values, type Result } from "@/lib/calculator-registry";
import { convertUnit, unitFamilies, type UnitFamily } from "@/lib/brewing";
import { useSettings } from "@/lib/settings";

const format = (value: number, digits = 2, locale = "pt-BR") => Number(value).toLocaleString(locale, { minimumFractionDigits: digits, maximumFractionDigits: digits });
function ResultPanel({ results, error, locale }: { results: Result[]; error?: string; locale: string }) {
  return <section aria-label="Resultados" aria-live="polite" className="border-t border-border pt-6 mt-6">
    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-muted-foreground mb-4">RESULTADOS</h3>
    {error ? <p role="alert" className="text-destructive text-sm">{error}</p> : <dl className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-5">{results.map((result, i) => <div key={`${result.label}-${i}`} className="min-w-0"><dt className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-1">{result.label}</dt><dd className="text-2xl font-semibold text-primary break-words tabular-nums">{format(result.value, result.digits, locale)} <span className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">{result.unit}</span></dd></div>)}</dl>}
  </section>;
}
function UnitConverter({ fixed, locale }: { fixed: CalculatorDefinition["converter"]; locale: string }) {
  const [family, setFamily] = useState<UnitFamily>(fixed && fixed !== "all" ? fixed : "volume");
  const units = Object.keys(unitFamilies[family].units);
  const [from, setFrom] = useState(units[0]);
  const [to, setTo] = useState(units[1]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("1");
  const number = Number(input.replace(",", "."));
  const celsius = family === "temperature" ? convertUnit(number, family, from, "°C") : 0;
  const error = !input.trim() || !Number.isFinite(number) ? "Informe um número válido." : family === "temperature" && celsius < -273.15 ? "A temperatura não pode ser inferior ao zero absoluto." : family !== "temperature" && number < 0 ? "Informe um valor maior ou igual a zero." : undefined;
  return <>
    <div className="flex justify-end mb-4"><Button size="icon" variant="ghost" aria-label="Restaurar conversão" title="Restaurar conversão" onClick={() => {setInput("1");setFrom(units[0]);setTo(units[1]);}}><RotateCcw /></Button></div>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      {fixed === "all" && <div className="sm:col-span-2 space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="family">Grandeza</Label><Select value={family} onValueChange={value => { const next = value as UnitFamily; const keys = Object.keys(unitFamilies[next].units);setFamily(next);setFrom(keys[0]);setTo(keys[1]); }}><SelectTrigger id="family"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{Object.entries(unitFamilies).map(([key, item]) => <SelectItem key={key} value={key}>{item.label}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select></div>}
      <div className="space-y-2 sm:col-span-2"><Label htmlFor="convert-value">Valor</Label><Input id="convert-value" inputMode="decimal" value={input} onChange={e => setInput(e.target.value)} /></div>
      {[{id:"from",label:"De",value:from,set:setFrom},{id:"to",label:"Para",value:to,set:setTo}].map(field => <div className="space-y-2" key={field.id}><Label htmlFor={field.id}>{field.label}</Label><Select value={field.value} onValueChange={field.set}><SelectTrigger id={field.id}><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{units.map(unit => <SelectItem key={unit} value={unit}>{unit}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select></div>)}
    </div>
    <div className="flex justify-center mt-3"><Button variant="outline" size="icon" title="Inverter unidades" aria-label="Inverter unidades" onClick={()=>{setFrom(to);setTo(from);}}><ArrowLeftRight /></Button></div>
    <ResultPanel locale={locale} error={error} results={[{label:"Valor convertido",value:convertUnit(number,family,from,to),unit:to,digits:6}]} />
  </>;
}
function CalculationForm({ calculator, locale }: { calculator: CalculatorDefinition; locale: string }) {
  const defaults = Object.fromEntries(calculator.fields.map(field => [field.id, field.initial]));
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Values>(defaults);
  let error = validateCalculator(calculator, values);
  const results = error ? [] : calculator.calculate(values);
  if (!error && results.some(result => !Number.isFinite(result.value))) error = "Não foi possível calcular com estes valores. Confira as entradas.";
  return <>
    <div className="flex justify-end mb-4"><Button size="icon" variant="ghost" title="Restaurar valores" aria-label="Restaurar valores" onClick={() => setValues(defaults)}><RotateCcw /></Button></div>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">{calculator.fields.map(field => <div key={field.id} className="space-y-2 min-w-0"><Label htmlFor={`calc-${field.id}`} className="leading-relaxed">{field.label}</Label>{field.options ? <Select value={values[field.id]} onValueChange={value => setValues(old=>({...old,[field.id]:value}))}><SelectTrigger id={`calc-${field.id}`} className="h-auto min-h-10 text-left [&>span]:whitespace-normal"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{field.options.map(option=><SelectItem key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select> : <Input id={`calc-${field.id}`} inputMode="decimal" value={values[field.id]} onChange={e=>setValues(old=>({...old,[field.id]:e.target.value}))} />}</div>)}</div>
    <ResultPanel locale={locale} results={results} error={error} />
    {calculator.note && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed border-t border-border pt-4 mt-6">{calculator.note}</p>}
  </>;
}
const FAVORITES_KEY = "brewfather-calculator-favorites";
const readFavorites = () => {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return [] as string[];
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(FAVORITES_KEY) || "[]");
    return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string") : [];
  } catch {
    return [] as string[];
  }
};

export default function CalculatorsPage() {
  const [settings] = useSettings();
  const locale = settings.language || "pt-BR";
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState("alcohol");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<string[]>(readFavorites);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.localStorage.setItem(FAVORITES_KEY, JSON.stringify(favorites));
  }, [favorites]);

  const calculator = calculators.find(item => item.id === selected) ?? calculators[0];
  const normalized = (text: string) => text.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").toLowerCase();
  const filtered = calculators
    .filter(item => normalized(item.title).includes(normalized(search)))
    .sort((a, b) => Number(favorites.includes(b.id)) - Number(favorites.includes(a.id)) || a.title.localeCompare(b.title, "pt-BR") || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));

  const toggleFavorite = (id: string) => {
    setFavorites(current => current.includes(id) ? current.filter(item => item !== id) : [id, ...current]);
  };

  return <div className="space-y-6">
    <header><div className="flex items-center gap-3"><Calculator className="h-7 w-7 text-primary shrink-0" /><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Calculadoras</h1></div><p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2">Geral e mostura · {calculators.length} calculadoras</p></header>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-6 items-start">
      <aside aria-label="Escolher calculadora" className="min-w-0"><div className="relative mb-4"><Search className="absolute left-3 top-3 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" /><Input aria-label="Buscar calculadora" placeholder="Buscar calculadora" className="pl-9" value={search} onChange={e=>setSearch(e.target.value)} /></div>
        <nav className="max-h-72 lg:max-h-none overflow-y-auto">{(["Geral","Mostura"] as const).map(group=>{const items=filtered.filter(item=>item.group===group);return items.length>0 && <section key={group} className="mb-5"><h2 className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">{group==="Mostura"&&<Thermometer className="h-4 w-4" />}{group}</h2><div className="space-y-1">{items.map(item=><div key={item.id} className="flex items-center gap-1"><Button variant={selected===item.id?"secondary":"ghost"} aria-current={selected===item.id?"page":undefined} onClick={()=>setSelected(item.id)} className="flex-1 h-auto min-h-10 justify-between text-left px-3 py-2 whitespace-normal gap-2 hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground"><span className="min-w-0 leading-5">{item.title}</span>{selected===item.id&&<ChevronRight className="shrink-0" />}</Button><Button type="button" size="icon" variant="ghost" aria-label={favorites.includes(item.id) ? `Remover ${item.title} dos favoritos` : `Adicionar ${item.title} aos favoritos`} title={favorites.includes(item.id) ? "Favorita" : "Favoritar"} onClick={(event)=>{event.stopPropagation();toggleFavorite(item.id);}} className="shrink-0 h-9 w-9 rounded-full"><Star className={`h-4 w-4 ${favorites.includes(item.id) ? "fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400" : "text-muted-foreground"}`} /></Button></div>)}</div></section>;})}{filtered.length===0&&<p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground py-4">Nenhuma calculadora encontrada.</p>}</nav>
      </aside>
      <section aria-label={calculator.title} className="min-w-0 bg-card border border-border rounded-lg p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-foam">
        <div className="border-b border-border pb-4"><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1">{calculator.group}</p><h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-foreground break-words">{calculator.title}</h2></div>
        {calculator.converter ? <UnitConverter key={calculator.id} fixed={calculator.converter} locale={locale} /> : <CalculationForm key={calculator.id} calculator={calculator} locale={locale} />}
      </section>
    </div>
  </div>;
}

import type { CSSProperties, SVGProps } from "react";
import "@/assets/fonts/mug-fonts.css";

interface CanecaLimpaProps extends SVGProps<SVGSVGElement> {
  color: string;
  metrics?: { og: string; fg: string; abv: string; ibu: string; srm: string };
}

function beerInk(color: string): CSSProperties {
  let r: number, g: number, b: number;
  const hex = /^#?([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(color.trim());
  const rgb = /^rgb\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*\)$/i.exec(color.trim());
  if (hex) {
    const n = parseInt(hex[1], 16);
    r = (n >> 16) & 255; g = (n >> 8) & 255; b = n & 255;
  } else if (rgb) {
    r = Number(rgb[1]); g = Number(rgb[2]); b = Number(rgb[3]);
  } else {
    return {};
  }
  const lum = (0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b) / 255;
  // Cerveja escura pede letras claras; cerveja clara pede letras escuras.
  return lum < 0.45
    ? ({ "--mug-ink": "#f2e3c6", "--mug-accent": "#f0b429" } as CSSProperties)
    : ({ "--mug-ink": "#2a1503", "--mug-accent": "#8a4b0f" } as CSSProperties);
}

=== ARQUIVO: src/components/Dashboard.tsx ===
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Plus, Beaker, FileText, Package, TrendingUp, Calculator, Droplets, Clock3 } from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { batchStatuses, reportSummary } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { fmt, useBatches, useInventory, useRecipes } from "@/lib/store";

export default function Dashboard() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [recipes] = useRecipes();
  const [batches] = useBatches();
  const [inventory] = useInventory();
  const active = batches.filter(b => b.status !== "concluido" && b.status !== "planejamento");
  const low = inventory.filter(i => i.amount <= i.minAmount);
  const rep = reportSummary(batches);
  const stats = [
    { title: "Receitas", value: String(recipes.length), icon: FileText, change: "no seu caderno", to: "/recipes" },
    { title: "Lotes em andamento", value: String(active.length), icon: Beaker, change: `${batches.length} lotes no total`, to: "/batches" },
    { title: "Itens em estoque", value: String(inventory.length), icon: Package, change: `${low.length} com estoque baixo`, to: "/inventory" },
    { title: "Litros produzidos", value: `${fmt(rep.liters, 0)} L`, icon: TrendingUp, change: "lotes já brassados", to: "/reports" },
  ];
  const recent = [...batches].sort((a, b) => b.number - a.number).slice(0, 5);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Dashboard</h1><p className="text-muted-foreground">Visão geral da sua cervejaria.</p></div>
      <div className="grid gap-4 grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {stats.map(s => (
          <Card key={s.title} className="shadow-card cursor-pointer hover:shadow-brew" onClick={() => navigate(s.to)}>
            <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2"><CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">{s.title}</CardTitle><s.icon className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" /></CardHeader>
            <CardContent><div className="text-2xl font-bold">{s.value}</div><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{s.change}</p></CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Lotes recentes</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-2">
            {recent.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum lote ainda.</p>}
            {recent.map(b => (
              <button key={b.id} onClick={() => navigate(`/batches/${b.id}`)} className="w-full flex justify-between items-center rounded-md p-2 hover:bg-muted text-left">
                <span className="font-medium">#{b.number} {b.name}</span><Badge variant="secondary">{batchStatuses.find(s => s.id === b.status)?.label}</Badge>
              </button>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Ações rápidas</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <Button onClick={() => navigate("/recipes/new")} className="bg-gradient-primary text-primary-foreground"><Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Nova receita</Button>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate("/batches")}><Beaker className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Novo lote</Button>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate("/calculators")}><Calculator className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Calculadoras</Button>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate("/brew-day")}><Clock3 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Brew Day</Button>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate("/water")}><Droplets className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Química da água</Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function SmartHopIcon({ className = "w-6 h-6" }: { className?: string }) {

    <svg

      fill="none"

      strokeWidth="2"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      className={className}
    >
      <path d="M18.5 2l1 2.5 2.5 1-2.5 1-1 2.5-1-2.5-2.5-1 2.5-1 1-2.5z" />
      <path d="M13.5 3.5l.5 1.5 1.5.5-1.5.5-.5 1.5-.5-1.5-1.5-.5 1.5-.5.5-1.5z" />
      <path d="M11 22c-4.5 0-8-4-8-9 0-3.5 2.5-6.5 6-7.5h4c3 1 5 3.5 5 6.5 0 1-.2 2-.5 3" />
      <path d="M11 22v-9" />
      <path d="M6 12c1-2 3-3 5-3" />
      <path d="M15 11c-1-1.5-2.5-2.5-4-2.5" />
    </svg>
  );
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/components/InventoryPage.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { AlertTriangle, Pencil, Plus, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { fermentables, hops, miscs, yeasts } from "@/lib/catalog";
import type { InventoryItem, InventoryKind } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { fmt, money, uid, useInventory } from "@/lib/store";

const kinds: { id: InventoryKind; label: string; unit: string; priceLabel: string }[] = [
  { id: "fermentavel", label: "Fermentáveis", unit: "kg", priceLabel: "R$/kg" },
  { id: "lupulo", label: "Lúpulos", unit: "g", priceLabel: "R$/g" },
  { id: "levedura", label: "Leveduras", unit: "pacote", priceLabel: "R$/pacote" },
  { id: "outro", label: "Outros", unit: "un", priceLabel: "R$/un" },
];
const catalogFor = (k: InventoryKind) =>
  (k === "fermentavel" ? fermentables.map(f => ({ name: f.name, price: f.price, extra: { potential: f.potential, color: f.color } }))
  : k === "lupulo" ? hops.map(h => ({ name: h.name, price: h.price / 1000, extra: { alpha: h.alpha } }))
  : k === "levedura" ? yeasts.map(y => ({ name: y.name, price: y.price, extra: { attenuation: y.attenuation } }))
  : miscs.map(m => ({ name: m.name, price: m.price, extra: {} })))
  .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "pt-BR"));

export default function InventoryPage() {
  const [items, save] = useInventory();
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<InventoryKind>("fermentavel");
  const [form, setForm] = useState<InventoryItem | null>(null);
  const def = kinds.find(k => k.id === kind)!;
  const list = items.filter(i => i.kind === kind).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "pt-BR"));
  const low = items.filter(i => i.amount <= i.minAmount);
  const total = items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.amount * i.price, 0);
  const blank = (): InventoryItem => ({ id: uid(), kind, name: "", amount: 0, unit: def.unit, price: 0, minAmount: 0 });
  const submit = () => {
    if (!form || !form.name.trim()) return;
    save(items.some(i => i.id === form.id) ? items.map(i => (i.id === form.id ? form : i)) : [...items, form]);
    setForm(null);
  };
  const num = (v: string) => Number(v.replace(",", ".")) || 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Inventário</h1><p className="text-muted-foreground">{items.length} itens · valor em estoque {money(total)}</p></div>
        <Button onClick={() => setForm(blank())} className="bg-gradient-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-brew"><Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Adicionar item</Button>
      </div>
      {low.length > 0 && (
        <Card className="border-destructive/50"><CardContent className="p-4 flex gap-3 items-start text-sm">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 text-destructive shrink-0" />
          <div><b>Estoque baixo:</b> {low.map(i => `${i.name} (${fmt(i.amount, 2)} ${i.unit})`).join(", ")}</div>
        </CardContent></Card>
      )}
      <Tabs value={kind} onValueChange={v => { setKind(v as InventoryKind); setForm(null); }}>
        <TabsList className="flex-wrap h-auto">{kinds.map(k => <TabsTrigger key={k.id} value={k.id}>{k.label} ({items.filter(i => i.kind === k.id).length})</TabsTrigger>)}</TabsList>
      </Tabs>

      {form && (
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0"><CardTitle>{items.some(i => i.id === form.id) ? "Editar item" : `Novo item · ${def.label}`}</CardTitle><Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Fechar" onClick={() => setForm(null)}><X className="h-4 w-4" /></Button></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-5 items-end">
            <div className="space-y-1 sm:col-span-2"><Label>Do catálogo</Label>
              <Select value="" onValueChange={n => { const c = catalogFor(form.kind).find(x => x.name === n); if (c) setForm({ ...form, name: c.name, price: c.price, ...c.extra }); }}>
                <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Escolher do catálogo" /></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>{catalogFor(form.kind).map(c => <SelectItem key={c.name} value={c.name}>{c.name}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
              </Select></div>
            <div className="space-y-1 sm:col-span-3"><Label>Nome</Label><Input value={form.name} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label>Quantidade ({form.unit})</Label><Input type="number" step="0.01" value={form.amount} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, amount: num(e.target.value) })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label>Mínimo ({form.unit})</Label><Input type="number" step="0.01" value={form.minAmount} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, minAmount: num(e.target.value) })} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label>{def.priceLabel}</Label><Input type="number" step="0.01" value={form.price} onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, price: num(e.target.value) })} /></div>
            {form.kind === "lupulo" && (() => { const h = hops.find(x => x.name === form.name); return h ? (
              <div className="sm:col-span-5 rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 p-3 space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                <div><b className="text-foreground">Uso:</b> {h.usage} · {h.origin} · {h.alpha}% AA</div>
                <div><b className="text-foreground">Perfil:</b> {h.profile}</div>
                <div><b className="text-foreground">Substitutos:</b> {h.substitutes.join(", ")}</div>
                <div><b className="text-foreground">História:</b> {h.history}</div>
              </div>
            ) : null; })()}
            <Button onClick={submit} className="sm:col-span-2">Salvar</Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {list.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted-foreground">Nenhum item nesta categoria.</p>}
      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {list.map(i => {
          const hopInfo = i.kind === "lupulo" ? hops.find(h => h.name === i.name) : undefined;
          return (
          <Card key={i.id} className="shadow-card"><CardContent className="p-4 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              <div className="font-semibold">{i.name}</div>
              <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{fmt(i.amount, 2)} {i.unit} · {money(i.amount * i.price)}{i.alpha ? ` · ${i.alpha}% AA` : ""}{i.color !== undefined ? ` · ${i.color}°L` : ""}</div>
              {i.amount <= i.minAmount && <Badge variant="destructive" className="mt-1">Estoque baixo</Badge>}
              {hopInfo && (
                <div className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <div><b className="text-foreground">Uso:</b> {hopInfo.usage} · {hopInfo.origin}</div>
                  <div><b className="text-foreground">Perfil:</b> {hopInfo.profile}</div>
                  <div><b className="text-foreground">Substitutos:</b> {hopInfo.substitutes.join(", ")}</div>
                  <div><b className="text-foreground">História:</b> {hopInfo.history}</div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="flex">
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Editar" onClick={() => setForm(i)}><Pencil className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Excluir" onClick={() => save(items.filter(x => x.id !== i.id))}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
            </div>
          </CardContent></Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== ARQUIVO: src/components/ProfilesPage.tsx ===
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Plus, Pencil, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Link, Navigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

interface ProfileField {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  type?: "text" | "number";
  step?: string;
  placeholder?: string;
}

interface ProfileDef {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  storageKey: string;
  fields: ProfileField[];
}

interface SavedProfile {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  descricao?: string;
  values: Record<string, string>;
}

const PROFILE_TYPES: ProfileDef[] = [
  {
    slug: "equipamento",
    title: "Equipamento",
    description: "Configure os volumes e perdas do seu equipamento.",
    storageKey: "brewfather-profiles-equipamento",
    fields: [
      { key: "volumeLote", label: "Volume do lote (L)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "20" },
      { key: "volumeFervura", label: "Volume de fervura (L)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "26" },
      { key: "evaporacao", label: "Evaporação (%/h)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "10" },
      { key: "perdasTrub", label: "Perdas no trub e lúpulo (L)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "1" },
      { key: "perdasFermentador", label: "Perdas no fermentador (L)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "1" },
      { key: "eficiencia", label: "Eficiência de mostura (%)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "75" },
    ],
  },
  {
    slug: "mostura",
    title: "Mostura",
    description: "Perfil de temperaturas e tempos de repouso.",
    storageKey: "brewfather-profiles-mostura",
    fields: [
      { key: "tipo", label: "Tipo", placeholder: "Ex: Simples repouso" },
      { key: "passos", label: "Passos", placeholder: "Ex: 66°C por 60 min" },
      { key: "tempAlvo", label: "Temperatura alvo (°C)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "66" },
      { key: "tempoTotal", label: "Tempo total (min)", type: "number", placeholder: "60" },
      { key: "relacaoAgua", label: "Relação água/malte (L/kg)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "3" },
    ],
  },
  {
    slug: "fermentacao",
    title: "Fermentação",
    description: "Temperaturas e leveduras do seu processo.",
    storageKey: "brewfather-profiles-fermentacao",
    fields: [
      { key: "levedura", label: "Levedura", placeholder: "Ex: US-05" },
      { key: "tempMin", label: "Temperatura mínima (°C)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "18" },
      { key: "tempMax", label: "Temperatura máxima (°C)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "20" },
      { key: "duracao", label: "Duração (dias)", type: "number", placeholder: "10" },
    ],
  },
  {
    slug: "agua",
    title: "Água",
    description: "Perfil químico da sua água (ppm).",
    storageKey: "brewfather-profiles-agua",
    fields: [
      { key: "calcio", label: "Cálcio (ppm)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "50" },
      { key: "magnesio", label: "Magnésio (ppm)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "10" },
      { key: "sodio", label: "Sódio (ppm)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "20" },
      { key: "sulfato", label: "Sulfato (ppm)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "50" },
      { key: "cloreto", label: "Cloreto (ppm)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "50" },
      { key: "bicarbonato", label: "Bicarbonato (ppm)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "120" },
    ],
  },

      { key: "ogMin", label: "OG mínima", type: "number", step: "0.001", placeholder: "1.050" },
      { key: "ogMax", label: "OG máxima", type: "number", step: "0.001", placeholder: "1.065" },
      { key: "fgMin", label: "FG mínima", type: "number", step: "0.001", placeholder: "1.010" },
      { key: "fgMax", label: "FG máxima", type: "number", step: "0.001", placeholder: "1.014" },
      { key: "ibu", label: "IBU", type: "number", placeholder: "40" },
      { key: "srm", label: "SRM", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "8" },
      { key: "abv", label: "ABV (%)", type: "number", step: "0.1", placeholder: "5.5" },
    ],
  },
];

const readProfiles = (storageKey: string): SavedProfile[] => {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as SavedProfile[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
};

export default function ProfilesPage() {
  const { type } = useParams<{ type: string }>();
  const def = PROFILE_TYPES.find((p) => p.slug === type);
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<SavedProfile[]>([]);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<SavedProfile | null>(null);
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<SavedProfile>({ id: "", name: "", descricao: "", values: {} });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (def) setProfiles(readProfiles(def.storageKey));
  }, [def]);

  if (!def) return <Navigate to="/profiles/equipamento" replace />;

  const openNew = () => {
    setForm({ id: "", name: "", descricao: "", values: {} });
    setEditing(null);
    setShowForm(true);
  };

  const openEdit = (profile: SavedProfile) => {
    setForm({ ...profile, values: { ...profile.values } });
    setEditing(profile);
    setShowForm(true);
  };

  const persist = (next: SavedProfile[]) => {
    setProfiles(next);
    localStorage.setItem(def.storageKey, JSON.stringify(next));
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!form.name.trim()) return;
    const entry: SavedProfile = { ...form, id: form.id || crypto.randomUUID() };
    const next = editing
      ? profiles.map((p) => (p.id === editing.id ? entry : p))
      : [...profiles, entry];
    persist(next);
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditing(null);
  };

  const handleDelete = (id: string) => {
    persist(profiles.filter((p) => p.id !== id));
    if (editing?.id === id) {
      setShowForm(false);
      setEditing(null);
    }
  };

  const handleFieldChange = (key: string, value: string) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, values: { ...prev.values, [key]: value } }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Perfis</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">{def.title} — {def.description}</p>
        </div>
        <Button
          onClick={openNew}
          className="bg-gradient-primary hover:bg-primary-glow text-primary-foreground shadow-brew"
        >
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
          Novo Perfil
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {PROFILE_TYPES.map((t) => (
          <Link
            key={t.slug}
            to={`/profiles/${t.slug}`}
            className={
              t.slug === def.slug
                ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground font-medium px-4 py-2 rounded-md shadow-brew"
                : "hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground px-4 py-2 rounded-md"
            }
          >
            {t.title}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>

      {showForm && (
        <Card className="shadow-foam border-border/50">
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0">
            <CardTitle className="text-foreground">
              {editing ? "Editar Perfil" : "Novo Perfil"} — {def.title}
            </CardTitle>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => setShowForm(false)} aria-label="Fechar">
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="profile-name">Nome do Perfil</Label>
                <Input
                  id="profile-name"
                  value={form.name}
                  onChange={(e) => setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, name: e.target.value }))}
                  placeholder={`Ex: ${def.title === "Água" ? "Minha água" : "Meu perfil"}`}
                  className="border-border"
                />
              </div>
              {def.fields.map((field) => (
                <div key={field.key} className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor={`profile-${field.key}`}>{field.label}</Label>
                  <Input
                    id={`profile-${field.key}`}
                    type={field.type ?? "text"}
                    step={field.step}
                    value={form.values[field.key] ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => handleFieldChange(field.key, e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={field.placeholder}
                    className="border-border"
                  />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="profile-descricao">Descrição</Label>
              <Textarea
                id="profile-descricao"
                value={form.descricao ?? ""}
                onChange={(e) => setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, descricao: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="Observações sobre este perfil..."
                className="border-border min-h-[80px]"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowForm(false)}>
                Cancelar
              </Button>
              <Button
                onClick={handleSave}
                disabled={!form.name.trim()}
                className="bg-gradient-primary hover:bg-primary-glow text-primary-foreground shadow-brew"
              >
                Salvar Perfil
              </Button>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {profiles.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 text-muted-foreground border-2 border-dashed border-border rounded-lg">
          <p>Nenhum perfil de {def.title.toLowerCase()} salvo ainda</p>
          <p className="text-sm">Clique em "Novo Perfil" para criar o primeiro</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {profiles.map((profile) => (
            <Card key={profile.id} className="shadow-foam border-border/50">
              <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between space-y-0">
                <div>
                  <CardTitle className="text-foreground">{profile.name}</CardTitle>
                  {profile.descricao && (
                    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">{profile.descricao}</p>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => openEdit(profile)} aria-label="Editar perfil">
                    <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </Button>
                  <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => handleDelete(profile.id)} aria-label="Excluir perfil">
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm">
                  {def.fields.map((field) => {
                    const value = profile.values[field.key];
                    if (!value) return null;
                    return (
                      <div key={field.key}>
                        <dt className="text-muted-foreground">{field.label}</dt>
                        <dd className="text-foreground font-medium">{value}</dd>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </dl>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";

import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { ArrowLeft, Beaker, Plus, Save, ShoppingCart, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { fermentables as catFerm, hops as catHops, yeasts as catYeasts, miscs as catMiscs } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { defaultEquipment, recipeStats, shoppingList, srmToHex, styleCheck, type HopUse, type Recipe } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
