const emptyRecipe = (): Recipe => {
  const t = new Date().toISOString();
  return { id: uid(), name: "", styleCode: "18B", author: "", notes: "", equipment: { ...defaultEquipment }, mashSteps: [{ name: "Sacarificação", tempC: 66, minutes: 60 }], fermentationTempC: 19, fermentationDays: 14, fermentables: [], hops: [], yeasts: [], miscs: [], createdAt: t, updatedAt: t };
};
const numIn = (v: string) => { const n = Number(v.replace(",", ".")); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0; };
const hopUses: { id: HopUse; label: string }[] = [{ id: "fervura", label: "Fervura" }, { id: "primeiro_mosto", label: "1º mosto" }, { id: "whirlpool", label: "Whirlpool" }, { id: "dryhop", label: "Dry hop" }];

function NumField({ label, value, onChange, step = "0.1" }: { label: string; value: number; onChange: (v: number) => void; step?: string }) {
  return <div className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</Label><Input type="number" step={step} value={value} onChange={e => onChange(numIn(e.target.value))} /></div>;
}

function CatalogPicker<T extends { name: string }>({ items, placeholder, onPick, describe }: { items: T[]; placeholder: string; onPick: (i: T) => void; describe: (i: T) => string }) {
  return (
    <Select value="" onValueChange={v => { const i = items.find(x => x.name === v); if (i) onPick(i); }}>
      <SelectTrigger className="w-full sm:w-64"><Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" /><SelectValue placeholder={placeholder} /></SelectTrigger>
      <SelectContent>{items.map(i => <SelectItem key={i.name} value={i.name}>{i.name} <span className="text-muted-foreground">· {describe(i)}</span></SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
    </Select>
  );
}

const statusTone = (s: "baixo" | "ok" | "alto") => (s === "ok" ? "bg-success text-success-foreground" : "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground");

export default function RecipeFormPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [recipes, saveRecipes] = useRecipes();
  const [batches, saveBatches] = useBatches();
  const [inventory] = useInventory();
  const existing = recipes.find(r => r.id === id);
  const [r, setR] = useState<Recipe>(() => existing ? structuredClone(existing) : emptyRecipe());
  const [showShopping, setShowShopping] = useState(false);
  const styles = useMemo(() => allStyles(), []);
  const style = styles.find(s => s.code === r.styleCode);
  const stats = recipeStats(r);
  const check = style ? styleCheck(stats, style) : undefined;
  const set = (patch: Partial<Recipe>) => setR(prev => ({ ...prev, ...patch }));
  const setEq = (k: keyof Recipe["equipment"], v: number) => setR(p => ({ ...p, equipment: { ...p.equipment, [k]: v } }));
  function updateList<K extends "fermentables" | "hops" | "yeasts" | "miscs">(key: K, idx: number, patch: Partial<Recipe[K][number]>) {
    setR(p => ({ ...p, [key]: p[key].map((x, i) => (i === idx ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)) }));
  }
  const removeFrom = (key: "fermentables" | "hops" | "yeasts" | "miscs", idx: number) => setR(p => ({ ...p, [key]: (p[key] as unknown[]).filter((_, i) => i !== idx) }));

  const equipmentProfiles = readProfiles("equipamento"), mashProfiles = readProfiles("mostura"), fermProfiles = readProfiles("fermentacao"), waterProfiles = readProfiles("agua");
  const applyEquipment = (pid: string) => {
    const p = equipmentProfiles.find(x => x.id === pid); if (!p) return;
    const v = p.values, batchL = pn(v.volumeLote, r.equipment.batchL), boil = pn(v.volumeFervura, 0);
    const evapPct = pn(v.evaporacao, 0);
    set({ equipmentProfileId: pid, equipment: { ...r.equipment, batchL, trubLossL: pn(v.perdasTrub, r.equipment.trubLossL), fermenterLossL: pn(v.perdasFermentador, r.equipment.fermenterLossL), efficiency: pn(v.eficiencia, r.equipment.efficiency), evaporationLh: evapPct && boil ? boil * evapPct / 100 : r.equipment.evaporationLh } });
  };
  const applyMash = (pid: string) => {
    const p = mashProfiles.find(x => x.id === pid); if (!p) return;
    set({ mashProfileId: pid, mashSteps: [{ name: p.values.tipo || p.name, tempC: pn(p.values.tempAlvo, 66), minutes: pn(p.values.tempoTotal, 60) }] });
  };
  const applyFerm = (pid: string) => {
    const p = fermProfiles.find(x => x.id === pid); if (!p) return;
    const min = pn(p.values.tempMin, 18), max = pn(p.values.tempMax, min);
    set({ fermentationProfileId: pid, fermentationTempC: (min + max) / 2, fermentationDays: pn(p.values.duracao, r.fermentationDays) });
  };

  const save = (silent = false) => {
    if (!r.name.trim()) { toast.error("Dê um nome à receita."); return false; }
    const next = { ...r, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    saveRecipes(recipes.some(x => x.id === r.id) ? recipes.map(x => (x.id === r.id ? next : x)) : [...recipes, next]);
    if (!silent) { toast.success("Receita salva"); navigate("/recipes"); }
    return true;
  };
  const createBatch = () => {
    if (!save(true)) return;
    const number = batches.reduce((m, b) => Math.max(m, b.number), 0) + 1;
    const b = { id: uid(), number, name: r.name, recipeId: r.id, recipe: structuredClone(r), status: "planejamento" as const, brewDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), measurements: [], notes: "", inventoryDeducted: false, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    saveBatches([...batches, b]);
    toast.success(`Lote #${number} criado`);
    navigate(`/batches/${b.id}`);
  };
  const shopping = shoppingList(r, inventory);

  const Stat = ({ label, value, k }: { label: string; value: string; k?: keyof NonNullable<typeof check> }) => (
    <div className="rounded-md bg-muted/50 p-3">
      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-lg font-bold text-foreground">{value}</div>
      {check && k && style && <Badge className={`mt-1 text-[10px] ${statusTone(check[k])}`}>{check[k] === "ok" ? "no estilo" : check[k]}</Badge>}
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-16">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" onClick={() => navigate("/recipes")} aria-label="Voltar"><ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">{existing ? "Editar Receita" : "Nova Receita"}</h1>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setShowShopping(s => !s)}><ShoppingCart className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Lista de compras</Button>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={createBatch}><Beaker className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Criar lote</Button>
          <Button onClick={() => save()} className="bg-gradient-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-brew"><Save className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Salvar</Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Card className="shadow-card sticky top-16 z-10">
        <CardContent className="p-4 grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-7 gap-2">
          <Stat label="OG" value={fmt(stats.og, 3)} k="og" />
          <Stat label="FG" value={fmt(stats.fg, 3)} k="fg" />
          <Stat label="ABV" value={`${fmt(stats.abv)}%`} k="abv" />
          <Stat label="IBU" value={fmt(stats.ibu, 0)} k="ibu" />
          <div className="rounded-md bg-muted/50 p-3 flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-full border border-border" style={{ background: srmToHex(stats.srm) }} />
            <div><div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">SRM</div><div className="text-lg font-bold">{fmt(stats.srm)}</div>{check && <Badge className={`text-[10px] ${statusTone(check.srm)}`}>{check.srm === "ok" ? "no estilo" : check.srm}</Badge>}</div>
          </div>
          <Stat label="Custo" value={money(stats.cost.total)} />
          <Stat label="R$/litro" value={money(stats.costPerLiter)} />
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {showShopping && (
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Lista de compras</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-2">
            {shopping.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Adicione ingredientes à receita.</p>}
            {shopping.map(s => (
              <div key={s.kind + s.name} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 border-b border-border py-2 text-sm">
                <span className="font-medium">{s.name}</span>
                <span className="text-muted-foreground">precisa {fmt(s.amount, 2)} {s.unit} · tem {fmt(s.have, 2)}</span>
                <span className={s.missing > 0 ? "text-destructive font-semibold" : "text-success font-semibold"}>{s.missing > 0 ? `comprar ${fmt(s.missing, 2)} ${s.unit} (${money(s.cost)})` : "ok"}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold pt-2">Total a comprar: {money(shopping.reduce((a, s) => a + s.cost, 0))}</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Informações</CardTitle></CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="space-y-1"><Label>Nome</Label><Input value={r.name} onChange={e => set({ name: e.target.value })} placeholder="Ex: IPA Tropical" /></div>
          <div className="space-y-1"><Label>Estilo</Label>
            <Select value={r.styleCode} onValueChange={v => set({ styleCode: v })}>
              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>{styles.map(s => <SelectItem key={s.code} value={s.code}>{s.code.startsWith("custom") ? "" : `${s.code} · `}{s.name}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
            </Select>
            {style && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">OG {style.og.map(x => fmt(x, 3)).join("–")} · IBU {style.ibu.map(x => fmt(x, 0)).join("–")} · SRM {style.srm.map(x => fmt(x, 0)).join("–")} · ABV {style.abv.map(x => fmt(x)).join("–")}%</p>}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1"><Label>Autor</Label><Input value={r.author} onChange={e => set({ author: e.target.value })} /></div>
          <div className="space-y-1 sm:col-span-2"><Label>Notas</Label><Textarea value={r.notes} onChange={e => set({ notes: e.target.value })} /></div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Equipamento e volumes</CardTitle></CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <ProfileSelect label="Perfil de equipamento" value={r.equipmentProfileId} profiles={equipmentProfiles} onChange={applyEquipment} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
            <NumField label="Volume do lote (L)" value={r.equipment.batchL} onChange={v => setEq("batchL", v)} />
            <NumField label="Fervura (min)" value={r.equipment.boilMinutes} onChange={v => setEq("boilMinutes", v)} step="1" />
            <NumField label="Evaporação (L/h)" value={r.equipment.evaporationLh} onChange={v => setEq("evaporationLh", v)} />
            <NumField label="Perda trub (L)" value={r.equipment.trubLossL} onChange={v => setEq("trubLossL", v)} />
            <NumField label="Perda fermentador (L)" value={r.equipment.fermenterLossL} onChange={v => setEq("fermenterLossL", v)} />
            <NumField label="Eficiência (%)" value={r.equipment.efficiency} onChange={v => setEq("efficiency", v)} step="1" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Pré-fervura {fmt(stats.volumes.preBoil)} L · pós-fervura {fmt(stats.volumes.postBoil)} L · envase {fmt(stats.volumes.packaged)} L · {fmt(stats.grainKg, 2)} kg de fermentáveis</p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardHeader className="flex flex-row flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 space-y-0"><CardTitle>Fermentáveis</CardTitle>
          <CatalogPicker items={catFerm} placeholder="Adicionar fermentável" describe={i => `${i.color}°L`} onPick={i => set({ fermentables: [...r.fermentables, { id: uid(), name: i.name, kg: 1, potential: i.potential, color: i.color, type: i.type, price: i.price }] })} />
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-3">
          {r.fermentables.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum fermentável.</p>}
          {r.fermentables.map((f, i) => (
            <div key={f.id} className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_1fr_1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 items-end border-b border-border pb-3">
              <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nome</Label><Input value={f.name} onChange={e => updateList("fermentables", i, { name: e.target.value })} /><span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{stats.grainKg ? fmt(f.kg / stats.grainKg * 100, 0) : 0}%</span></div>
              <NumField label="kg" value={f.kg} step="0.01" onChange={v => updateList("fermentables", i, { kg: v })} />
              <NumField label="Cor (°L)" value={f.color} onChange={v => updateList("fermentables", i, { color: v })} />
              <NumField label="Potencial (SG)" value={f.potential} step="0.001" onChange={v => updateList("fermentables", i, { potential: v })} />
              <NumField label="R$/kg" value={f.price} onChange={v => updateList("fermentables", i, { price: v })} />
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover" onClick={() => removeFrom("fermentables", i)}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardHeader className="flex flex-row flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 space-y-0"><CardTitle>Lúpulos</CardTitle>
          <CatalogPicker items={catHops} placeholder="Adicionar lúpulo" describe={i => `${i.alpha}% AA`} onPick={i => set({ hops: [...r.hops, { id: uid(), name: i.name, grams: 20, alpha: i.alpha, minutes: 60, use: "fervura", price: i.price }] })} />
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-3">
          {r.hops.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum lúpulo.</p>}
          {r.hops.map((h, i) => {
            const cat = catHops.find(c => c.name === h.name);
            return (
            <div key={h.id} className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_1fr_1.2fr_1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 items-end border-b border-border pb-3">
              <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nome</Label><Input value={h.name} onChange={e => updateList("hops", i, { name: e.target.value })} /></div>
              <NumField label="g" value={h.grams} step="1" onChange={v => updateList("hops", i, { grams: v })} />
              <NumField label="Alfa (%)" value={h.alpha} onChange={v => updateList("hops", i, { alpha: v })} />
              <div><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Uso</Label>
                <Select value={h.use} onValueChange={v => updateList("hops", i, { use: v as HopUse })}><SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{hopUses.map(u => <SelectItem key={u.id} value={u.id}>{u.label}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select>
              </div>
              {h.use === "dryhop"
                ? <NumField label="Dia" value={h.dryHopDay ?? 3} step="1" onChange={v => updateList("hops", i, { dryHopDay: v })} />
                : <NumField label="Minutos" value={h.minutes} step="1" onChange={v => updateList("hops", i, { minutes: v })} />}
              <NumField label="R$/kg" value={h.price} onChange={v => updateList("hops", i, { price: v })} />
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover" onClick={() => removeFrom("hops", i)}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
              {cat && (
                <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-full text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <b className="text-foreground">Substitutos:</b> {cat.substitutes.join(", ")} · <b className="text-foreground">Perfil:</b> {cat.profile}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            );
          })}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 space-y-0"><CardTitle>Leveduras</CardTitle>
            <CatalogPicker items={catYeasts} placeholder="Adicionar levedura" describe={i => `${i.lab} · ${i.attenuation}%`} onPick={i => set({ yeasts: [...r.yeasts, { id: uid(), name: i.name, attenuation: i.attenuation, packs: 1, price: i.price }] })} />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            {r.yeasts.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma levedura (atenuação padrão de 75%).</p>}
            {r.yeasts.map((y, i) => (
              <div key={y.id} className="grid grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 items-end">
                <div><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nome</Label><Input value={y.name} onChange={e => updateList("yeasts", i, { name: e.target.value })} /></div>
                <NumField label="Aten. %" value={y.attenuation} step="1" onChange={v => updateList("yeasts", i, { attenuation: v })} />
                <NumField label="Pacotes" value={y.packs} step="1" onChange={v => updateList("yeasts", i, { packs: v })} />
                <NumField label="R$/pct" value={y.price} onChange={v => updateList("yeasts", i, { price: v })} />
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover" onClick={() => removeFrom("yeasts", i)}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 space-y-0"><CardTitle>Outros</CardTitle>
            <CatalogPicker items={catMiscs} placeholder="Adicionar item" describe={i => i.use} onPick={i => set({ miscs: [...r.miscs, { id: uid(), name: i.name, amount: 1, unit: i.price < 1 ? "g" : "un", use: i.use, price: i.price }] })} />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            {r.miscs.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum item.</p>}
            {r.miscs.map((m, i) => (
              <div key={m.id} className="grid grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 items-end">
                <div><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nome</Label><Input value={m.name} onChange={e => updateList("miscs", i, { name: e.target.value })} /></div>
                <NumField label={`Qtd (${m.unit})`} value={m.amount} onChange={v => updateList("miscs", i, { amount: v })} />
                <div><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Uso</Label><Input value={m.use} onChange={e => updateList("miscs", i, { use: e.target.value })} /></div>
                <NumField label="R$/un" value={m.price} onChange={v => updateList("miscs", i, { price: v })} />
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover" onClick={() => removeFrom("miscs", i)}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Mostura</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            <ProfileSelect label="Perfil de mostura" value={r.mashProfileId} profiles={mashProfiles} onChange={applyMash} />
            {r.mashSteps.map((s, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 items-end">
                <div><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Passo</Label><Input value={s.name} onChange={e => set({ mashSteps: r.mashSteps.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, name: e.target.value } : x)) })} /></div>
                <NumField label="°C" value={s.tempC} onChange={v => set({ mashSteps: r.mashSteps.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, tempC: v } : x)) })} />
                <NumField label="min" value={s.minutes} step="1" onChange={v => set({ mashSteps: r.mashSteps.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, minutes: v } : x)) })} />
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover" onClick={() => set({ mashSteps: r.mashSteps.filter((_, j) => j !== i) })}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
              </div>
            ))}
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => set({ mashSteps: [...r.mashSteps, { name: "Novo passo", tempC: 72, minutes: 15 }] })}><Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />Passo</Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Fermentação e água</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            <ProfileSelect label="Perfil de fermentação" value={r.fermentationProfileId} profiles={fermProfiles} onChange={applyFerm} />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <NumField label="Temperatura (°C)" value={r.fermentationTempC} onChange={v => set({ fermentationTempC: v })} />
              <NumField label="Duração (dias)" value={r.fermentationDays} step="1" onChange={v => set({ fermentationDays: v })} />
            </div>
            <ProfileSelect label="Perfil de água" value={r.waterProfileId} profiles={waterProfiles} onChange={v => set({ waterProfileId: v })} />
            {r.waterProfileId && <Button variant="link" className="px-0" onClick={() => navigate(`/water?profile=${r.waterProfileId}&liters=${fmt(stats.volumes.preBoil, 0)}`)}>Ajustar sais para este perfil →</Button>}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ProfileSelect({ label, value, profiles, onChange }: { label: string; value?: string; profiles: { id: string; name: string }[]; onChange: (id: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</Label>
      {profiles.length === 0 ? <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nenhum perfil salvo em Perfis.</p> : (
        <Select value={value ?? ""} onValueChange={onChange}><SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Escolher perfil" /></SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>{profiles.map(p => <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/components/RecipesPage.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Copy, Plus, Search, Star, Trash2, List, LayoutGrid, Grid3X3 } from "lucide-react";
import { recipeStats, srmToHex } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { allStyles, fmt, money, uid, useRecipes } from "@/lib/store";
import CanecaLimpa from "@/components/CanecaLimpa";

export default function RecipesPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [recipes, save] = useRecipes();
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [view, setView] = useState<"list" | "large" | "medium">("large");
  const styles = allStyles();
  const list = [...recipes]
    .filter(r => r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()))
    .sort((a, b) => Number(Boolean(b.favorite)) - Number(Boolean(a.favorite)) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "pt-BR") || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
  const duplicate = (id: string) => { const r = recipes.find(x => x.id === id); if (!r) return; save([...recipes, { ...structuredClone(r), id: uid(), name: `${r.name} (cópia)` }]); };
  const remove = (id: string) => { if (confirm("Excluir esta receita?")) save(recipes.filter(r => r.id !== id)); };
  const toggleFavorite = (id: string) => {
    save(recipes.map(r => r.id === id ? { ...r, favorite: !r.favorite, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } : r));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Receitas</h1><p className="text-muted-foreground">{recipes.length} receita(s)</p></div>
        <Button onClick={() => navigate("/recipes/new")} className="bg-gradient-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-brew"><Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Nova Receita</Button>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="relative w-full max-w-md"><Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" /><Input className="pl-9" placeholder="Buscar receitas..." value={q} onChange={e => setQ(e.target.value)} /></div>
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1" role="group" aria-label="Visualização das receitas">
          <Button size="sm" variant={view === "list" ? "default" : "ghost"} aria-pressed={view === "list"} onClick={() => setView("list")}><List />Lista</Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant={view === "large" ? "default" : "ghost"} aria-pressed={view === "large"} onClick={() => setView("large")}><LayoutGrid />Grande</Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant={view === "medium" ? "default" : "ghost"} aria-pressed={view === "medium"} onClick={() => setView("medium")}><Grid3X3 />Médio</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {list.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma receita encontrada.</p>}
      <div className={view === "list" ? "divide-y divide-border border-y border-border" : view === "medium" ? "grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4" : "grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3"}>
        {list.map(r => {
          const s = recipeStats(r);
          const style = styles.find(x => x.code === r.styleCode);
          const actions = (
            <div className="flex shrink-0" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={r.favorite ? "Remover dos favoritos" : "Adicionar aos favoritos"} onClick={() => toggleFavorite(r.id)}><Star className={`h-4 w-4 ${r.favorite ? "fill-primary text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`} /></Button>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Duplicar" onClick={() => duplicate(r.id)}><Copy className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Excluir" onClick={() => remove(r.id)}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
            </div>
          );
          if (view === "list") return (
            <div key={r.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 py-4">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 basis-48">
                <Button variant="link" className="h-auto max-w-full justify-start whitespace-normal px-0 text-left text-lg text-foreground" onClick={() => navigate(`/recipes/${r.id}`)}>{r.name}</Button>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{style?.name ?? "Sem estilo"}</p>
              </div>
              <dl className="grid flex-1 basis-72 grid-cols-5 gap-3 text-sm">
                {[["OG", fmt(s.og, 3)], ["FG", fmt(s.fg, 3)], ["ABV", `${fmt(s.abv)}%`], ["IBU", fmt(s.ibu, 0)], ["SRM", fmt(s.srm)]].map(([label, value]) => (
                  <div key={label}><dt className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</dt><dd className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{value}</dd></div>
                ))}
              </dl>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><span>{fmt(r.equipment.batchL)} L</span><Badge variant="secondary">{money(s.cost.total)}</Badge></div>
              {actions}
            </div>
          );
          return (
            <div key={r.id}>
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="min-w-0"><h2 className="text-lg font-semibold break-words">{r.name}</h2><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{style?.name ?? "Sem estilo"}</p></div>
                {actions}
              </div>
              <Card className="shadow-card hover:shadow-brew transition-shadow cursor-pointer overflow-hidden p-2" onClick={() => navigate(`/recipes/${r.id}`)}>
                <CanecaLimpa
                  color={srmToHex(s.srm)}
                  metrics={{ og: fmt(s.og, 3), fg: fmt(s.fg, 3), abv: `${fmt(s.abv)}%`, ibu: fmt(s.ibu, 0), srm: fmt(s.srm) }}
                  className={view === "large" ? "mx-auto block w-[179.2px] max-w-full h-auto" : "mx-auto block w-[134.4px] max-w-full h-auto"}
                  role="img"
                  aria-label={`OG ${fmt(s.og, 3)}, FG ${fmt(s.fg, 3)}, ABV ${fmt(s.abv)}%, IBU ${fmt(s.ibu, 0)}, SRM ${fmt(s.srm)}`}
                />
                <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground"><span>{fmt(r.equipment.batchL)} L</span><Badge variant="secondary">{money(s.cost.total)}</Badge></div>
              </Card>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/components/ReportsPage.tsx ===
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Bar, BarChart, CartesianGrid, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from "recharts";
import { recipeStats, reportSummary } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { fmt, money, useBatches, useInventory, useRecipes } from "@/lib/store";

export default function ReportsPage() {
  const [batches] = useBatches();
  const [recipes] = useRecipes();
  const [inventory] = useInventory();
  const rep = reportSummary(batches);
  const stock = inventory.reduce((s, i) => s + i.amount * i.price, 0);
  const cards = [
    { label: "Lotes", value: String(rep.totalBatches), sub: `${rep.finished} finalizados` },
    { label: "Litros produzidos", value: `${fmt(rep.liters)} L`, sub: "lotes já brassados" },
    { label: "Custo total", value: money(rep.cost), sub: `${money(rep.costPerLiter)} por litro` },
    { label: "Valor em estoque", value: money(stock), sub: `${inventory.length} itens` },
  ];
  const axis = { stroke: "hsl(var(--muted-foreground))", fontSize: 12 };
  const Chart = ({ data, x, y, title }: { data: Record<string, unknown>[]; x: string; y: string; title: string }) => (
    <Card className="shadow-card"><CardHeader><CardTitle className="text-lg">{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent className="h-64">
      {data.length === 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Sem dados ainda.</p> : (
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%"><BarChart data={data}><CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="hsl(var(--border))" /><XAxis dataKey={x} {...axis} /><YAxis {...axis} /><Tooltip /><Bar dataKey={y} fill="hsl(var(--primary))" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} /></BarChart></ResponsiveContainer>
      )}
    </CardContent></Card>
  );
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Relatórios</h1><p className="text-muted-foreground">Produção, custos e consumo de ingredientes.</p></div>
      <div className="grid gap-4 grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {cards.map(c => <Card key={c.label} className="shadow-card"><CardContent className="p-4"><div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{c.label}</div><div className="text-2xl font-bold">{c.value}</div><div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.sub}</div></CardContent></Card>)}
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Chart title="Litros por mês" data={rep.byMonth} x="month" y="litros" />
        <Chart title="Custo por mês (R$)" data={rep.byMonth.map(m => ({ ...m, custo: Math.round(m.custo * 100) / 100 }))} x="month" y="custo" />
        <Chart title="Lúpulos mais usados (g)" data={rep.hops} x="name" y="value" />
        <Chart title="Fermentáveis mais usados (kg)" data={rep.malts} x="name" y="value" />
      </div>
      <Card className="shadow-card"><CardHeader><CardTitle className="text-lg">Custo por receita</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent>
        <ul className="divide-y divide-border text-sm">{recipes.map(r => { const s = recipeStats(r); return <li key={r.id} className="flex justify-between py-2"><span>{r.name}</span><span>{money(s.cost.total)} · {money(s.costPerLiter)}/L</span></li>; })}</ul>
      </CardContent></Card>
    </div>
  );
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/components/SettingsPage.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Download, Upload, Trash2, Database, CheckCircle2, SlidersHorizontal, Cloud, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import SettingsPreferences from "@/components/SettingsPreferences";
import { defaultSettings, normalizeSettings } from "@/lib/settings-model";
import { readSettings, saveSettings } from "@/lib/settings";
import { restoreBackup } from "@/lib/backup";
import {
  cloudProviderLabels,
  connectProvider,
  disconnectProvider,
  getConnectedProviderAccountName,
  getProviderConnectionStatus,
  readCloudSyncConfig,
  resolveCloudConflict,
  saveCloudSyncConfig,
  supportedCloudProviders,
  syncCloudData,
  type CloudProvider,
} from "@/lib/cloud-sync";

const STORAGE_KEYS = {
  receitas: "brewfather-recipes",
  lotes: "brewfather-batches",
  "inventário": "brewfather-inventory",
  "perfil-equipamento": "brewfather-profiles-equipamento",
  "perfil-mostura": "brewfather-profiles-mostura",
  "perfil-fermentacao": "brewfather-profiles-fermentacao",
  "perfil-agua": "brewfather-profiles-agua",
  "perfil-estilos": "brewfather-profiles-estilos",
} as const;

type Backup = Record<string, unknown[]>;

const readAll = (): Backup => {
  const data: Backup = {};
  for (const [name, key] of Object.entries(STORAGE_KEYS)) {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
      data[name] = raw === null ? [] : (JSON.parse(raw) as unknown[]);
    } catch {
      data[name] = [];
    }
  }
  return data;
};

const writeAll = (data: Backup) => {
  const known = new Set(Object.values(STORAGE_KEYS));
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(data)) {
    const key = (STORAGE_KEYS as Record<string, string>)[name];
    if (key && Array.isArray(value)) {
      localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
    }
  }
  for (const key of known) {
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("brewfather-store", { detail: key.replace("brewfather-", "") }));
  }
};

const PROVIDERS: CloudProvider[] = supportedCloudProviders;

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const location = useLocation();
  const [counts, setCounts] = useState(readAll);
  const [importMsg, setImportMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [selectedProvider, setSelectedProvider] = useState<CloudProvider>("dropbox");
  const [cloudConfig, setCloudConfig] = useState<ReturnType<typeof readCloudSyncConfig>>(() => readCloudSyncConfig("dropbox"));
  const [draftCloudConfig, setDraftCloudConfig] = useState<ReturnType<typeof readCloudSyncConfig>>(() => readCloudSyncConfig("dropbox"));
  const [cloudSyncMsg, setCloudSyncMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [syncConflict, setSyncConflict] = useState<{ provider?: CloudProvider; [key: string]: unknown } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem("brewfather-cloud-conflict");
      setSyncConflict(raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null);
    } catch {
      setSyncConflict(null);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!location.hash) return;
    const target = document.getElementById(location.hash.slice(1));
    if (target) {
      target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    }
  }, [location.hash]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const next = readCloudSyncConfig(selectedProvider);
    setDraftCloudConfig(next);
    setCloudConfig(next);
  }, [selectedProvider]);

  const updateCloudConfig = (patch: Partial<typeof draftCloudConfig>) => {
    setDraftCloudConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch }));
  };

  const refreshCloudConfig = () => {
    const next = readCloudSyncConfig(selectedProvider);
    setCloudConfig(next);
    setDraftCloudConfig(next);
  };

  const handleSaveCloudConfig = () => {
    const next = saveCloudSyncConfig({ ...draftCloudConfig, provider: selectedProvider, enabled: draftCloudConfig.enabled });
    setCloudConfig(next);
    setDraftCloudConfig(next);

    if (!next.enabled) {
      setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: true, text: "Configuração da nuvem salva. A sincronização foi desativada." });
      return;
    }

    setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: true, text: `Configuração salva para ${cloudProviderLabels[selectedProvider]}.` });
  };

  const handleConnectProvider = async (provider: CloudProvider) => {
    try {
      const session = await connectProvider(provider);
      const next = readCloudSyncConfig(provider);
      setSelectedProvider(provider);
      setCloudConfig(next);
      setDraftCloudConfig(next);
      setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: true, text: `Conectado ao ${cloudProviderLabels[provider]} como ${session.accountName ?? "sua conta"}.` });
    } catch (error) {
      setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: false, text: error instanceof Error ? error.message : `Não foi possível conectar ao ${cloudProviderLabels[provider]}.` });
    }
  };

  const handleDisconnectProvider = async (provider: CloudProvider) => {
    try {
      await disconnectProvider(provider);
      refreshCloudConfig();
      setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: true, text: `Conexão com ${cloudProviderLabels[provider]} encerrada.` });
    } catch (error) {
      setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: false, text: error instanceof Error ? error.message : `Não foi possível desconectar o ${cloudProviderLabels[provider]}.` });
    }
  };

  const handleSyncProvider = async (provider: CloudProvider) => {
    const next = saveCloudSyncConfig({ ...readCloudSyncConfig(provider), provider, enabled: true });
    setCloudConfig(next);
    setDraftCloudConfig(next);
    setSelectedProvider(provider);

    try {
      const result = await syncCloudData(provider);
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem("brewfather-cloud-conflict");
        setSyncConflict(raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null);
      } catch {
        setSyncConflict(null);
      }
      setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: true, text: `Sincronização com ${cloudProviderLabels[provider]}: ${result.message}` });
    } catch (error) {
      setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: false, text: error instanceof Error ? error.message : `Não foi possível sincronizar com ${cloudProviderLabels[provider]}.` });
    }
  };

  const totalItems = Object.values(counts).reduce((sum, list) => sum + list.length, 0);

  const exportData = () => {
    const payload = {
      app: "brewcraft-web",
      version: 2,
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      data: readAll(),
      settings: readSettings(),
    };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = "brewcraft-backup.json";
    anchor.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    setImportMsg({ ok: true, text: "Backup exportado com sucesso." });
  };

  const importData = async (file: File) => {
    try {
      const text = await file.text();
      const parsed = JSON.parse(text) as Record<string, unknown>;
      const data = (parsed.data ?? parsed) as Record<string, unknown>;
      const keys = Object.keys(data).filter((k) => k in STORAGE_KEYS && Array.isArray(data[k]));
      if (keys.length === 0) {
        setImportMsg({ ok: false, text: "Arquivo não parece um backup do BrewCraft. Nada foi alterado." });
        return;
      }
      if (parsed.settings && typeof parsed.settings === "object") {
        saveSettings(normalizeSettings(parsed.settings));
      }
      writeAll(data as Backup);
      setCounts(readAll());
      setImportMsg({ ok: true, text: `Backup restaurado: ${keys.length} seções importadas.` });
    } catch {
      setImportMsg({ ok: false, text: "Não consegui ler o arquivo. Verifique se é um backup .json válido." });
    }
  };

  const clearAll = () => {
    if (!window.confirm("Apagar TODOS os dados deste navegador (receitas, lotes, inventário, perfis e preferências)? Recomendo exportar um backup antes.")) return;
    writeAll(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(STORAGE_KEYS).map((k) => [k, []])));
    saveSettings(defaultSettings);
    setCounts(readAll());
    setImportMsg({ ok: true, text: "Todos os dados foram apagados." });
  };

  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    receitas: "Receitas",
    lotes: "Lotes",
    "inventário": "Itens de inventário",
    "perfil-equipamento": "Perfis de equipamento",
    "perfil-mostura": "Perfis de mostura",
    "perfil-fermentacao": "Perfis de fermentação",
    "perfil-agua": "Perfis de água",
    "perfil-estilos": "Estilos personalizados",
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-3xl">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Configurações</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Gerencie seus dados: backup, restauração, limpeza e sincronização em nuvem.</p>
      </div>

      <Card className="shadow-foam border-border/50">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-foreground">
            <Database className="h-5 w-5 text-accent" />
            Seus dados neste navegador
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            {totalItems} itens salvos no total. Os dados ficam apenas neste navegador — exporte um backup para não perder nada.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <dl className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
            {Object.keys(STORAGE_KEYS).map((name) => (
              <div key={name} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-md border border-border px-3 py-2 bg-background">
                <dt className="text-muted-foreground">{labels[name]}</dt>
                <dd className="font-medium text-foreground">{counts[name]?.length ?? 0}</dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </dl>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="shadow-foam border-border/50">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-foreground">
            <SlidersHorizontal className="h-5 w-5 text-accent" />
            Preferências
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Configure tema, fórmulas, unidades, impressão de datas e comportamento do app.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <SettingsPreferences />
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card id="cloud-sync" className="shadow-foam border-border/50">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-foreground">
            <Cloud className="h-5 w-5 text-accent" />
            Nuvem e sincronização
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Conecte diretamente ao Dropbox, Google Drive ou OneDrive sem servidor próprio.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-5">
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
            {PROVIDERS.map((provider) => {
              const status = getProviderConnectionStatus(provider);
              const connectedName = getConnectedProviderAccountName(provider) ?? "Não conectada";
              const isSelected = selectedProvider === provider;
              return (
                <div
                  key={provider}
                  className={`cursor-pointer rounded-md border p-3 transition-colors ${isSelected ? "border-primary bg-primary/5" : "border-border bg-muted/20"}`}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedProvider(provider)}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{cloudProviderLabels[provider]}</span>
                    <span className={`rounded-full border px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-medium ${status.connected ? "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300" : "border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/15 text-red-700 dark:text-red-300"}`}>
                      {status.connected ? "Conectado" : "Desconectado"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Conta: {connectedName}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Última sincronização: {status.lastSync ? new Date(status.lastSync).toLocaleString() : "Nunca"}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Resultado: {status.lastSyncStatus}</p>
                  <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
                    {!status.connected ? (
                      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => void handleConnectProvider(provider)}>
                        Conectar
                      </Button>
                    ) : (
                      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => void handleDisconnectProvider(provider)}>
                        Desconectar
                      </Button>
                    )}
                    <Button size="sm" variant="default" onClick={() => void handleSyncProvider(provider)}>
                      <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                      Sincronizar
                    </Button>
                  </div>

                  {syncConflict && syncConflict.provider === provider && (
                    <div className="mt-3 rounded-md border border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200">
                      <p className="font-medium">Conflito detectado: Já existe um backup na nuvem. Qual versão você deseja manter?</p>
                      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="outline"
                          onClick={async () => {
                            await resolveCloudConflict(provider, "local-wins");
                            setSyncConflict(null);
                          }}
                        >
                          Manter local
                        </Button>
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="default"
                          onClick={async () => {
                            await resolveCloudConflict(provider, "remote-wins");
                            setSyncConflict(null);
                          }}
                        >
                          Manter nuvem
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label>Provedor ativo</Label>
              <div className="flex h-10 items-center rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium">
                {cloudProviderLabels[selectedProvider]}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="cloud-enabled">Estado</Label>
              <div className="flex h-10 items-center rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm">
                {draftCloudConfig.enabled ? "Sincronização ativada" : "Sincronização desativada"}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="cloud-folder">Pasta</Label>
              <Input
                id="cloud-folder"
                value={draftCloudConfig.folder}
                onChange={(event) => updateCloudConfig({ folder: event.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="cloud-file">Arquivo de sincronização</Label>
              <Input
                id="cloud-file"
                value={draftCloudConfig.fileName}
                onChange={(event) => updateCloudConfig({ fileName: event.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            <Button
              variant={draftCloudConfig.enabled ? "default" : "outline"}
              onClick={() => {
                const next = { ...draftCloudConfig, provider: selectedProvider, enabled: true };
                setDraftCloudConfig(next);
                saveCloudSyncConfig(next);
                setCloudConfig(next);
                setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: true, text: `Sincronização ativada para ${cloudProviderLabels[selectedProvider]}.` });
              }}
            >
              Ativar sincronização
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant={!draftCloudConfig.enabled ? "default" : "outline"}
              onClick={() => {
                const next = { ...draftCloudConfig, provider: selectedProvider, enabled: false };
                setDraftCloudConfig(next);
                saveCloudSyncConfig(next);
                setCloudConfig(next);
                setCloudSyncMsg({ ok: true, text: `Sincronização desativada para ${cloudProviderLabels[selectedProvider]}.` });
              }}
            >
              Desativar
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={() => handleSaveCloudConfig()} className="bg-gradient-primary hover:bg-primary-glow text-primary-foreground shadow-brew">
              Salvar
            </Button>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 p-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {cloudConfig.enabled && cloudConfig.token
              ? `Status: ${cloudConfig.lastSyncStatus || "pronto para sincronizar"}. Arquivo remoto: ${cloudConfig.folder}/${cloudConfig.fileName}`
              : "Ative a sincronização e conecte um provedor para sincronizar os dados."}
          </div>

          {cloudSyncMsg && (
            <div
              role="status"
              className={
                cloudSyncMsg.ok
                  ? "mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200"
                  : "mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-red-500/40 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-red-900 dark:text-red-200"
              }
            >
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{cloudSyncMsg.text}</span>
            </div>
          )}

        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="shadow-foam border-border/50">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-foreground">
            <Download className="h-5 w-5 text-accent" />
            Backup
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Baixe um arquivo com todas as suas receitas, lotes, inventário, perfis e preferências.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <Button onClick={exportData} className="bg-gradient-primary hover:bg-primary-glow text-primary-foreground shadow-brew">
            <Download className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            Exportar backup (.json)
          </Button>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}>
            <Upload className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            Restaurar backup
          </Button>
          <input
            ref={fileRef}
            type="file"
            accept="application/json,.json"
            className="hidden"
            onChange={(event) => {
              const file = event.target.files?.[0];
              if (file) void importData(file);
              event.target.value = "";
            }}
          />
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {importMsg && (
        <div
          role="status"
          className={
            importMsg.ok
              ? "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-success/40 bg-success/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-foreground"
              : "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-foreground"
          }
        >
          <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
          {importMsg.text}
        </div>
      )}

      <Card className="shadow-foam border-destructive/40">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-destructive">
            <Trash2 className="h-5 w-5" />
            Zona de perigo
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Apaga tudo de uma vez. Não dá para desfazer.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <Button variant="destructive" onClick={clearAll}>
            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            Apagar todos os dados
          </Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

import { type ReactNode, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Globe, Palette, Calculator, SlidersHorizontal, Ruler, Calendar, Beaker, Save, Package, Thermometer, FlaskConical } from "lucide-react";
import { useSettings } from "@/lib/settings";
import { measurementOptions, type AppSettings } from "@/lib/settings-model";

function Section({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border"><h2 className="border-b border-border bg-muted px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold">{title}</h2><div className="divide-y divide-border px-4">{children}</div></section>;
}
function Row({ label, description, icon: Icon = SlidersHorizontal, children }: { label: string; description?: string; icon?: typeof Globe; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 py-4"><div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3"><Icon className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" /><div className="min-w-0"><p className="text-sm font-medium">{label}</p>{description && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>}</div></div>{children}</div>;
}
export default function SettingsPreferences() {
  const [s, save] = useSettings();
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<"mash" | "measurements" | null>(null);
  const isEnglish = s.language === "en-US";
  const t = (pt: string, en: string) => (isEnglish ? en : pt);
  const select = (key: keyof AppSettings, label: string, options: [string, string][]) => <Select value={String(s[key])} onValueChange={v => save({ [key]: v })}><SelectTrigger aria-label={label} className="w-40 max-w-full"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{options.map(([v, text]) => <SelectItem key={v} value={v}>{text}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select>;
  const toggle = (key: keyof AppSettings, label: string) => <Switch aria-label={label} checked={s[key] === true} onCheckedChange={v => save({ [key]: v })} />;
  const segmented = (key: keyof AppSettings, label: string, options: [string, string][]) => <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1" role="group" aria-label={label}>{options.map(([v, text]) => <Button key={v} size="sm" variant={s[key] === v ? "default" : "outline"} aria-pressed={s[key] === v} onClick={() => save({ [key]: v })}>{text}</Button>)}</div>;
  const preset = (mode: "metric" | "imperial" | "us") => save(mode === "metric" ? { volume: "L", weight: "kg", hopWeight: "g", temperature: "°C", pressure: "bar", altitude: "m" } : { volume: mode === "us" ? "gal (US)" : "gal (UK)", weight: "lb", hopWeight: "oz", temperature: "°F", pressure: "psi", altitude: "ft" });
  return <>
    <div className="grid items-start gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <Section title={t("Geral", "General")}>
          <Row label={t("Próximo Lote #", "Next Batch #")} icon={Beaker}><Input aria-label={t("Próximo Lote", "Next Batch")} type="number" min={1} step={1} className="w-24" value={s.nextBatch} onChange={e => save({ nextBatch: Number(e.target.value) })} /></Row>
          <Row label={t("Idioma", "Language")} icon={Globe}>{select("language", t("Idioma", "Language"), [["pt-BR", "Português (Brasil)"], ["en-US", "English (US)"], ["es-ES", "Español"], ["fr-FR", "Français"], ["de-DE", "Deutsch"]])}</Row>
          <Row label={t("Tema", "Theme")} icon={Palette}>{select("theme", t("Tema", "Theme"), [["system", t("Automático", "Automatic")], ["light", t("Claro", "Light")], ["dark", t("Escuro", "Dark")]])}</Row>
        </Section>
        <Section title={t("Fórmulas", "Formulas")}>
          <Row label={t("Álcool por Volume", "ABV")} icon={Calculator}>{select("abvFormula", t("Álcool por Volume", "ABV"), [["standard", t("Padrão", "Standard")], ["alternative", t("Alternativa", "Alternative")]])}</Row>
          <Row label={t("Densidade Final", "Final Gravity")} description={t("Estimativa pela atenuação da levedura; ajuste opcional pela temperatura de mostura.", "Estimate based on yeast attenuation; optional mash-temperature adjustment.")} icon={Calculator}>{select("fgFormula", t("Densidade Final", "Final Gravity"), [["normal", t("Normal", "Normal")], ["mash", t("Temperatura da mostura", "Mash temperature")]])}</Row>
          <Row label={t("Fórmula do IBU", "IBU Formula")} icon={Calculator}>{select("ibuFormula", t("Fórmula do IBU", "IBU Formula"), [["tinseth", "Tinseth"], ["rager", "Rager"]])}</Row>
        </Section>
        <Section title={t("Recursos", "Features")}>
          <Row label={t("Monitoramento da Brassagem", "Brew monitoring")} icon={Thermometer}>{toggle("brewMonitoring", t("Monitoramento da Brassagem", "Brew monitoring"))}</Row>
          <Row label={t("Estoque", "Inventory")} icon={Package}>{toggle("inventory", t("Estoque", "Inventory"))}</Row>
          <Row label={t("Permitir estoque negativo", "Allow negative inventory")} description={t("O valor do estoque pode ser negativo.", "Inventory can go below zero.")} icon={Package}>{toggle("negativeStock", t("Permitir estoque negativo", "Allow negative inventory"))}</Row>

      <Button onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Concluir</Button>

import { ArrowLeft, BookOpenText } from "lucide-react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { allStyles } from "@/lib/store";

const fmtRange = (value: [number, number], digits = 3) => {
  const format = (n: number) => Number(n).toFixed(digits).replace(/0+$/, "").replace(/\.$/, "");
  return `${format(value[0])}–${format(value[1])}`;
};

export default function StyleDetailPage() {
  const { code } = useParams<{ code: string }>();
  const styles = allStyles();
  const style = styles.find(item => item.code === decodeURIComponent(code ?? ""));

  if (!style) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Estilo não encontrado</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Este estilo não existe ou foi removido.</p>
        <Link to="/styles"><Button variant="outline">Voltar para estilos</Button></Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isCustom = style.code.startsWith("custom-");
  const detail = style.details ?? {
    category: style.name,
    type: isCustom ? "Personalizado" : "Ale",
    overview: "Estilo com perfil característico e faixa recomendada para comparação com receitas.",
    aroma: "Aroma principal do estilo, com equilíbrio e boa limpeza fermentativa.",
    appearance: "Cor e espuma compatíveis com a categoria desta cerveja.",
    flavor: "Sabor correspondente ao estilo, com equilíbrio entre malte, lúpulo e fermentação.",
    mouthfeel: "Corpo e carbonatação adequados ao estilo proposto.",
    comments: "Observações gerais sobre o perfil do estilo para comparação na receita.",
    history: "Histórico do estilo e evolução de produção ao longo do tempo.",
    historicalStyle: "Classificação histórica da categoria.",
    ingredients: "Maltes, lúpulos e leveduras típicas da categoria.",
    comparison: "Comparação com estilos semelhantes da família cervejeira.",
    examples: "Exemplos relevantes do estilo.",
    origin: "Origem histórica do estilo.",
  };
  const ebcRange: [number, number] = [style.srm[0] * 1.97, style.srm[1] * 1.97];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <BookOpenText className="h-7 w-7 text-primary" />
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Estilo</p>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">{style.name}</h1>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Link to="/styles">
          <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Voltar
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">

            {style.code}
          </span>
          <span className="rounded border border-border bg-background px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {isCustom ? "Estilo personalizado" : "BJCP 2021"}
          </span>
        </div>

        <p className="mt-5 text-base leading-relaxed text-foreground">{detail.overview}</p>

        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-6">
          {([

            ["OG", style.og, ""],
            ["FG", style.fg, ""],
            ["IBU", style.ibu, ""],
            ["EBC", ebcRange, ""],
            ["SRM", style.srm, ""],
          ] as [string, [number, number], string][]).map(([label, range, suffix]) => (
            <div key={label} className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-background p-4">
              <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-muted-foreground">{label}</div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-foreground">
                {fmtRange(range, label === "OG" || label === "FG" || label === "ABV" ? 3 : 0)}
                {suffix}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-8 space-y-6 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">

            <p className="mt-1">{detail.historicalStyle}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Ingredientes</h3>
            <p className="mt-1">{detail.ingredients}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Comparação</h3>
            <p className="mt-1">{detail.comparison}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Exemplos</h3>
            <p className="mt-1">{detail.examples}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Origem</h3>
            <p className="mt-1">{detail.origin}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/components/StylesPage.tsx ===
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowRight, BookOpenText, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { allStyles } from "@/lib/store";

const fmtRange = (value: [number, number], digits = 3) => {
  const format = (n: number) => Number(n).toFixed(digits).replace(/0+$/, "").replace(/\.$/, "");
  return `${format(value[0])}–${format(value[1])}`;
};

export default function StylesPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const styles = useMemo(() => allStyles(), []);
  const normalized = (text: string) => text.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").toLowerCase();
  const filtered = styles.filter(style => normalized(`${style.code} ${style.name}`).includes(normalized(search)));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <BookOpenText className="h-7 w-7 text-primary" />
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Estilos</h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground">BJCP 2021 + estilos personalizados</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-3 shadow-foam">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-3 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            aria-label="Buscar estilo"
            placeholder="Pesquisar detalhadamente no guia de estilos..."
            className="pl-9"
            value={search}
            onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden shadow-foam">
        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum estilo encontrado.</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-border">
            {filtered.map(style => (
              <Link key={`${style.code}-${style.name}`} to={`/styles/${encodeURIComponent(style.code)}`} className="block">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-4 sm:p-5 hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors">
                  <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
                    <div className="mt-1 flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted text-muted-foreground">
                      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">{style.name}</span>
                        <span className="rounded bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
                          {style.code}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                        {style.code.startsWith("custom-") ? "Estilo personalizado" : "BJCP 2021"}
                      </p>
                      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                        <span className="rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1">OG {fmtRange(style.og, 3)}</span>
                        <span className="rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1">FG {fmtRange(style.fg, 3)}</span>
                        <span className="rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1">IBU {fmtRange(style.ibu, 0)}</span>
                        <span className="rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1">SRM {fmtRange(style.srm, 0)}</span>
                        <span className="rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1">ABV {fmtRange(style.abv, 1)}%</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

        icon: "h-10 w-10",
      },

    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },

);

  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,

}
