=== ARQUIVO: src/components/WaterPage.tsx ===
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Wand2 } from "lucide-react";
import { applySalts, ions, salts, suggestSalts, sulfateChlorideRatio, type WaterProfile } from "@/lib/recipe-calc";
import { fmt, profileNumber as pn, readProfiles } from "@/lib/store";

const zero: WaterProfile = { ca: 0, mg: 0, na: 0, so4: 0, cl: 0, hco3: 0 };
const presets: Record<string, WaterProfile> = {
  "Destilada / osmose": zero,
  "Pilsen (Plzeň)": { ca: 7, mg: 3, na: 2, so4: 5, cl: 5, hco3: 15 },
  "Munique": { ca: 76, mg: 17, na: 2, so4: 41, cl: 2, hco3: 152 },
  "Burton on Trent": { ca: 275, mg: 40, na: 25, so4: 610, cl: 35, hco3: 270 },
  "Dublin": { ca: 118, mg: 4, na: 12, so4: 54, cl: 19, hco3: 160 },
  "IPA americana (lupulada)": { ca: 110, mg: 18, na: 16, so4: 280, cl: 50, hco3: 0 },
  "NEIPA (macia)": { ca: 120, mg: 10, na: 15, so4: 75, cl: 175, hco3: 0 },
  "Equilibrada": { ca: 80, mg: 5, na: 25, so4: 80, cl: 75, hco3: 50 },
};
const fromProfile = (v: Record<string, string>): WaterProfile => ({ ca: pn(v.calcio), mg: pn(v.magnesio), na: pn(v.sodio), so4: pn(v.sulfato), cl: pn(v.cloreto), hco3: pn(v.bicarbonato) });

export default function WaterPage() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const saved = useMemo(() => readProfiles("agua"), []);
  const options: Record<string, WaterProfile> = { ...presets, ...Object.fromEntries(saved.map(p => [`Meu perfil: ${p.name}`, fromProfile(p.values)])) };
  const initialTarget = saved.find(p => p.id === params.get("profile"));
  const [sourceName, setSourceName] = useState("Destilada / osmose");
  const [targetName, setTargetName] = useState(initialTarget ? `Meu perfil: ${initialTarget.name}` : "Equilibrada");
  const [source, setSource] = useState<WaterProfile>(presets["Destilada / osmose"]);
  const [liters, setLiters] = useState(Number(params.get("liters")) || 30);
  const [add, setAdd] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const target = options[targetName] ?? zero;
  const result = applySalts(source, add, liters);
  const ratio = sulfateChlorideRatio(result);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Química da Água</h1><p className="text-muted-foreground">Calcule os sais para chegar ao perfil de água desejado.</p></div>
      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Água de origem</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            <Select value={sourceName} onValueChange={n => { setSourceName(n); setSource(options[n]); }}>
              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{Object.keys(options).map(n => <SelectItem key={n} value={n}>{n}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
            </Select>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              {ions.map(i => <div key={i.id} className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{i.label}</Label><Input type="number" value={source[i.id]} onChange={e => setSource({ ...source, [i.id]: Number(e.target.value) || 0 })} /></div>)}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Volume total de água (L)</Label><Input type="number" value={liters} onChange={e => setLiters(Number(e.target.value) || 0)} /></div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="shadow-card">
          <CardHeader><CardTitle>Sais (g)</CardTitle></CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-end">
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-48 space-y-1"><Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Perfil alvo</Label>
                <Select value={targetName} onValueChange={setTargetName}><SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{Object.keys(options).map(n => <SelectItem key={n} value={n}>{n}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select></div>
              <Button onClick={() => setAdd(suggestSalts(source, target, liters))}><Wand2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Sugerir sais</Button>
            </div>
            {salts.map(s => <div key={s.id} className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_6rem] items-center gap-2"><Label>{s.label}</Label><Input type="number" step="0.1" value={add[s.id] ?? 0} onChange={e => setAdd({ ...add, [s.id]: Number(e.target.value) || 0 })} /></div>)}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
      <Card className="shadow-card">
        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Resultado (ppm)</CardTitle></CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
            {ions.map(i => {
              const diff = result[i.id] - target[i.id];
              return <div key={i.id} className="rounded-md bg-muted/50 p-3"><div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{i.label}</div><div className="text-xl font-bold">{fmt(result[i.id], 0)}</div><div className={`text-xs ${Math.abs(diff) <= Math.max(10, target[i.id] * 0.1) ? "text-success" : "text-destructive"}`}>alvo {fmt(target[i.id], 0)} ({diff >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{fmt(diff, 0)})</div></div>;
            })}
          </div>
          <p className="mt-4 text-sm">Relação sulfato/cloreto: <b>{fmt(ratio, 2)}</b> — {ratio === 0 ? "sem cloreto" : ratio > 2 ? "realça o amargor (seca)" : ratio < 0.8 ? "realça o malte (macia)" : "equilibrada"}</p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/hooks/use-gemini-live.ts ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";

export interface UseGeminiLiveOptions {
  apiKey: string;
  model: string;
  systemInstruction?: string;
  onConnected?: () => void;
  onDisconnected?: () => void;
  onError?: (error: Error) => void;
  onResponse?: (text: string) => void;
}

export function useGeminiLive({
  apiKey,
  model,
  systemInstruction,
  onConnected,
  onDisconnected,
  onError,
  onResponse,
}: UseGeminiLiveOptions) {
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(false);
  const [isConnecting, setIsConnecting] = useState(false);
  const [isSpeaking, setIsSpeaking] = useState(false);
  const [isInterrupted, setIsInterrupted] = useState(false);
  
  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const audioContextRef = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
  const mediaStreamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const processorRef = useRef<ScriptProcessorNode | null>(null);
  const audioQueueRef = useRef<Int16Array[]>([]);
  const isPlayingRef = useRef(false);

  const stopAudio = useCallback(() => {
    if (processorRef.current) {
      processorRef.current.disconnect();
      processorRef.current = null;
    }
    if (mediaStreamRef.current) {
      mediaStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      mediaStreamRef.current = null;
    }
    if (audioContextRef.current) {
      // Don't close context to avoid issues with multiple sessions, just suspend if needed
    }
    audioQueueRef.current = [];
    isPlayingRef.current = false;
    setIsSpeaking(false);
  }, []);

  const disconnect = useCallback(() => {
    if (wsRef.current) {
      wsRef.current.close();
      wsRef.current = null;
    }
    stopAudio();
    setIsActive(false);
    setIsConnecting(false);
    onDisconnected?.();
  }, [onDisconnected, stopAudio]);

  const playNextInQueue = useCallback(() => {
    if (audioQueueRef.current.length === 0 || isPlayingRef.current || !audioContextRef.current) {
      if (audioQueueRef.current.length === 0) setIsSpeaking(false);
      return;
    }

    isPlayingRef.current = true;
    setIsSpeaking(true);
    
    const audioData = audioQueueRef.current.shift()!;
    const float32Data = new Float32Array(audioData.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < audioData.length; i++) {
      float32Data[i] = audioData[i] / 32768.0;
    }

    const buffer = audioContextRef.current.createBuffer(1, float32Data.length, 24000);
    buffer.getChannelData(0).set(float32Data);

    const source = audioContextRef.current.createBufferSource();
    source.buffer = buffer;
    source.connect(audioContextRef.current.destination);
    
    source.onended = () => {
      isPlayingRef.current = false;
      playNextInQueue();
    };
    
    source.start();
  }, []);

  const connect = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isActive || isConnecting) return;
    
    setIsConnecting(true);
    try {
      if (!audioContextRef.current) {
        audioContextRef.current = new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)({ sampleRate: 24000 });
      }

      const url = `wss://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/ws/google.ai.generativelanguage.v1alpha.GenerativeService.BidiGenerateContent?key=${apiKey}`;
      const ws = new WebSocket(url);
      wsRef.current = ws;

      ws.onopen = () => {
        setIsActive(true);
        setIsConnecting(false);
        onConnected?.();

        // Send setup
        const setup = {
          setup: {
            model: `models/${model}`,
            generation_config: {
              response_modalities: ["AUDIO"],
            },
            system_instruction: systemInstruction ? {
              parts: [{ text: systemInstruction }]
            } : undefined,
          }
        };
        ws.send(JSON.stringify(setup));
      };

      ws.onmessage = async (event) => {
        let data;
        if (event.data instanceof Blob) {
           const text = await event.data.text();
           data = JSON.parse(text);
        } else {
           data = JSON.parse(event.data);
        }

        if (data.serverContent) {
          if (data.serverContent.modelDraft) {
             onResponse?.(data.serverContent.modelDraft);
          }
          if (data.serverContent.inlineOutput) {
            const audioB64 = data.serverContent.inlineOutput.data;
            const binaryString = atob(audioB64);
            const len = binaryString.length;
            const bytes = new Int16Array(len / 2);
            const view = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(len));
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              view.setUint8(i, binaryString.charCodeAt(i));
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
              bytes[i] = view.getInt16(i * 2, true);
            }
            audioQueueRef.current.push(bytes);
            playNextInQueue();
          }
          if (data.serverContent.interrupted) {
            audioQueueRef.current = [];
            setIsInterrupted(true);
            setTimeout(() => setIsInterrupted(false), 1000);
          }
        }
      };

      ws.onerror = (ev) => {
        console.error("WebSocket Error:", ev);
        onError?.(new Error("WebSocket connection error"));
        disconnect();
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        disconnect();
      };

      // Start Microphone
      mediaStreamRef.current = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const source = audioContextRef.current.createMediaStreamSource(mediaStreamRef.current);
      processorRef.current = audioContextRef.current.createScriptProcessor(4096, 1, 1);

      processorRef.current.onaudioprocess = (e) => {
        if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
          const inputData = e.inputBuffer.getChannelData(0);
          // Convert Float32 to Int16
          const pcmData = new Int16Array(inputData.length);
          for (let i = 0; i < inputData.length; i++) {
            pcmData[i] = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, inputData[i])) * 0x7FFF;
          }
          
          const b64Data = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(pcmData.buffer)));
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({
            realtime_input: {
              media_chunks: [{
                mime_type: "audio/pcm;rate=24000",
                data: b64Data
              }]
            }
          }));
        }
      };

      source.connect(processorRef.current);
      processorRef.current.connect(audioContextRef.current.destination);

    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to connect to Gemini Live:", err);
      setIsConnecting(false);
      onError?.(err instanceof Error ? err : new Error("Failed to initialize audio or websocket"));
    }
  }, [apiKey, model, systemInstruction, isActive, isConnecting, onConnected, onDisconnected, onError, onResponse, playNextInQueue, disconnect]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      disconnect();
    };
  }, [disconnect]);

  return {
    isActive,
    isConnecting,
    isSpeaking,
    isInterrupted,
    connect,
    disconnect,
  };
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/hooks/use-mobile.tsx ===
import * as React from "react";

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768;

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`);
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT);
    };
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT);
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, []);

  return !!isMobile;
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/hooks/use-toast.ts ===
import * as React from "react";

import type { ToastActionElement, ToastProps } from "@/components/ui/toast";

const TOAST_LIMIT = 1;
const TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000;

type ToasterToast = ToastProps & {
  id: string;
  title?: React.ReactNode;
  description?: React.ReactNode;
  action?: ToastActionElement;
};

const actionTypes = {
  ADD_TOAST: "ADD_TOAST",
  UPDATE_TOAST: "UPDATE_TOAST",
  DISMISS_TOAST: "DISMISS_TOAST",
  REMOVE_TOAST: "REMOVE_TOAST",
} as const;

let count = 0;

function genId() {
  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  return count.toString();
}

type ActionType = typeof actionTypes;

type Action =
  | {
      type: ActionType["ADD_TOAST"];
      toast: ToasterToast;
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType["UPDATE_TOAST"];
      toast: Partial<ToasterToast>;
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType["DISMISS_TOAST"];
      toastId?: ToasterToast["id"];
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType["REMOVE_TOAST"];
      toastId?: ToasterToast["id"];
    };

interface State {
  toasts: ToasterToast[];
}

const toastTimeouts = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();

const addToRemoveQueue = (toastId: string) => {
  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {
    return;
  }

  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
    dispatch({
      type: "REMOVE_TOAST",
      toastId: toastId,
    });
  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY);

  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout);
};

export const reducer = (state: State, action: Action): State => {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "ADD_TOAST":
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),
      };

    case "UPDATE_TOAST":
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) => (t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t)),
      };

    case "DISMISS_TOAST": {
      const { toastId } = action;

      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,
      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity
      if (toastId) {
        addToRemoveQueue(toastId);
      } else {
        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {
          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id);
        });
      }

      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined
            ? {
                ...t,
                open: false,
              }
            : t,
        ),
      };
    }
    case "REMOVE_TOAST":
      if (action.toastId === undefined) {
        return {
          ...state,
          toasts: [],
        };
      }
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),
      };
  }
};

const listeners: Array<(state: State) => void> = [];

let memoryState: State = { toasts: [] };

function dispatch(action: Action) {
  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action);
  listeners.forEach((listener) => {
    listener(memoryState);
  });
}

type Toast = Omit<ToasterToast, "id">;

function toast({ ...props }: Toast) {
  const id = genId();

  const update = (props: ToasterToast) =>
    dispatch({
      type: "UPDATE_TOAST",
      toast: { ...props, id },
    });
  const dismiss = () => dispatch({ type: "DISMISS_TOAST", toastId: id });

  dispatch({
    type: "ADD_TOAST",
    toast: {
      ...props,
      id,
      open: true,
      onOpenChange: (open) => {
        if (!open) dismiss();
      },
    },
  });

  return {
    id: id,
    dismiss,
    update,
  };
}

function useToast() {
  const [state, setState] = React.useState<State>(memoryState);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    listeners.push(setState);
    return () => {
      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState);
      if (index > -1) {
        listeners.splice(index, 1);
      }
    };
  }, [state]);

  return {
    ...state,
    toast,
    dismiss: (toastId?: string) => dispatch({ type: "DISMISS_TOAST", toastId }),
  };
}

export { useToast, toast };


=== ARQUIVO: src/lib/backup.ts ===
import { read } from "./store";
import { readSettings, saveSettings } from "./settings";
import { normalizeSettings } from "./settings-model";

export const storageKeys: Record<string, string> = {
  receitas: "brewfather-recipes", lotes: "brewfather-batches", inventário: "brewfather-inventory",
  "perfil-equipamento": "brewfather-profiles-equipamento", "perfil-mostura": "brewfather-profiles-mostura",
  "perfil-fermentacao": "brewfather-profiles-fermentacao", "perfil-agua": "brewfather-profiles-agua", "perfil-estilos": "brewfather-profiles-estilos",
};
export type BackupData = Record<string, unknown[]>;
export function readBackupData(): BackupData {
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(storageKeys).map(([name, key]) => {
    if (name === "receitas") return [name, read("recipes")];
    if (name === "lotes") return [name, read("batches")];
    if (name === "inventário") return [name, read("inventory")];
    try { const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "[]"); return [name, Array.isArray(value) ? value : []]; } catch { return [name, []]; }
  }));
}
export const makeBackup = () => ({ app: "brewcraft-web", version: 2, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), data: readBackupData(), settings: readSettings() });
export type BackupPayload = ReturnType<typeof makeBackup>;
const HISTORY = "brewfather-backup-history";
export function readHistory(): { id: string; label: string; payload: BackupPayload }[] {
  try { const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(HISTORY) || "[]"); return Array.isArray(value) ? value : []; } catch { return []; }
}
export function snapshot(label: string) {
  const history = [{ id: crypto.randomUUID(), label, payload: makeBackup() }, ...readHistory()].slice(0, 5);
  localStorage.setItem(HISTORY, JSON.stringify(history));
}
export function restoreBackup(value: unknown): number {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error("Backup inválido");
  const input = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const data = (input.data ?? input) as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error("Backup inválido");
  const entries = Object.entries(storageKeys).filter(([name]) => Array.isArray(data[name]));
  if (!entries.length) throw new Error("Backup inválido");
  for (const [name] of entries) {
    const list = data[name] as unknown[];
    if (list.some(item => !item || typeof item !== "object" || Array.isArray(item))) throw new Error("Backup inválido");
  }
  snapshot("Antes da restauração");
  for (const [name, key] of entries) localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data[name]));
  if (input.settings) saveSettings(normalizeSettings(input.settings));
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("brewfather-store"));
  return entries.length;
}
export function clearData() {
  snapshot("Antes de apagar os dados");
  for (const key of Object.values(storageKeys)) localStorage.setItem(key, "[]");
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("brewfather-store"));
}

/// <reference types="node" />
import { strict as assert } from "node:assert";

import { calculators, validateCalculator } from "./calculator-registry";

const near = (actual: number, expected: number, tolerance = 0.00001) => assert.ok(Math.abs(actual - expected) < tolerance, `${actual} ≠ ${expected}`);
test("Álcool / Atenuação / Calorias", () => {const a=alcohol(1.05,1.01,355);near(a.abv,5.25);near(a.apparent,80);near(a.calories,163.74,0.01);});

test("Conversão de Cor",()=>{const c=calculators.find(c=>c.id==="color");assert.ok(c);near(c.calculate({value:"10",unit:"srm"})[1].value,19.7);});
test("Conversão de unidade",()=>near(convertUnit(1,"length","in","mm"),25.4));
test("Conversão de volumes",()=>near(convertUnit(1,"volume","gal (US)","L"),3.785411784));
test("Conversão de peso",()=>near(convertUnit(1,"weight","lb","g"),453.59237));
test("Conversão de Temperatura",()=>{near(convertUnit(20,"temperature","°C","°F"),68);near(convertUnit(273.15,"temperature","K","°C"),0);});

=== ARQUIVO: src/lib/brewing.ts ===
/** Brewing calculations use liters, kilograms, Celsius and SG unless stated otherwise. */
export const sgToPlato = (sg: number) => -616.868 + 1111.14 * sg - 630.272 * sg ** 2 + 135.997 * sg ** 3;
export const platoToSg = (p: number) => 1 + p / (258.6 - (p / 258.2) * 227.1);
export const alcohol = (og: number, fg: number, serving: number) => {
  const abv = (og - fg) * 131.25;
  const apparent = (og - fg) / (og - 1) * 100;
  const op = sgToPlato(og), fp = sgToPlato(fg);
  const realExtract = 0.1808 * op + 0.8192 * fp;
  const abw = (op - realExtract) / (2.0664 - 0.010664 * op);
  const calories = Math.max(0, (6.9 * abw + 4 * (realExtract - 0.1)) * fg * serving / 100);
  return { abv, apparent, real: (op - realExtract) / op * 100, calories };
};
export const correctedRefractometer = (originalBrix: number, currentBrix: number, correction: number) => {
  const ob = originalBrix / correction, fb = currentBrix / correction;
  // Sean Terrill linear model for fermented beer.
  return 1 - 0.00085683 * ob + 0.0034941 * fb;
};
const hydrometerFactor = (f: number) => 1.00130346 - 0.000134722124 * f + 0.00000204052596 * f ** 2 - 0.00000000232820948 * f ** 3;
export const correctedHydrometer = (sg: number, measuredC: number, calibrationC: number) => sg * hydrometerFactor(measuredC * 1.8 + 32) / hydrometerFactor(calibrationC * 1.8 + 32);
export const gravityAfterVolumeChange = (sg: number, initial: number, final: number) => 1 + (sg - 1) * initial / final;
export const carbonation = (temperature: number, target: number, liters: number, sugarFactor: number) => {
  const f = temperature * 1.8 + 32;
  const residual = 3.0378 - 0.050062 * f + 0.00026555 * f ** 2;
  const psi = -16.6999 - 0.0101059 * f + 0.00116512 * f ** 2 + 0.173354 * f * target + 4.24267 * target - 0.0684226 * target ** 2;
  return { residual, sugar: Math.max(0, target - residual) * liters * sugarFactor, psi, bar: psi / 14.5037738 };
};
export const hopFreshness = (alpha: number, lossSixMonths: number, days: number, temperature: number, packaging: number) => {
  const k = -Math.log(1 - lossSixMonths / 100) / 180;
  const retention = Math.exp(-k * days * 2 ** ((temperature - 20) / 10) * packaging);
  return { alpha: alpha * retention, retention: retention * 100 };
};
export const strikeTemperature = (grainKg: number, waterL: number, grainC: number, targetC: number, tunEquivalentL: number, tunC: number) => targetC + (0.41 * grainKg * (targetC - grainC) + tunEquivalentL * (targetC - tunC)) / waterL;
export const infusionWater = (grainKg: number, waterL: number, currentC: number, targetC: number, infusionC: number) => (targetC - currentC) * (0.41 * grainKg + waterL) / (infusionC - targetC);
export const tunCalibration = (grainKg: number, waterL: number, grainC: number, waterC: number, tunC: number, finalC: number) => (waterL * (waterC - finalC) - 0.41 * grainKg * (finalC - grainC)) / (finalC - tunC);
export const yeastPitch = (liters: number, sg: number, rate: number, packs: number, cellsPerPack: number, viability: number, starterLiters: number, yieldPerGram: number) => {
  const needed = liters * sgToPlato(sg) * rate;
  const available = packs * cellsPerPack * viability / 100;
  const extractGrams = starterLiters * 100;
  return { needed, available, deficit: Math.max(0, needed - available), propagated: available + extractGrams * yieldPerGram, extractGrams };
};
export const ibuTinseth = (grams: number, alphaPercent: number, minutes: number, liters: number, sg: number) => 1.65 * 0.000125 ** (sg - 1) * (1 - Math.exp(-0.04 * minutes)) / 4.15 * grams * alphaPercent * 10 / liters;
export const moreySrm = (kg1: number, lovibond1: number, kg2: number, lovibond2: number, liters: number) => 1.4922 * ((kg1 * lovibond1 + kg2 * lovibond2) * 2.2046226218 / (liters / 3.785411784)) ** 0.6859;

export const unitFamilies = {
  volume: { label: "Volume", units: { L: 1, mL: 0.001, "m³": 1000, "gal (US)": 3.785411784, "gal (UK)": 4.54609, "fl oz (US)": 0.0295735295625, "pint (US)": 0.473176473 } },
  weight: { label: "Peso", units: { kg: 1, g: 0.001, mg: 0.000001, lb: 0.45359237, oz: 0.028349523125 } },
  pressure: { label: "Pressão", units: { bar: 1, psi: 0.0689475729318, kPa: 0.01, Pa: 0.00001, atm: 1.01325 } },
  temperature: { label: "Temperatura", units: { "°C": 1, "°F": 1, K: 1 } },
  length: { label: "Comprimento", units: { m: 1, cm: 0.01, mm: 0.001, in: 0.0254, ft: 0.3048 } },
} as const;
export type UnitFamily = keyof typeof unitFamilies;
export function convertUnit(value: number, family: UnitFamily, from: string, to: string) {
  if (family === "temperature") {
    const c = from === "°F" ? (value - 32) / 1.8 : from === "K" ? value - 273.15 : value;
    return to === "°F" ? c * 1.8 + 32 : to === "K" ? c + 273.15 : c;
  }
  const factors: Record<string, number> = unitFamilies[family].units;
  return value * factors[from] / factors[to];
}

  {id:"color",title:"Conversão de Cor",group:"Geral",fields:[f("value","Cor",10,0),select("unit","Unidade de entrada","srm",[["srm","SRM"],["ebc","EBC"],["lovibond","°Lovibond"]])],note:"SRM = 1,3546 × °Lovibond − 0,76; EBC = 1,97 × SRM. Conversão aproximada de Lovibond; limitada a zero na faixa muito clara.",calculate:v=>{const x=n(v,"value"),srm=v.unit==="srm"?x:v.unit==="ebc"?x/1.97:Math.max(0,1.3546*x-0.76);return [r("Cor SRM",srm,"SRM"),r("Cor EBC",srm*1.97,"EBC"),r("Cor Lovibond",(srm+0.76)/1.3546,"°L")];}},
  ...([ ["units","Conversão de unidade","all"],["volume","Conversão de volumes","volume"],["weight","Conversão de peso","weight"],["temperature","Conversão de Temperatura","temperature"],["pressure","Conversão de pressão","pressure"] ] as const).map(([id,title,converter]):CalculatorDefinition=>({id,title,group:"Geral",fields:[],calculate:()=>[],converter})),
  {id:"strike",title:"Calculadora de Temperatura Desejada",group:"Mostura",fields:[f("grain","Malte (kg)",5,0.001),f("water","Água de mostura (L)",15,0.001),f("grainTemp","Temperatura do malte (°C)",20,0,40),f("target","Temperatura desejada da mostura (°C)",67,35,80),f("tun","Equivalente térmico da panela (L de água)",0,0),f("tunTemp","Temperatura da panela (°C)",20,0,80)],note:"Temperatura da água de ataque por balanço térmico. Equivalente térmico zero representa panela pré-aquecida; ignora perdas para o ambiente.",calculate:v=>[r("Temperatura da água de ataque",strikeTemperature(n(v,"grain"),n(v,"water"),n(v,"grainTemp"),n(v,"target"),n(v,"tun"),n(v,"tunTemp")),"°C",1)]},
  {id:"rest",title:"Calculadora da Temperatura de Repouso",group:"Mostura",fields:[f("grain","Malte (kg)",5,0.001),f("water","Água atual na mostura (L)",15,0.001),f("current","Temperatura atual (°C)",63,0,95),f("target","Novo repouso (°C)",72,0,95),f("infusion","Temperatura da água adicionada (°C)",100,0,100)],validate:v=>n(v,"target")<=n(v,"current")?"O novo repouso deve ser mais quente que o atual.":n(v,"infusion")<=n(v,"target")?"A água adicionada deve estar acima da temperatura do novo repouso.":undefined,note:"Infusão para elevar a temperatura, sem perdas térmicas. Confira se o volume total cabe na panela.",calculate:v=>{const added=infusionWater(n(v,"grain"),n(v,"water"),n(v,"current"),n(v,"target"),n(v,"infusion"));return [r("Água a adicionar",added,"L"),r("Água total após infusão",added+n(v,"water"),"L"),r("Relação água / malte",(added+n(v,"water"))/n(v,"grain"),"L/kg")];}},
  {id:"tun",title:"Calibração da Panela de Mostura",group:"Mostura",fields:[f("grain","Malte (kg)",5,0),f("water","Água adicionada (L)",15,0.001),f("grainTemp","Temperatura inicial do malte (°C)",20,0,40),f("waterTemp","Temperatura inicial da água (°C)",75,0,100),f("tunTemp","Temperatura inicial da panela (°C)",20,0,80),f("final","Temperatura medida após mistura (°C)",67,0,95)],validate:v=>n(v,"final")<=n(v,"tunTemp")?"A temperatura final deve superar a temperatura inicial da panela.":n(v,"final")>=n(v,"waterTemp")?"A temperatura final deve estar abaixo da temperatura inicial da água.":tunCalibration(n(v,"grain"),n(v,"water"),n(v,"grainTemp"),n(v,"waterTemp"),n(v,"tunTemp"),n(v,"final"))<0?"Estas medições resultam em capacidade térmica negativa; confira as temperaturas.":undefined,note:"Equivalente térmico em litros de água, não volume da panela. Misture e meça rapidamente; perdas de calor aumentam o valor estimado.",calculate:v=>{const equivalent=tunCalibration(n(v,"grain"),n(v,"water"),n(v,"grainTemp"),n(v,"waterTemp"),n(v,"tunTemp"),n(v,"final"));return [r("Equivalente térmico da panela",equivalent,"L de água",3),r("Capacidade térmica",equivalent*4.186,"kJ/°C")];}},
  {id:"ibu",title:"Amargor / IBU",group:"Geral",fields:[f("weight","Lúpulo (g)",30,0),f("alpha","Ácido alfa (%)",12.5,0,30),f("time","Fervura (min)",60,0),volume(),og()],note:"Modelo Tinseth para uma adição na fervura. Não estima dry hopping ou whirlpool.",calculate:v=>[r("Amargor",ibuTinseth(n(v,"weight"),n(v,"alpha"),n(v,"time"),n(v,"volume"),n(v,"og")),"IBU",1)]},
  {id:"srm",title:"Cor da receita / SRM",group:"Geral",fields:[f("grain1","Malte 1 (kg)",4,0),f("color1","Cor do malte 1 (°L)",2,0),f("grain2","Malte 2 (kg)",0.5,0),f("color2","Cor do malte 2 (°L)",40,0),volume()],note:"Modelo Morey com pesos convertidos para libras e volume para galões americanos.",calculate:v=>{const srm=moreySrm(n(v,"grain1"),n(v,"color1"),n(v,"grain2"),n(v,"color2"),n(v,"volume"));return [r("Cor estimada",srm,"SRM",1),r("Cor estimada",srm*1.97,"EBC",1)];}},
];

export function validateCalculator(calculator: CalculatorDefinition, values: Values): string | undefined {
  for (const field of calculator.fields) {
    if (field.options) continue;
    const raw = values[field.id]?.trim();
    const value = Number(raw?.replace(",", "."));
    if (!raw || !Number.isFinite(value)) return `Informe um número válido em “${field.label}”.`;
    if (field.min !== undefined && value < field.min) return `“${field.label}” deve ser no mínimo ${field.min.toLocaleString(getLocale(readSettings()))}.`;
    if (field.max !== undefined && value > field.max) return `“${field.label}” deve ser no máximo ${field.max.toLocaleString(getLocale(readSettings()))}.`;
  }
  return calculator.validate?.(values);
}

export interface BjcpStyleDetail {
  category: string;
  type: string;
  overview: string;
  aroma: string;
  appearance: string;
  flavor: string;
  mouthfeel: string;
  comments: string;
  history: string;
  historicalStyle: string;
  ingredients: string;
  comparison: string;
  examples: string;
  origin: string;
}

export interface BjcpStyle { code: string; name: string; og: [number, number]; fg: [number, number]; ibu: [number, number]; srm: [number, number]; abv: [number, number]; details?: BjcpStyleDetail }

const defaultDetail = (name: string): BjcpStyleDetail => {
  const base = name.toLowerCase();
  const type = /ipa|ale|stout|porter|brown|amber|pale|wit|saison|tripel|dubbel|lager|pils|helles|märzen|bock|weiss|alt|sour/i.test(base) ? (/ipa|ale|stout|porter|brown|amber|pale|wit|saison|tripel|dubbel|alt|sour/i.test(base) ? "Ale" : "Lager") : "Ale";
  return {
    category: name,
    type,
    overview: `Cerveja ${name.toLowerCase()} com perfil equilibrado entre malte e amargor, voltada para uma bebida limpa, saborosa e bem integrada em temperatura de serviço.`,
    aroma: `Aroma típico da categoria ${name.toLowerCase()}, com notas maltadas e de lúpulo em equilíbrio, com boa limpeza fermentativa e ausência de defeitos agressivos.`,
