  content: string;
  image?: string; // Base64 string
}

export interface AiRecipeResponse {
  recipe: Partial<Recipe>;
  explanation: string;
}

// System Prompt
const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Você é o agente autônomo do BrewCraft. Quando o usuário pedir para criar uma receita, adicionar algo ao inventário ou alterar uma configuração, você DEVE gerar um único bloco de código JSON com esta estrutura exata, e nada mais:
{
  "action": "ADD_INVENTORY" | "CREATE_RECIPE" | "UPDATE_RECIPE" | "UPDATE_SETTING",
  "payload": { ...dados específicos da ação... },
  "message": "Uma resposta em texto natural e amigável confirmando o que você acabou de fazer."
}
Nunca crie JSON de receita quando o usuário pedir para mexer no inventário.

Você tem acesso e conhecimento sobre as ferramentas do sistema: Calculadoras (IBU, ABV, Correção de Densidade, etc.), Inventário (Maltes, Lúpulos, Leveduras), Receitas, Configurações e histórico de uso do app. Quando o usuário tiver uma dúvida que possa ser resolvida por essas ferramentas, sugira o uso delas no menu lateral.

Seu objetivo é ajudar cervejeiros com dúvidas técnicas, criação de receitas, análise de problemas e sugestões de melhoria.

Conhecimentos específicos:
- Brassagem e mosturação (rampas, tempos, pH).
- Maltes, adjuntos e suas contribuições.
- Lúpulos, amargor (IBU) e técnicas (First Wort, Whirlpool, Dry Hop).
- Leveduras e fermentação (taxas de inoculação, atenuação, perfis).
- Cálculos cervejeiros: OG, FG, ABV, SRM (cor), BU/GU.
- Tratamento de água: sais minerais, pH e perfis de estilo.
- Estilos de cerveja (BJCP).
- Defeitos (off-flavors) e como corrigi-los.
- Equipamentos e boas práticas de sanitização.

Diretrizes:
- Seja tecnicamente preciso, didático e objetivo.
- Se não tiver certeza de algo, informe a incerteza em vez de inventar dados.
- Quando o usuário pedir para criar, salvar ou alterar uma receita, NUNCA diga que não consegue. Em vez disso, responda com JSON no formato da ação CREATE_RECIPE ou UPDATE_RECIPE conforme o caso.
- Quando o usuário pedir para adicionar ou ajustar itens no inventário, responda exclusivamente com JSON de ação ADD_INVENTORY.
- Para ADD_INVENTORY, o 'payload' DEVE seguir estritamente o formato: { name: string, type: 'Fermentable' | 'Hop' | 'Yeast' | 'Misc', amount: number, unit: 'kg' | 'g' | 'L' | 'ml' | 'un' }. Não invente outros campos. Use somente essas chaves, esses valores permitidos e o nome real do item do inventário (ex.: "Pale Ale", "Cascade", "SafAle US-05").
- Para CREATE_RECIPE, o 'payload' DEVE obrigatoriamente conter a estrutura completa de uma receita do BrewCraft: { name: string, style: string, batchSize: number, boilTime: number, efficiency: number, fermentables: [{ name, amount, color, yield, type }], hops: [{ name, amount, alpha, use, time }], yeasts: [{ name, attenuation }] }. Você DEVE popular esses arrays de ingredientes com dados realistas para que o sistema consiga calcular a cor, o IBU e o ABV. Nunca envie arrays vazios.
- Para UPDATE_RECIPE, o payload DEVE conter obrigatoriamente o 'id' da receita que será editada (você tem acesso aos IDs reais no contexto injetado), juntamente com a estrutura completa ou parcial da receita atualizada. Nunca apague receitas.
- Quando o usuário pedir para alterar uma configuração, responda exclusivamente com JSON de ação UPDATE_SETTING.
- Quando o usuário tiver dúvida que pode ser resolvida por ferramentas do app, sugira usar o menu lateral correspondente.
`;

export enum GeminiErrorClassification {
  MODEL_LIMIT = "MODEL_LIMIT",         // RPM, TPM, RPD for specific model
  GLOBAL_QUOTA = "GLOBAL_QUOTA",       // Billing, Project-wide limit
  SERVICE_OVERLOAD = "SERVICE_OVERLOAD", // 503, temporary busy
  AUTH_INVALID = "AUTH_INVALID",       // 401, 403
  NOT_FOUND = "NOT_FOUND",             // 404
  OTHER = "OTHER"
}

export interface GeminiError extends Error {
  status?: number;
  statusCode?: number;
  reason?: string;
  retryAfter?: number;
  classification?: GeminiErrorClassification;
}

export interface ChatResponse {
  text: string;
  modelUsed: string;
  isFallback: boolean;
  classification?: GeminiErrorClassification;
}

// ---- Rate-limit safeguards (shared by every Gemini text request) ----
export const GEMINI_LIMITS = {
  maxRequestsPerMinute: 10,
  minIntervalMs: 3000,
  maxAttemptsPerMessage: 3,
  defaultCooldownMs: 60000,
} as const;

export class GeminiRateLimitError extends Error {
  waitMs: number;
  constructor(waitMs: number) {
    super(`Limite de uso da IA atingido. Aguarde ${Math.max(1, Math.ceil(waitMs / 1000))}s antes de enviar outra mensagem.`);
    this.name = "GeminiRateLimitError";
    this.waitMs = waitMs;
  }
}

export class RequestLimiter {
  private stamps: number[] = [];
  private cooldownUntil = 0;
  constructor(
    private maxPerMinute: number = GEMINI_LIMITS.maxRequestsPerMinute,
    private minIntervalMs: number = GEMINI_LIMITS.minIntervalMs,
  ) {}

  /** Returns 0 when a request may go out now, otherwise how many ms to wait. */
  waitTime(now = Date.now()): number {
    this.stamps = this.stamps.filter((t) => now - t < 60000);
    let wait = Math.max(0, this.cooldownUntil - now);
    const last = this.stamps[this.stamps.length - 1];
    if (last !== undefined) wait = Math.max(wait, last + this.minIntervalMs - now);
    if (this.stamps.length >= this.maxPerMinute) wait = Math.max(wait, this.stamps[0] + 60000 - now);
    return wait;
  }

  /** Reserves a slot or throws GeminiRateLimitError (never waits silently). */
  acquire(now = Date.now()) {
    const wait = this.waitTime(now);
    if (wait > 0) throw new GeminiRateLimitError(wait);
    this.stamps.push(now);
  }

  pauseFor(ms: number, now = Date.now()) {
    this.cooldownUntil = Math.max(this.cooldownUntil, now + ms);
  }
}

export const geminiLimiter = new RequestLimiter();

/** Reads the server-suggested retry delay ("retryDelay":"23s" / "retry in 23.5s"). */
export const parseRetryDelayMs = (error: unknown): number | null => {
  const err = error as { message?: string; retryAfter?: number };
  if (typeof err?.retryAfter === "number" && err.retryAfter > 0) return err.retryAfter * 1000;
  const msg = String(err?.message ?? "");
  const m = msg.match(/retryDelay"?\s*:\s*"?(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)s/i) || msg.match(/retry in (\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*s/i);
  return m ? Math.ceil(parseFloat(m[1]) * 1000) : null;
};

// Global registry for model availability to avoid retrying failed models too soon
const modelBlacklist: Record<string, { until: number; reason: GeminiErrorClassification }> = {};

export class GeminiService {
  private ai: GoogleGenAI;
  private apiKey: string;
  private modelName: string;

  constructor(apiKey: string, modelName: string = DEFAULT_GEMINI_3_MODEL) {
    this.apiKey = apiKey;
    this.modelName = resolveGeminiModelName(modelName);
    this.ai = new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey });
  }

  private getRequestConfig(signal?: AbortSignal): GenerateContentConfig {
    return {
      abortSignal: signal,
      systemInstruction: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
      tools: [
        {
          googleSearch: {},
        },
      ],
    };
  }

  private buildContents(messages: Message[], context?: unknown): Content[] {
    return messages.map((message, index) => {
      const role = message.role === "system" ? "user" : message.role === "model" ? "model" : "user";
      const parts: Part[] = [];
      const isLastMessage = index === messages.length - 1;

      let text = message.content ?? "";
      if (isLastMessage && context !== undefined && context !== null && message.role === "user") {
        text += `\n\nContexto do BrewCraft: ${JSON.stringify(context)}`;
      }

      if (text) {
        parts.push({ text });
      }

      if (message.image) {
        const imageData = message.image.includes(",") ? message.image.split(",")[1] : message.image;
        const mimeMatch = message.image.match(/^data:(image\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+-]+);base64,/);
        const mimeType = mimeMatch ? mimeMatch[1] : "image/jpeg";
        parts.push({
          inlineData: {
            mimeType,
            data: imageData,
          },
        });
      }

      return {
        role,
        parts,
      };
    });
  }

  private classifyError(error: unknown): GeminiErrorClassification {
    const err = error as any;
    const message = (err.message || "").toUpperCase();
    const status = err.status || err.statusCode;

    if (status === 401 || status === 403 || message.includes("API_KEY_INVALID") || message.includes("UNAUTHORIZED")) {
      return GeminiErrorClassification.AUTH_INVALID;
    }

    if (status === 404) return GeminiErrorClassification.NOT_FOUND;

    if (status === 429) {
      if (message.includes("BILLING") || message.includes("PLAN") || message.includes("PROJECT QUOTA") || message.includes("ACCOUNT")) {
        return GeminiErrorClassification.GLOBAL_QUOTA;
      }
      return GeminiErrorClassification.MODEL_LIMIT;
    }

    if (status >= 500 || message.includes("OVERLOADED") || message.includes("BUSY") || message.includes("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE")) {
      return GeminiErrorClassification.SERVICE_OVERLOAD;
    }

    return GeminiErrorClassification.OTHER;
  }

  private async wait(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }

  private isModelAvailable(model: string): boolean {
    const record = modelBlacklist[model];
    if (!record) return true;
    if (Date.now() > record.until) {
      delete modelBlacklist[model];
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private markModelUnavailable(model: string, classification: GeminiErrorClassification, durationMs: number = 60000) {
    modelBlacklist[model] = {
      until: Date.now() + durationMs,
      reason: classification
    };
  }

  private modelsToTry() {
    const allModels = [this.modelName, ...GEMINI_3_FALLBACK_MODELS.filter((model) => model !== this.modelName)];
    const available = allModels.filter((m) => this.isModelAvailable(m));
    if (available.length === 0) {
      const soonest = Math.min(...allModels.map((m) => modelBlacklist[m]?.until ?? Date.now()));
      throw new GeminiRateLimitError(soonest - Date.now());
    }
    return available.slice(0, GEMINI_LIMITS.maxAttemptsPerMessage);
  }

  /** Runs one request with strict limits: at most maxAttemptsPerMessage calls, no hidden loops. */
  private async run<T>(request: (model: string) => Promise<T>): Promise<{ result: T; modelUsed: string }> {
    const models = this.modelsToTry();
    let lastError: GeminiError | null = null;
    let overloadRetried = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < models.length; i++) {
      const modelName = models[i];
      geminiLimiter.acquire();
      try {
        return { result: await request(modelName), modelUsed: modelName };
      } catch (error: unknown) {
        lastError = error as GeminiError;
        const classification = this.classifyError(error);
        lastError.classification = classification;

        if (lastError.name === "AbortError" || String(lastError.message).includes("aborted")) throw lastError;
        if (classification === GeminiErrorClassification.AUTH_INVALID) throw new Error("Chave da API Gemini inválida ou sem autorização.");
        if (classification === GeminiErrorClassification.GLOBAL_QUOTA) {
          geminiLimiter.pauseFor(parseRetryDelayMs(error) ?? GEMINI_LIMITS.defaultCooldownMs * 5);
          throw new Error("Cota do projeto esgotada no Google AI Studio. Trocar de modelo não resolve; aguarde ou verifique o faturamento.");
        }
        if (classification === GeminiErrorClassification.MODEL_LIMIT) {
          const delay = parseRetryDelayMs(error) ?? GEMINI_LIMITS.defaultCooldownMs;
          this.markModelUnavailable(modelName, classification, delay);
          // short pause so the fallback does not fire in the same instant
          geminiLimiter.pauseFor(GEMINI_LIMITS.minIntervalMs);
          if (i === models.length - 1) throw new GeminiRateLimitError(delay);
          await this.wait(GEMINI_LIMITS.minIntervalMs);
          continue;
        }
        if (classification === GeminiErrorClassification.NOT_FOUND) {
          // model does not exist for this key: skip it for an hour instead of retrying every message
          this.markModelUnavailable(modelName, classification, 60 * 60 * 1000);
          continue;
        }
        if (classification === GeminiErrorClassification.SERVICE_OVERLOAD && !overloadRetried) {
          overloadRetried = true;
          await this.wait(GEMINI_LIMITS.minIntervalMs);
          i--; // one single retry on the same model
          continue;
        }
        console.error(`Gemini API Error (${modelName}):`, error);
        throw lastError;
      }
    }
    throw lastError || new Error("Falha ao comunicar com os modelos Gemini.");
  }

  async chat(messages: Message[], context?: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<ChatResponse> {
    try {
      const { result, modelUsed } = await this.run((model) =>
        this.ai.models.generateContent({ model, contents: this.buildContents(messages, context), config: this.getRequestConfig(signal) }),
      );
      return { text: result.text?.trim() || "", modelUsed, isFallback: modelUsed !== this.modelName };
    } catch (error) {
      const err = error as Error;
      if (err.name === "AbortError" || String(err.message).includes("aborted")) return { text: "", modelUsed: this.modelName, isFallback: false };
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async chatStream(messages: Message[], context?: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal) {
    const { result, modelUsed } = await this.run((model) =>
      this.ai.models.generateContentStream({ model, contents: this.buildContents(messages, context), config: this.getRequestConfig(signal) }),
    );
    return { stream: result, modelUsed, isFallback: modelUsed !== this.modelName };
  }

  // Extract JSON recipe from text response
  static extractRecipe(text: string): Partial<Recipe> | null {
    try {
      const fenced = text.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/i);
      const jsonMatch = fenced || text.match(/(\{[\s\S]*\})/);
      if (jsonMatch) {
        const jsonStr = fenced ? fenced[1] : jsonMatch[1];
        return JSON.parse(jsonStr.trim());
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to extract recipe JSON", e);
    }
    return null;
  }
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/lib/recipe-calc.test.ts ===
// @ts-expect-error bun test runtime types are not installed
import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { applySalts, deductInventory, finalGravity, originalGravity, recipeCost, shoppingList, styleCheck, volumes, type InventoryItem, type Recipe } from "./recipe-calc";

const eq = { batchL: 20, boilMinutes: 60, evaporationLh: 4, trubLossL: 1, fermenterLossL: 1, efficiency: 75 };
const recipe: Recipe = { id: "r", name: "T", styleCode: "18B", author: "", notes: "", equipment: eq, mashSteps: [], fermentationTempC: 19, fermentationDays: 14,
  fermentables: [{ id: "a", name: "Pale Ale", kg: 4, potential: 1.037, color: 3, type: "grao", price: 20 }],
  hops: [{ id: "h", name: "Cascade", grams: 50, alpha: 6, minutes: 60, use: "fervura", price: 250 }],
  yeasts: [{ id: "y", name: "US-05", attenuation: 80, packs: 1, price: 35 }], miscs: [], createdAt: "", updatedAt: "" };

describe("recipe", () => {
  test("volumes add trub and evaporation", () => { const v = volumes(eq); expect(v.postBoil).toBe(21); expect(v.preBoil).toBe(25); expect(v.packaged).toBe(19); });
  test("OG from 4 kg at 75% in 21 L", () => expect(originalGravity(recipe.fermentables, eq)).toBeCloseTo(1.0441, 4));
  test("FG uses yeast attenuation", () => expect(finalGravity(1.05, recipe.yeasts)).toBeCloseTo(1.01, 5));
  test("cost sums ingredients", () => expect(recipeCost(recipe).total).toBeCloseTo(80 + 12.5 + 35, 5));
  test("style check flags out-of-range", () => expect(styleCheck({ og: 1.04, fg: 1.012, ibu: 40, srm: 7, abv: 5 }, { code: "x", name: "x", og: [1.045, 1.06], fg: [1.01, 1.015], ibu: [30, 50], srm: [5, 10], abv: [4.5, 6.2] }).og).toBe("baixo"));
});
describe("inventory", () => {
  const inv: InventoryItem[] = [{ id: "1", kind: "fermentavel", name: "Pale Ale", amount: 3, unit: "kg", price: 20, minAmount: 0 }, { id: "2", kind: "lupulo", name: "cascade", amount: 100, unit: "g", price: 0.25, minAmount: 0 }];
  test("shopping list reports missing", () => { const s = shoppingList(recipe, inv); expect(s.find(x => x.name === "Pale Ale")!.missing).toBe(1); expect(s.find(x => x.name === "US-05")!.missing).toBe(1); });
  test("deduct never below zero", () => { const d = deductInventory(recipe, inv); expect(d[0].amount).toBe(0); expect(d[1].amount).toBe(50); });
});
test("1 g gypsum in 10 L adds 23 ppm Ca", () => expect(applySalts({ ca: 0, mg: 0, na: 0, so4: 0, cl: 0, hco3: 0 }, { gypsum: 1 }, 10).ca).toBeCloseTo(23.28, 2));


=== ARQUIVO: src/lib/recipe-calc.ts ===
/** Pure recipe, water, inventory and report calculations. Units: kg, g, L, °C, SG. */
import { alcohol, ibuTinseth } from "./brewing";
import { calculateAbv, defaultSettings, type AppSettings } from "./settings-model";
import type { BjcpStyle } from "./catalog";

export type HopUse = "fervura" | "whirlpool" | "dryhop" | "primeiro_mosto";
export interface RecipeFermentable { id: string; name: string; kg: number; potential: number; color: number; type: string; price: number }
export interface RecipeHop { id: string; name: string; grams: number; alpha: number; minutes: number; use: HopUse; price: number; dryHopDay?: number }
export interface RecipeYeast { id: string; name: string; attenuation: number; packs: number; price: number }
export interface RecipeMisc { id: string; name: string; amount: number; unit: string; use: string; price: number }
export interface Equipment { batchL: number; boilMinutes: number; evaporationLh: number; trubLossL: number; fermenterLossL: number; efficiency: number }
export interface MashStep { name: string; tempC: number; minutes: number }

export interface Recipe {
  id: string; name: string; styleCode: string; author: string; notes: string;
  equipment: Equipment; mashSteps: MashStep[]; fermentationTempC: number; fermentationDays: number;
  waterProfileId?: string; equipmentProfileId?: string; mashProfileId?: string; fermentationProfileId?: string;
  fermentables: RecipeFermentable[]; hops: RecipeHop[]; yeasts: RecipeYeast[]; miscs: RecipeMisc[];
  favorite?: boolean; createdAt: string; updatedAt: string;
}

export const defaultEquipment: Equipment = { batchL: 20, boilMinutes: 60, evaporationLh: 4, trubLossL: 1, fermenterLossL: 1, efficiency: 72 };

export function volumes(eq: Equipment) {
  const postBoil = eq.batchL + eq.trubLossL;
  const preBoil = postBoil + eq.evaporationLh * eq.boilMinutes / 60;
  return { postBoil, preBoil, fermenter: eq.batchL, packaged: Math.max(0, eq.batchL - eq.fermenterLossL) };
}

export function originalGravity(fermentables: RecipeFermentable[], eq: Equipment) {
  const vol = volumes(eq).postBoil;
  if (vol <= 0) return 1;
  const points = fermentables.reduce((sum, f) => {
    const eff = f.type === "grao" || f.type === "adjunto" ? eq.efficiency / 100 : 1;
    // potential is SG per lb/gal (PPG); 1 kg/L = 8.3454 lb/gal
    return sum + (f.potential - 1) * 1000 * 8.3454 * f.kg * eff;
  }, 0);
  return 1 + points / vol / 1000;
}

export function finalGravity(og: number, yeasts: RecipeYeast[]) {
  const att = yeasts.length ? Math.max(...yeasts.map(y => y.attenuation)) : 75;
  return 1 + (og - 1) * (1 - att / 100);
}

export function recipeIbu(hops: RecipeHop[], og: number, eq: Equipment, formula: AppSettings["ibuFormula"] = "tinseth") {
  const vol = volumes(eq).postBoil;
  return hops.reduce((sum, h) => {
    if (h.use === "dryhop") return sum;
    const minutes = h.use === "primeiro_mosto" ? eq.boilMinutes + 20 : h.use === "whirlpool" ? Math.min(h.minutes, 30) * 0.5 : h.minutes;
    if (vol <= 0) return sum;
    if (formula === "rager") {
      const utilization = (18.11 + 13.86 * Math.tanh((minutes - 31.32) / 18.27)) / 100;
      return sum + utilization * h.grams * h.alpha * 10 / (vol * (1 + Math.max(0, (og - 1.05) / 0.2)));
    }
    return sum + ibuTinseth(h.grams, h.alpha, minutes, vol, og);
  }, 0);
}

export function recipeSrm(fermentables: RecipeFermentable[], eq: Equipment) {
  const gal = volumes(eq).postBoil / 3.785411784;
  if (gal <= 0) return 0;
  const mcu = fermentables.reduce((s, f) => s + f.kg * 2.2046226218 * f.color, 0) / gal;
  return mcu <= 0 ? 0 : 1.4922 * mcu ** 0.6859;
}

export function recipeCost(r: Pick<Recipe, "fermentables" | "hops" | "yeasts" | "miscs">) {
  const fermentables = r.fermentables.reduce((s, f) => s + f.kg * f.price, 0);
  const hops = r.hops.reduce((s, h) => s + h.grams / 1000 * h.price, 0);
  const yeasts = r.yeasts.reduce((s, y) => s + y.packs * y.price, 0);
  const miscs = r.miscs.reduce((s, m) => s + m.amount * m.price, 0);
  return { fermentables, hops, yeasts, miscs, total: fermentables + hops + yeasts + miscs };
}

export function recipeStats(r: Recipe, settings: AppSettings = defaultSettings) {
  const og = originalGravity(r.fermentables, r.equipment);
  const baseFg = finalGravity(og, r.yeasts);
  const saccharification = r.mashSteps.filter(s => s.tempC >= 55 && s.tempC <= 75 && s.minutes > 0);
  const mashMinutes = saccharification.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.minutes, 0);
  const mashTemp = mashMinutes ? saccharification.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.tempC * s.minutes, 0) / mashMinutes : settings.mashReference;
  const fg = settings.fgFormula === "mash" ? Math.min(og, Math.max(1, baseFg + (og - 1) * (mashTemp - settings.mashReference) * settings.mashAdjustment / 100)) : baseFg;
  const ibu = recipeIbu(r.hops, og, r.equipment, settings.ibuFormula);
  const buGu = og > 1 ? ibu / ((og - 1) * 1000) : 0;
  const attenuation = og > 1 ? (og - fg) / (og - 1) : 0;
  const cost = recipeCost(r);
  const vol = volumes(r.equipment);
  return {
    og, fg, abv: calculateAbv(og, fg, settings.abvFormula), ibu, srm: recipeSrm(r.fermentables, r.equipment),
    buGu, rbr: buGu * (1 + attenuation - 0.765), calories: alcohol(og, fg, 100).calories,
    grainKg: r.fermentables.reduce((s, f) => s + f.kg, 0), volumes: vol, cost, costPerLiter: vol.packaged > 0 ? cost.total / vol.packaged : 0,
  };
}

export type StyleStatus = "baixo" | "ok" | "alto";
export function styleCheck(stats: { og: number; fg: number; ibu: number; srm: number; abv: number }, style: BjcpStyle) {
  const check = (v: number, [min, max]: [number, number]): StyleStatus => (v < min ? "baixo" : v > max ? "alto" : "ok");
  return { og: check(stats.og, style.og), fg: check(stats.fg, style.fg), ibu: check(stats.ibu, style.ibu), srm: check(stats.srm, style.srm), abv: check(stats.abv, style.abv) };
}

/** Approximate sRGB for an SRM value (for color swatches). */
export function srmToHex(srm: number) {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.min(40, srm));
  const r = Math.round(Math.min(255, Math.max(0, 255 * 0.975 ** s)));
  const g = Math.round(Math.min(255, Math.max(0, 255 * 0.88 ** s)));
  const b = Math.round(Math.min(255, Math.max(0, 255 * 0.7 ** s)));
  return `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;
}

// ---------- Water chemistry ----------
export type Ion = "ca" | "mg" | "na" | "so4" | "cl" | "hco3";
export type WaterProfile = Record<Ion, number>;
export const ions: { id: Ion; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "ca", label: "Cálcio (Ca)" }, { id: "mg", label: "Magnésio (Mg)" }, { id: "na", label: "Sódio (Na)" },
  { id: "so4", label: "Sulfato (SO₄)" }, { id: "cl", label: "Cloreto (Cl)" }, { id: "hco3", label: "Bicarbonato (HCO₃)" },
];
/** ppm contributed by 1 g of salt in 1 L. */
export const salts: { id: string; label: string; ppm: Partial<WaterProfile> }[] = [
  { id: "gypsum", label: "Gipsita (CaSO₄·2H₂O)", ppm: { ca: 232.8, so4: 557.9 } },
  { id: "cacl2", label: "Cloreto de Cálcio (CaCl₂·2H₂O)", ppm: { ca: 272.6, cl: 482.3 } },
  { id: "epsom", label: "Sal Amargo (MgSO₄·7H₂O)", ppm: { mg: 98.6, so4: 389.6 } },
  { id: "nacl", label: "Sal de Cozinha (NaCl)", ppm: { na: 393.4, cl: 606.6 } },
  { id: "bicarb", label: "Bicarbonato de Sódio (NaHCO₃)", ppm: { na: 273.7, hco3: 726.4 } },
  { id: "chalk", label: "Giz (CaCO₃)", ppm: { ca: 400.4, hco3: 1219.2 } },
];

export function applySalts(source: WaterProfile, additionsG: Record<string, number>, liters: number): WaterProfile {
  const out = { ...source };
  if (liters <= 0) return out;
  for (const s of salts) {
    const g = additionsG[s.id] || 0;
    for (const [ion, ppm] of Object.entries(s.ppm) as [Ion, number][]) out[ion] += g * ppm / liters;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Greedy non-negative salt suggestion to approach a target profile using gypsum, CaCl₂, Epsom and NaCl. */
export function suggestSalts(source: WaterProfile, target: WaterProfile, liters: number) {
  const g = (deficit: number, ppm: number) => Math.max(0, deficit) * liters / ppm;
  const add: Record<string, number> = {};
  add.epsom = g(target.mg - source.mg, 98.6);
  let cur = applySalts(source, add, liters);
  add.gypsum = g(target.so4 - cur.so4, 557.9);
  cur = applySalts(source, add, liters);
  add.cacl2 = g(target.cl - cur.cl, 482.3);
  cur = applySalts(source, add, liters);
  add.nacl = g(target.na - cur.na, 393.4);
  for (const k of Object.keys(add)) add[k] = Math.round(add[k] * 10) / 10;
  return add;
}

export const sulfateChlorideRatio = (w: WaterProfile) => (w.cl > 0 ? w.so4 / w.cl : 0);

// ---------- Inventory ----------
export type InventoryKind = "fermentavel" | "lupulo" | "levedura" | "outro";
export interface InventoryItem { id: string; kind: InventoryKind; name: string; amount: number; unit: string; price: number; minAmount: number; alpha?: number; potential?: number; color?: number; attenuation?: number }

/** Required amounts per recipe in inventory units (kg, g, pacote, un). */
export function recipeRequirements(r: Recipe) {
  const req: { kind: InventoryKind; name: string; amount: number; unit: string; price: number }[] = [];
  const add = (kind: InventoryKind, name: string, amount: number, unit: string, price: number) => {
    const ex = req.find(x => x.kind === kind && x.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());
    if (ex) ex.amount += amount; else req.push({ kind, name, amount, unit, price });
  };
  r.fermentables.forEach(f => add("fermentavel", f.name, f.kg, "kg", f.price));
  r.hops.forEach(h => add("lupulo", h.name, h.grams, "g", h.price / 1000));
  r.yeasts.forEach(y => add("levedura", y.name, y.packs, "pacote", y.price));
  r.miscs.forEach(m => add("outro", m.name, m.amount, m.unit, m.price));
  return req;
}

const findItem = (inv: InventoryItem[], kind: InventoryKind, name: string) => inv.find(i => i.kind === kind && i.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());

export function shoppingList(r: Recipe, inv: InventoryItem[]) {
  return recipeRequirements(r).map(req => {
    const have = findItem(inv, req.kind, req.name)?.amount ?? 0;
    const missing = Math.max(0, req.amount - have);
    return { ...req, have, missing, cost: missing * req.price };
  });
}

export function deductInventory(r: Recipe, inv: InventoryItem[], allowNegative = false): InventoryItem[] {
  const next = inv.map(i => ({ ...i }));
  for (const req of recipeRequirements(r)) {
    const item = findItem(next, req.kind, req.name);
    if (item) {
      const amount = Math.round((item.amount - req.amount) * 1000) / 1000;
      item.amount = allowNegative ? amount : Math.max(0, amount);
    } else if (allowNegative) next.push({ id: `missing-${req.kind}-${req.name}`, ...req, amount: -req.amount, minAmount: 0 });
  }
  return next;
}

// ---------- Batches ----------
export type BatchStatus = "planejamento" | "brassagem" | "fermentacao" | "condicionamento" | "concluido";
export const batchStatuses: { id: BatchStatus; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "planejamento", label: "Planejamento" }, { id: "brassagem", label: "Brassagem" }, { id: "fermentacao", label: "Fermentação" },
  { id: "condicionamento", label: "Condicionamento" }, { id: "concluido", label: "Concluído" },
];
export interface Measurement { id: string; date: string; sg: number; tempC: number; note?: string }
export interface Batch {
  id: string; number: number; name: string; recipeId: string; recipe: Recipe; status: BatchStatus;
  brewDate: string; measuredOg?: number; measuredFg?: number; measuredVolumeL?: number;
  measurements: Measurement[]; notes: string; inventoryDeducted: boolean; createdAt: string;
  additionalMeasurements?: Record<string, number>;
}

export function batchAbv(b: Batch, settings: AppSettings = defaultSettings) {
  const og = b.measuredOg ?? recipeStats(b.recipe, settings).og;
  const last = [...b.measurements].sort((a, c) => a.date.localeCompare(c.date)).at(-1);
  const fg = b.measuredFg ?? last?.sg;
  return fg ? calculateAbv(og, fg, settings.abvFormula) : undefined;
}

/** Boil-timer events: hop/misc additions sorted by minutes remaining. */
export function boilSchedule(r: Recipe) {
  const events = r.hops.filter(h => h.use === "fervura" || h.use === "primeiro_mosto")
    .map(h => ({ minutes: h.use === "primeiro_mosto" ? r.equipment.boilMinutes : h.minutes, label: `${h.grams} g ${h.name}` }));
  r.hops.filter(h => h.use === "whirlpool").forEach(h => events.push({ minutes: 0, label: `Whirlpool: ${h.grams} g ${h.name}` }));
  r.miscs.filter(m => m.use === "Fervura").forEach(m => events.push({ minutes: 10, label: `${m.amount} ${m.unit} ${m.name}` }));
  return events.sort((a, b) => b.minutes - a.minutes);
}

// ---------- Reports ----------
export function reportSummary(batches: Batch[]) {
  const done = batches.filter(b => b.status === "concluido" || b.status === "condicionamento");
  const liters = batches.filter(b => b.status !== "planejamento").reduce((s, b) => s + (b.measuredVolumeL ?? b.recipe.equipment.batchL), 0);
  const cost = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + recipeCost(b.recipe).total, 0);
  const byMonth = new Map<string, { month: string; litros: number; custo: number; lotes: number }>();
  for (const b of batches) {
    const month = b.brewDate.slice(0, 7);
    const e = byMonth.get(month) ?? { month, litros: 0, custo: 0, lotes: 0 };
    e.litros += b.status === "planejamento" ? 0 : b.measuredVolumeL ?? b.recipe.equipment.batchL;
    e.custo += recipeCost(b.recipe).total; e.lotes += 1;
    byMonth.set(month, e);
  }
  const hopUse = new Map<string, number>();
  const maltUse = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const b of batches) {
    b.recipe.hops.forEach(h => hopUse.set(h.name, (hopUse.get(h.name) ?? 0) + h.grams));
    b.recipe.fermentables.forEach(f => maltUse.set(f.name, (maltUse.get(f.name) ?? 0) + f.kg));
  }
  const top = (m: Map<string, number>) => [...m.entries()].map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value })).sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value).slice(0, 8);
  return {
    totalBatches: batches.length, finished: done.length, liters, cost, costPerLiter: liters > 0 ? cost / liters : 0,
    byMonth: [...byMonth.values()].sort((a, b) => a.month.localeCompare(b.month)), hops: top(hopUse), malts: top(maltUse),
  };
}


=== ARQUIVO: src/lib/settings-model.ts ===
import { convertUnit, sgToPlato, platoToSg } from "./brewing";

export interface AppSettings {
  language: "pt-BR" | "en-US" | "es-ES" | "fr-FR" | "de-DE";
  theme: "light" | "dark" | "system";
  nextBatch: number;
  abvFormula: "standard" | "alternative";
  fgFormula: "normal" | "mash";
  ibuFormula: "tinseth" | "rager";
  inventory: boolean;
  negativeStock: boolean;
  brewMonitoring: boolean;
  nutrition: boolean;
  autoSave: boolean;
  buGu: boolean;
  rbr: boolean;
  gravity: "sg" | "plato";
  sgFormat: "decimal" | "points";
  color: "srm" | "ebc";
  grainColor: "lovibond" | "srm" | "ebc";
  temperature: "°C" | "°F";
  volume: "L" | "gal (US)" | "gal (UK)";
  weight: "kg" | "lb";
  hopWeight: "g" | "oz";
  carbonation: "vol" | "g/L";
  pressure: "bar" | "psi" | "kPa";
  altitude: "m" | "ft";
  diastatic: "lintner" | "wk";
  dateFormat: "auto" | "dmy" | "mdy" | "iso";
  timeFormat: "auto" | "12" | "24";
  mashReference: number;
  mashAdjustment: number;
  additionalMeasurements: string[];
}

export const defaultSettings: AppSettings = {
  language: "pt-BR", theme: "dark", nextBatch: 1, abvFormula: "standard", fgFormula: "normal", ibuFormula: "tinseth",
  inventory: true, negativeStock: false, brewMonitoring: true, nutrition: false, autoSave: false,
  buGu: true, rbr: false, gravity: "sg", sgFormat: "decimal", color: "srm", grainColor: "lovibond",
  temperature: "°C", volume: "L", weight: "kg", hopWeight: "g", carbonation: "vol", pressure: "bar",
  altitude: "m", diastatic: "lintner", dateFormat: "auto", timeFormat: "auto", mashReference: 66,
  mashAdjustment: 1, additionalMeasurements: [],
};
export const measurementOptions = [
  { id: "preBoilSg", label: "Densidade pré-fervura", unit: "SG" },
  { id: "preBoilL", label: "Volume pré-fervura", unit: "L" },
  { id: "postBoilL", label: "Volume pós-fervura", unit: "L" },
  { id: "mashPh", label: "pH da mostura", unit: "pH" },
  { id: "finalPh", label: "pH final", unit: "pH" },
  { id: "pressure", label: "Pressão", unit: "bar" },
  { id: "carbonation", label: "Carbonatação", unit: "vol" },
];
export function normalizeSettings(value: unknown): AppSettings {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return { ...defaultSettings };
  const out = { ...defaultSettings };
  const input = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const choices: Partial<Record<keyof AppSettings, readonly string[]>> = {
    language: ["pt-BR", "en-US", "es-ES", "fr-FR", "de-DE"],
    theme: ["light", "dark", "system"], abvFormula: ["standard", "alternative"], fgFormula: ["normal", "mash"], ibuFormula: ["tinseth", "rager"], gravity: ["sg", "plato"], sgFormat: ["decimal", "points"], color: ["srm", "ebc"], grainColor: ["lovibond", "srm", "ebc"], temperature: ["°C", "°F"], volume: ["L", "gal (US)", "gal (UK)"], weight: ["kg", "lb"], hopWeight: ["g", "oz"], carbonation: ["vol", "g/L"], pressure: ["bar", "psi", "kPa"], altitude: ["m", "ft"], diastatic: ["lintner", "wk"], dateFormat: ["auto", "dmy", "mdy", "iso"], timeFormat: ["auto", "12", "24"],
  };
  for (const key of Object.keys(out) as (keyof AppSettings)[]) {
    const v = input[key];
    if (typeof out[key] === "boolean" && typeof v === "boolean") Object.assign(out, { [key]: v });
    if (choices[key]?.includes(String(v))) Object.assign(out, { [key]: v });
  }
  if (typeof input.nextBatch === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(input.nextBatch) && input.nextBatch >= 1) out.nextBatch = input.nextBatch;
  if (typeof input.mashReference === "number" && input.mashReference >= 55 && input.mashReference <= 75) out.mashReference = input.mashReference;
  if (typeof input.mashAdjustment === "number" && input.mashAdjustment >= 0 && input.mashAdjustment <= 3) out.mashAdjustment = input.mashAdjustment;
  if (Array.isArray(input.additionalMeasurements)) out.additionalMeasurements = measurementOptions.filter(o => input.additionalMeasurements instanceof Array && input.additionalMeasurements.includes(o.id)).map(o => o.id);
  return out;
}
export const nextBatchNumber = (batches: { number: number }[], preferred: number) => Math.max(preferred, ...batches.map(b => b.number + 1), 1);
export const calculateAbv = (og: number, fg: number, formula: AppSettings["abvFormula"] = "standard") => formula === "alternative" ? 76.08 * (og - fg) / (1.775 - og) * fg / 0.794 : (og - fg) * 131.25;
export const unitValue = (v: number, kind: "volume" | "weight" | "hopWeight" | "temperature" | "pressure" | "altitude", s: AppSettings, reverse = false) => {
  const family = kind === "hopWeight" ? "weight" : kind === "altitude" ? "length" : kind;
  const base = { volume: "L", weight: "kg", hopWeight: "g", temperature: "°C", pressure: "bar", altitude: "m" }[kind];
  return convertUnit(v, family, reverse ? s[kind] : base, reverse ? base : s[kind]);
};
export const gravityValue = (sg: number, s: AppSettings) => s.gravity === "plato" ? sgToPlato(sg) : s.sgFormat === "points" ? (sg - 1) * 1000 : sg;
export const gravityInput = (v: number, s: AppSettings) => s.gravity === "plato" ? platoToSg(v) : s.sgFormat === "points" ? 1 + v / 1000 : v;
export const gravityLabel = (s: AppSettings) => s.gravity === "plato" ? "°P" : s.sgFormat === "points" ? "pontos" : "SG";
export const grainColorValue = (lovibond: number, s: AppSettings, reverse = false) => {
  if (s.grainColor === "lovibond") return lovibond;
  const srm = reverse && s.grainColor === "ebc" ? lovibond / 1.97 : lovibond;
  return reverse ? (srm + 0.76) / 1.3546 : (1.3546 * lovibond - 0.76) * (s.grainColor === "ebc" ? 1.97 : 1);
};

=== ARQUIVO: src/lib/settings.ts ===
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { defaultSettings, normalizeSettings, type AppSettings, gravityValue, gravityLabel, unitValue } from "./settings-model";

export const SETTINGS_KEY = "brewfather-settings";
export const SETTINGS_EVENT = "brewfather-settings-change";
export function readSettings(): AppSettings {
  try { return normalizeSettings(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY) || "null")); } catch { return { ...defaultSettings }; }
}
export function saveSettings(patch: Partial<AppSettings>) {
  const next = normalizeSettings({ ...readSettings(), ...patch });
  localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(SETTINGS_EVENT));
  return next;
}
export function useSettings() {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState(readSettings);
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = () => setSettings(readSettings());
    window.addEventListener(SETTINGS_EVENT, sync); window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => { window.removeEventListener(SETTINGS_EVENT, sync); window.removeEventListener("storage", sync); };
  }, []);
  return [settings, saveSettings] as const;
}
export const getLocale = (s: AppSettings = readSettings()) => s.language || navigator.language || "pt-BR";
export function SettingsTheme() {
  const [s] = useSettings();
  useEffect(() => {
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
    const apply = () => {
      const darkMode = s.theme === "dark" || (s.theme === "system" && media.matches);
      document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", darkMode);
      document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = darkMode ? "dark" : "light";
      document.documentElement.lang = getLocale(s);
    };
    apply();
    media.addEventListener("change", apply);
    return () => media.removeEventListener("change", apply);
  }, [s]);
  return null;
}
const number = (v: number, d = 1, locale = getLocale()) => Number(v).toLocaleString(locale, { minimumFractionDigits: d, maximumFractionDigits: d });
export const displayGravity = (v: number, s = readSettings()) => `${number(gravityValue(v, s), s.gravity === "sg" && s.sgFormat === "decimal" ? 3 : 1, getLocale(s))}${s.gravity === "sg" && s.sgFormat === "decimal" ? "" : ` ${gravityLabel(s)}`}`;
export const displayColor = (v: number, s = readSettings()) => `${number(s.color === "ebc" ? v * 1.97 : v, 1, getLocale(s))} ${s.color.toUpperCase()}`;
export const displayUnit = (v: number, kind: Parameters<typeof unitValue>[1], s = readSettings(), d = 1) => `${number(unitValue(v, kind, s), d, getLocale(s))} ${s[kind]}`;
export function displayDate(value: string | Date, s = readSettings(), withTime = false) {
  const d = new Date(value);
  const locale = s.dateFormat === "mdy" ? "en-US" : s.dateFormat === "dmy" ? "pt-BR" : getLocale(s);
  const date = s.dateFormat === "iso" ? `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}` : d.toLocaleDateString(locale);
  return withTime ? `${date} ${d.toLocaleTimeString(locale, { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hour12: s.timeFormat === "auto" ? undefined : s.timeFormat === "12" })}` : date;
}

=== ARQUIVO: src/lib/store.ts ===
/** Browser-local persistence for recipes, batches and inventory, with cross-component sync. */
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { defaultEquipment, type Batch, type InventoryItem, type Recipe } from "./recipe-calc";
import { readCloudSyncConfig, syncCloudData } from "./cloud-sync";
import { getLocale, readSettings } from "./settings";

const KEYS = { recipes: "brewfather-recipes", batches: "brewfather-batches", inventory: "brewfather-inventory" } as const;
type Key = keyof typeof KEYS;
const EVENT = "brewfather-store";


let autoSyncTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
let autoSyncRunning = false;
let autoSyncPending = false;

function triggerAutoSync() {
  if (autoSyncTimer) clearTimeout(autoSyncTimer);

  autoSyncTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    void runAutoSync();
  }, 1000);
}

async function runAutoSync() {
  if (autoSyncRunning) {
    autoSyncPending = true;
    return;
  }

  autoSyncRunning = true;

  try {
    do {
      autoSyncPending = false;

      const provider = readCloudSyncConfig().provider;
      const config = readCloudSyncConfig(provider);

      if (!config.enabled || !config.token) return;

      await syncCloudData(provider);
    } while (autoSyncPending);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Erro na sincronização automática:", error);
  } finally {
    autoSyncRunning = false;

    if (autoSyncPending) {
      triggerAutoSync();
    }
  }
}

export const uid = () => (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : Math.random().toString(36).slice(2));

const now = new Date().toISOString();
const seedRecipe: Recipe = {

  ],

  createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
};
const seedInventory: InventoryItem[] = [
  { id: "i1", kind: "fermentavel", name: "Pale Ale", amount: 10, unit: "kg", price: 20, minAmount: 5, potential: 1.037, color: 3 },
  { id: "i2", kind: "fermentavel", name: "Caramelo 40L", amount: 0.2, unit: "kg", price: 26, minAmount: 0.5, potential: 1.034, color: 40 },
  { id: "i3", kind: "lupulo", name: "Cascade", amount: 100, unit: "g", price: 0.25, minAmount: 50, alpha: 6 },
  { id: "i4", kind: "lupulo", name: "Magnum", amount: 50, unit: "g", price: 0.22, minAmount: 20, alpha: 13 },
  { id: "i5", kind: "levedura", name: "SafAle US-05", amount: 2, unit: "pacote", price: 35, minAmount: 1, attenuation: 81 },
];
const seeds: Record<Key, unknown[]> = { recipes: [seedRecipe], batches: [], inventory: seedInventory };

export function read<T>(key: Key): T[] {
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEYS[key]);
  if (raw === null) return seeds[key] as T[];
  try { return JSON.parse(raw) as T[]; } catch { return []; }
}
export function write<T>(key: Key, value: T[]) {
  localStorage.setItem(KEYS[key], JSON.stringify(value));
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(EVENT, { detail: key }));
  void triggerAutoSync();
}

function useCollection<T>(key: Key) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<T[]>(() => read<T>(key));
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = () => setItems(read<T>(key));
    window.addEventListener(EVENT, sync);
    window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => { window.removeEventListener(EVENT, sync); window.removeEventListener("storage", sync); };
  }, [key]);
  const save = useCallback((next: T[]) => write(key, next), [key]);
  return [items, save] as const;
}

export const useRecipes = () => useCollection<Recipe>("recipes");
export const useBatches = () => useCollection<Batch>("batches");
export const useInventory = () => useCollection<InventoryItem>("inventory");

export interface SavedProfile { id: string; name: string; descricao?: string; values: Record<string, string> }
export function readProfiles(slug: "equipamento" | "mostura" | "fermentacao" | "agua" | "estilos"): SavedProfile[] {
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(`brewfather-profiles-${slug}`) || "[]"); } catch { return []; }
}

export const fmt = (v: number, d = 1, locale = getLocale(readSettings())) => Number(v).toLocaleString(locale, { minimumFractionDigits: d, maximumFractionDigits: d });
export const money = (v: number, locale = getLocale(readSettings())) => new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, { style: "currency", currency: "BRL" }).format(v);

import { bjcpStyles, type BjcpStyle } from "./catalog";
const num = (v: string | undefined, d = 0) => { const n = Number((v ?? "").replace(",", ".")); return Number.isFinite(n) && v !== "" && v !== undefined ? n : d; };
/** BJCP styles plus the user's custom style profiles (single values become ±10% ranges). */
export function allStyles(): BjcpStyle[] {
  const custom = readProfiles("estilos").map(p => {
    const v = p.values; const around = (x: number): [number, number] => [x * 0.9, x * 1.1];
    return { code: `custom-${p.id}`, name: `${p.name} (personalizado)`, og: [num(v.ogMin, 1.04), num(v.ogMax, 1.06)], fg: [num(v.fgMin, 1.008), num(v.fgMax, 1.014)], ibu: around(num(v.ibu, 30)), srm: around(num(v.srm, 8)), abv: around(num(v.abv, 5)) } as BjcpStyle;
  });
  return [...bjcpStyles, ...custom].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "pt-BR") || a.code.localeCompare(b.code));
}
export const profileNumber = num;

import { useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useEffect } from "react";

const NotFound = () => {
  const location = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    console.error("404 Error: User attempted to access non-existent route:", location.pathname);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gray-100">
      <div className="text-center">
        <h1 className="mb-4 text-4xl font-bold">404</h1>
        <p className="mb-4 text-xl text-gray-600">Oops! Page not found</p>
        <a href="/" className="text-blue-500 underline hover:text-blue-700">
          Return to Home
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default NotFound;
